```
```
"""
poler_toolkit.recipes — встроенные рецепты анализа.

Каждый рецепт — это функция:
    recipe_<name>(filepaths, **opts) -> ReportBundle

ReportBundle = {
    "report": Dict[str, Any],    # для JSON
    "verdict_lines": List[str],  # для Markdown
    "artifacts": Dict[str, Path] # PNG-файлы
}

Рецепты:
  - analyze     — глубокий анализ одного файла (POLER + sklearn)
  - compare     — сравнение двух файлов по метрикам
  - structure   — только структурный анализ (главы, кластеры, PCA)
  - characters  — карта персонажей по тексту
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
import math
import logging
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
from collections import Counter

import numpy as np
from scipy.sparse import hstack
import scipy.sparse as sp
from sklearn.feature_extraction.text import TfidfVectorizer
from sklearn.metrics.pairwise import cosine_similarity
from sklearn.cluster import KMeans
from sklearn.decomposition import PCA
from sklearn.metrics import silhouette_score

from . import viz
from . import core
from . import report
from .errors import RecipeError, ConfigurationError

# Extension recipes (Stage 2) — built by Agents A-E.
# Each module exposes a single public function recipe_<name>(...) that
# returns the standard {report, verdict_lines, artifacts} bundle.
from .recipes_ext.theme_evolution import recipe_theme_evolution
from .recipes_ext.themes_alpha import recipe_themes_alpha
from .recipes_ext.diff_versions import recipe_diff_versions
from .recipes_ext.batch_directory import recipe_batch_directory
from .recipes_ext.custom_plugin import recipe_custom_plugin
from .recipes_ext.smart_toolkit import (
    recipe_smart_read,
    recipe_smart_search,
    recipe_smart_batch,
    recipe_smart_cache,
    recipe_smart_diff,
)

log = logging.getLogger("poler_toolkit.recipes")


# ============================================================
# Общие утилиты
# ============================================================
def _l2norm_sparse(X):
    norms = sp.linalg.norm(X, axis=1)
    norms[norms == 0] = 1.0
    return X.multiply(1.0 / norms[:, None])


def _shannon_entropy(text: str) -> float:
    tokens = re.findall(r"[\w’']+", text.lower())
    if not tokens:
        return 0.0
    counts = Counter(tokens)
    total = len(tokens)
    return -sum((c / total) * math.log2(c / total) for c in counts.values())


def _sklearn_features(paragraphs: List[str]) -> Tuple[Any, Any]:
    """Строит TF-IDF матрицу (word + char n-grams)."""
    if len(paragraphs) < 4:
        raise RecipeError(
            "too few paragraphs for sklearn analysis",
            hint="Нужно минимум 4 абзаца. Возможно, текст не разбит на абзацы.",
        )
    word_vec = TfidfVectorizer(
        max_features=5000, ngram_range=(1, 2), min_df=2,
        sublinear_tf=True, token_pattern=r"(?u)\b[\w’']+\b",
    )
    char_vec = TfidfVectorizer(
        analyzer="char_wb", ngram_range=(3, 5),
        max_features=5000, min_df=2, sublinear_tf=True,
    )
    Xw = word_vec.fit_transform(paragraphs)
    Xc = char_vec.fit_transform(paragraphs)
    X = hstack([Xw, Xc]).tocsr()
    X = _l2norm_sparse(X).tocsr()
    return X, (word_vec, char_vec)


def _best_kmeans(X, k_range: range) -> Tuple[int, float, Dict[int, float], KMeans]:
    """Подбирает K по silhouette."""
    best_k, best_sil = None, -1
    sils = {}
    best_km = None
    for k in k_range:
        try:
            km = KMeans(n_clusters=k, n_init=10, random_state=42)
            labels = km.fit_predict(X)
            if len(set(labels)) < 2:
                continue
            sil = silhouette_score(X, labels,
                                   sample_size=min(2000, X.shape[0]),
                                   random_state=42)
            sils[k] = sil
            if sil > best_sil:
                best_sil = sil
                best_k = k
                best_km = km
        except Exception as e:
            log.warning(f"KMeans k={k} failed: {e}")
    if best_km is None:
        # Fallback: K=3
        best_km = KMeans(n_clusters=3, n_init=10, random_state=42).fit(X)
        best_k = 3
        best_sil = 0.0
        sils = {3: 0.0}
    return best_k, best_sil, sils, best_km


# ============================================================
# RECIPE: analyze
# ============================================================
def recipe_analyze(
    filepaths: List[str | Path],
    *,
    keyword: Optional[str] = None,
    chapter_pattern: Optional[str] = None,
    lang: str = "auto",
    top_n: int = 15,
    out_dir: Path = None,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Глубокий анализ одного файла: POLER + sklearn.

    Что делает:
      1. Читает файл
      2. Разбивает на главы (core.split_chapters)
      3. POLER: analyze_large_file по keyword + cluster_fragments
      4. POLER: grep по keyword (карта позиций)
      5. sklearn: TF-IDF абзацев + KMeans + PCA + cosine соседей
      6. Shannon entropy по главам
      7. Генерирует 5 PNG + JSON + Markdown verdict
    """
    if len(filepaths) != 1:
        raise ConfigurationError(
            "analyze expects exactly 1 file",
            hint="Use 'compare' for 2 files.",
        )
    filepath = Path(filepaths[0])
    log.info(f"[analyze] file={filepath}, keyword={keyword}")

    # 1. Чтение
    text, fmt = core.read_any(filepath)
    log.info(f"  read: {len(text)} chars, format={fmt}")

    # 2. Главы
    chapters = core.split_chapters(text, pattern=chapter_pattern, lang=lang)
    log.info(f"  chapters: {len(chapters)}")

    # 3. keyword
    kw = core.pick_keyword(text, hint=keyword)
    log.info(f"  keyword: {kw}")

    # 4. POLER analyze_large_file
    poler_big = core.safe_call(
        "analyze_large_file",
        str(filepath),
        keyword=kw,
        window_size=3000,
        chunk_size=100000,
        top_n=top_n,
        progress=False,
        phi_decay=0.85,
        kappa=1.0,
    )
    fragments = poler_big.get("top_fragments", [])
    clusters = []
    cluster_sizes = []
    if fragments:
        try:
            clusters = core.safe_call("cluster_fragments", fragments, adaptive=True)
            cluster_sizes = [len(c) for c in clusters]
        except Exception as e:
            log.warning(f"cluster_fragments failed: {e}")
    log.info(f"  POLER: {poler_big.get('total_occurrences', 0)} occ, "
             f"{len(fragments)} frag, {len(clusters)} clusters")

    # 5. POLER grep по keyword
    grep_results = core.safe_call(
        "grep_search_file", str(filepath), kw,
        ignore_case=True, count_only=False,
    )
    char_positions = [r.line_number for r in grep_results]

    # 6. sklearn features
    all_paragraphs = []
    para_chapter_idx = []
    for ci, ch in enumerate(chapters):
        for p in ch["paragraphs"]:
            if len(p.split()) >= 4:
                all_paragraphs.append(p)
                para_chapter_idx.append(ci)
    if len(all_paragraphs) < 4:
        raise RecipeError(
            f"only {len(all_paragraphs)} paragraphs after filtering",
            hint="Возможно, EPUB без переносов строк — попробуйте --lang inline_ru",
        )
    X, _ = _sklearn_features(all_paragraphs)
    log.info(f"  sklearn X: {X.shape}")

    # 7. Cosine соседей
    sims = np.array([
        cosine_similarity(X.getrow(i - 1), X.getrow(i))[0, 0]
        for i in range(1, X.shape[0])
    ])

    # 8. KMeans + silhouette
    best_k, best_sil, sils, km = _best_kmeans(X, range(3, 13))
    cluster_labels = km.labels_

    # 9. PCA
    pca = PCA(n_components=2, random_state=42)
    coords = pca.fit_transform(X.toarray())

    # 10. Chapter matrix
    chap_centroids = np.zeros((len(chapters), X.shape[1]))
    for ci in range(len(chapters)):
        idxs = [i for i, c in enumerate(para_chapter_idx) if c == ci]
        if idxs:
            v = X[idxs].mean(axis=0).A1
            n = np.linalg.norm(v)
            if n > 0:
                v = v / n
            chap_centroids[ci] = v
    chap_sim = cosine_similarity(chap_centroids)
    chap_sim_mean = float((chap_sim.sum() - np.trace(chap_sim)) /
                          (chap_sim.size - len(chap_sim)))

    # 11. Энтропия
    entropies = np.array([_shannon_entropy(ch["body"]) for ch in chapters])

    # 12. EMOTIONAL_MARKERS
    markers = sorted(core.get_poler().EMOTIONAL_MARKERS)
    ch_marker_matrix = np.zeros((len(chapters), len(markers)))
    for ci, ch in enumerate(chapters):
        body = ch["body"].lower()
        tokens = re.findall(r"[\w’']+", body)
        tc = Counter(tokens)
        for mi, m in enumerate(markers):
            ch_marker_matrix[ci, mi] = tc.get(m.lower(), 0)
    chap_sim_poler = None
    if ch_marker_matrix.sum() > 0:
        norm = ch_marker_matrix / (ch_marker_matrix.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True) + 1e-9)
        l2 = np.linalg.norm(norm, axis=1, keepdims=True)
        l2[l2 == 0] = 1
        norm = norm / l2
        chap_sim_poler = norm @ norm.T

    # 13. Вердикт
    verdict = report.coherence_verdict(float(sims.mean()), float(best_sil))

    # 14. Графики
    artifacts = {}
    if out_dir is not None:
        artifacts["chapter_matrix_sklearn"] = out_dir / "01_chapter_matrix_sklearn.png"
        viz.chapter_matrix(
            chap_sim, [c["title"] for c in chapters],
            artifacts["chapter_matrix_sklearn"],
            title=f"{filepath.stem} — главы (sklearn TF-IDF)",
            cmap="viridis",
        )

        if chap_sim_poler is not None:
            artifacts["chapter_matrix_poler"] = out_dir / "02_chapter_matrix_poler.png"
            viz.chapter_matrix(
                chap_sim_poler, [c["title"] for c in chapters],
                artifacts["chapter_matrix_poler"],
                title=f"{filepath.stem} — главы (POLER markers)",
                cmap="magma",
            )

        artifacts["transition"] = out_dir / "03_transition_smoothness.png"
        viz.transition_smoothness(
            sims, artifacts["transition"],
            title=f"{filepath.stem} — переходы абзацев",
        )

        artifacts["pca"] = out_dir / "04_pca_scatter.png"
        viz.pca_scatter(
            coords, cluster_labels,
            chapter_idx=para_chapter_idx,
            explained_variance=tuple(pca.explained_variance_ratio_),
            out_path=artifacts["pca"],
            title=f"{filepath.stem} — PCA (silhouette={best_sil:.3f})",
        )

        artifacts["entropy"] = out_dir / "05_entropy.png"
        viz.entropy_per_chapter(
            entropies, [c["title"] for c in chapters],
            artifacts["entropy"],
            title=f"{filepath.stem} — Shannon entropy",
        )

        if cluster_sizes:
            artifacts["clusters"] = out_dir / "06_cluster_sizes.png"
            viz.cluster_sizes_bar(
                cluster_sizes, artifacts["clusters"],
                title=f"{filepath.stem} — POLER clusters (keyword='{kw}')",
            )

        artifacts["character_map"] = out_dir / "07_character_map.png"
        viz.character_map(
            [kw], {kw: char_positions}, {kw: len(char_positions)},
            artifacts["character_map"],
            title=f"{filepath.stem} — карта '{kw}' ({len(char_positions)} згадок)",
        )

    # 15. Отчёт
    metrics = {
        "total_words": int(len(text.split())),
        "total_chapters": len(chapters),
        "total_paragraphs": len(all_paragraphs),
        "keyword": kw,
        "poler": {
            "total_occurrences": int(poler_big.get("total_occurrences", 0)),
            "unique_fragments": int(poler_big.get("unique_fragments", 0)),
            "fragments_returned": len(fragments),
            "n_clusters": len(clusters),
            "cluster_sizes": cluster_sizes[:15],
        },
        "sklearn": {
            "neighbor_cosine_mean": float(sims.mean()),
            "neighbor_cosine_std": float(sims.std()),
            "frac_below_0.05": float((sims < 0.05).mean()),
            "frac_below_0.10": float((sims < 0.10).mean()),
            "frac_above_0.20": float((sims > 0.20).mean()),
            "best_k": int(best_k),
            "silhouette": float(best_sil),
            "silhouettes_all": {int(k): float(v) for k, v in sils.items()},
            "pca_explained_variance": float(pca.explained_variance_ratio_.sum()),
            "chap_sim_mean_offdiag": chap_sim_mean,
            "X_shape": list(X.shape),
        },
        "shannon_entropy": {
            "mean": float(entropies.mean()),
            "std": float(entropies.std()),
            "min": float(entropies.min()),
            "max": float(entropies.max()),
        },
        "emotional_markers_found": int((ch_marker_matrix.sum(axis=0) > 0).sum()),
        "emotional_markers_total": int(len(markers)),
    }

    verdict_lines = report.verdict_block(
        f"Анализ: {filepath.name}",
        report.metric_table(
            ["Метрика", "Значение"],
            [
                ["Файл", str(filepath)],
                ["Слов", str(metrics["total_words"])],
                ["Глав", str(metrics["total_chapters"])],
                ["Абзацев", str(metrics["total_paragraphs"])],
                ["Keyword (auto)", kw],
                ["POLER occurrences", str(metrics["poler"]["total_occurrences"])],
                ["POLER fragments", str(metrics["poler"]["fragments_returned"])],
                ["POLER clusters", str(metrics["poler"]["n_clusters"])],
                ["sklearn neighbor cosine",
                 f"{metrics['sklearn']['neighbor_cosine_mean']:.3f}"],
                ["sklearn frac<0.10",
                 f"{metrics['sklearn']['frac_below_0.10']:.1%}"],
                ["sklearn silhouette",
                 f"{metrics['sklearn']['silhouette']:.3f}"],
                ["sklearn PCA expl.var",
                 f"{metrics['sklearn']['pca_explained_variance']:.3f}"],
                ["sklearn chap cosine",
                 f"{metrics['sklearn']['chap_sim_mean_offdiag']:.3f}"],
                ["Entropy mean",
                 f"{metrics['shannon_entropy']['mean']:.2f} bits/token"],
                ["EMOTIONAL_MARKERS",
                 f"{metrics['emotional_markers_found']}/{metrics['emotional_markers_total']}"],
            ],
        ),
        [f"## Вердикт", "", f"**{verdict}**", ""],
    )

    full_report = report.wrap_report(
        task_name="analyze",
        engine="POLER v6 + sklearn",
        input_files=[str(filepath)],
        metrics=metrics,
        output_files={k: str(v) for k, v in artifacts.items()},
        verdict=verdict,
    )

    return {
        "report": full_report,
        "verdict_lines": verdict_lines,
        "artifacts": artifacts,
    }


# ============================================================
# RECIPE: compare
# ============================================================
def recipe_compare(
    filepaths: List[str | Path],
    *,
    keyword: Optional[str] = None,
    chapter_pattern: Optional[str] = None,
    lang: str = "auto",
    out_dir: Path = None,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Сравнение двух файлов по всем метрикам."""
    if len(filepaths) != 2:
        raise ConfigurationError(
            "compare expects exactly 2 files",
            hint="Use 'analyze' for 1 file.",
        )
    fp_a, fp_b = Path(filepaths[0]), Path(filepaths[1])
    log.info(f"[compare] {fp_a.name} vs {fp_b.name}")

    # Анализ каждого файла (без генерации артефактов)
    res_a = recipe_analyze([fp_a], keyword=keyword, chapter_pattern=chapter_pattern,
                           lang=lang, out_dir=None)
    res_b = recipe_analyze([fp_b], keyword=keyword, chapter_pattern=chapter_pattern,
                           lang=lang, out_dir=None)

    m_a = res_a["report"]["metrics"]
    m_b = res_b["report"]["metrics"]

    # Метрики сравнения
    coh_a = m_a["sklearn"]["neighbor_cosine_mean"]
    coh_b = m_b["sklearn"]["neighbor_cosine_mean"]
    sil_a = m_a["sklearn"]["silhouette"]
    sil_b = m_b["sklearn"]["silhouette"]
    chap_a = m_a["sklearn"]["chap_sim_mean_offdiag"]
    chap_b = m_b["sklearn"]["chap_sim_mean_offdiag"]
    pca_a = m_a["sklearn"]["pca_explained_variance"]
    pca_b = m_b["sklearn"]["pca_explained_variance"]
    frac_a = m_a["sklearn"]["frac_below_0.10"]
    frac_b = m_b["sklearn"]["frac_below_0.10"]

    ratios = {
        "coherence_ratio": coh_b / max(coh_a, 1e-6),
        "silhouette_ratio": sil_b / max(sil_a, 1e-6),
        "chap_sim_ratio": chap_b / max(chap_a, 1e-6),
        "pca_variance_ratio": pca_b / max(pca_a, 1e-6),
        "frac_sharp_ratio": frac_a / max(frac_b, 1e-6),  # во сколько раз меньше скачков
    }

    # Вердикт
    verdict = (
        f"{fp_b.stem} vs {fp_a.stem}: "
        f"связность соседних абзацев в {ratios['coherence_ratio']:.1f}× "
        f"({coh_b:.3f} vs {coh_a:.3f}); "
        f"silhouette в {ratios['silhouette_ratio']:.1f}× "
        f"({sil_b:.3f} vs {sil_a:.3f}); "
        f"PCA expl.var в {ratios['pca_variance_ratio']:.1f}× "
        f"({pca_b:.3f} vs {pca_a:.3f}); "
        f"резких скачков в {ratios['frac_sharp_ratio']:.1f}× меньше."
    )

    # Графики
    artifacts = {}
    if out_dir is not None:
        # Dashboard sklearn
        artifacts["sklearn_dashboard"] = out_dir / "01_sklearn_dashboard.png"
        viz.comparison_dashboard(
            metric_names=[
                "Сосед. cosine", "Silhouette", "PCA expl.var",
                "Доля <0.10", "Доля >0.20", "Cosine глав",
            ],
            values_a=[coh_a, sil_a, pca_a, frac_a,
                      m_a["sklearn"]["frac_above_0.20"], chap_a],
            values_b=[coh_b, sil_b, pca_b, frac_b,
                      m_b["sklearn"]["frac_above_0.20"], chap_b],
            label_a=fp_a.stem[:15],
            label_b=fp_b.stem[:15],
            out_path=artifacts["sklearn_dashboard"],
            title="sklearn метрики",
        )

        # Dashboard POLER
        cl_a = m_a["poler"]["n_clusters"]
        cl_b = m_b["poler"]["n_clusters"]
        frag_a = m_a["poler"]["fragments_returned"]
        frag_b = m_b["poler"]["fragments_returned"]
        occ_a = m_a["poler"]["total_occurrences"]
        occ_b = m_b["poler"]["total_occurrences"]
        ent_a = m_a["shannon_entropy"]["mean"]
        ent_b = m_b["shannon_entropy"]["mean"]
        idx_a = m_a["total_words"]
        idx_b = m_b["total_words"]

        artifacts["poler_dashboard"] = out_dir / "02_poler_dashboard.png"
        viz.comparison_dashboard(
            metric_names=[
                "Слов", "POLER occurrences", "Фрагментов",
                "Кластеров", "Entropy mean", "Глав",
            ],
            values_a=[idx_a, occ_a, frag_a, cl_a, ent_a, m_a["total_chapters"]],
            values_b=[idx_b, occ_b, frag_b, cl_b, ent_b, m_b["total_chapters"]],
            label_a=fp_a.stem[:15],
            label_b=fp_b.stem[:15],
            out_path=artifacts["poler_dashboard"],
            title="POLER v6 метрики",
            color_a="#8e44ad", color_b="#16a085",
        )

    verdict_lines = report.verdict_block(
        f"Сравнение: {fp_a.name} vs {fp_b.name}",
        report.metric_table(
            ["Метрика", fp_a.stem[:20], fp_b.stem[:20], "Ratio"],
            [
                ["Сосед. cosine", f"{coh_a:.3f}", f"{coh_b:.3f}",
                 f"{ratios['coherence_ratio']:.2f}×"],
                ["Silhouette", f"{sil_a:.3f}", f"{sil_b:.3f}",
                 f"{ratios['silhouette_ratio']:.2f}×"],
                ["Cosine глав", f"{chap_a:.3f}", f"{chap_b:.3f}",
                 f"{ratios['chap_sim_ratio']:.2f}×"],
                ["PCA expl.var", f"{pca_a:.3f}", f"{pca_b:.3f}",
                 f"{ratios['pca_variance_ratio']:.2f}×"],
                ["Доля скачков <0.10", f"{frac_a:.1%}", f"{frac_b:.1%}",
                 f"{ratios['frac_sharp_ratio']:.2f}×"],
                ["Слов", str(m_a["total_words"]), str(m_b["total_words"]), ""],
                ["Глав", str(m_a["total_chapters"]), str(m_b["total_chapters"]), ""],
                ["POLER clusters", str(cl_a), str(cl_b), ""],
                ["Entropy mean", f"{ent_a:.2f}", f"{ent_b:.2f}", ""],
            ],
        ),
        ["## Вердикт", "", f"**{verdict}**", ""],
    )

    full_report = report.wrap_report(
        task_name="compare",
        engine="POLER v6 + sklearn",
        input_files=[str(fp_a), str(fp_b)],
        metrics={
            fp_a.stem: m_a, fp_b.stem: m_b,
            "ratios": ratios,
        },
        output_files={k: str(v) for k, v in artifacts.items()},
        verdict=verdict,
    )

    return {
        "report": full_report,
        "verdict_lines": verdict_lines,
        "artifacts": artifacts,
    }


# ============================================================
# RECIPE: structure
# ============================================================
def recipe_structure(
    filepaths: List[str | Path],
    *,
    chapter_pattern: Optional[str] = None,
    lang: str = "auto",
    out_dir: Path = None,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Только структурный анализ (без keyword, без POLER engine).
    Главы + TF-IDF + KMeans + PCA + entropy + chapter matrix.
    Быстрее analyze.
    """
    if len(filepaths) != 1:
        raise ConfigurationError("structure expects exactly 1 file")
    filepath = Path(filepaths[0])
    log.info(f"[structure] file={filepath}")

    text, fmt = core.read_any(filepath)
    chapters = core.split_chapters(text, pattern=chapter_pattern, lang=lang)
    log.info(f"  chapters: {len(chapters)}")

    all_paragraphs = []
    para_chapter_idx = []
    for ci, ch in enumerate(chapters):
        for p in ch["paragraphs"]:
            if len(p.split()) >= 4:
                all_paragraphs.append(p)
                para_chapter_idx.append(ci)
    if len(all_paragraphs) < 4:
        raise RecipeError(f"only {len(all_paragraphs)} paragraphs")

    X, _ = _sklearn_features(all_paragraphs)
    sims = np.array([
        cosine_similarity(X.getrow(i - 1), X.getrow(i))[0, 0]
        for i in range(1, X.shape[0])
    ])
    best_k, best_sil, sils, km = _best_kmeans(X, range(3, 13))
    pca = PCA(n_components=2, random_state=42)
    coords = pca.fit_transform(X.toarray())

    chap_centroids = np.zeros((len(chapters), X.shape[1]))
    for ci in range(len(chapters)):
        idxs = [i for i, c in enumerate(para_chapter_idx) if c == ci]
        if idxs:
            v = X[idxs].mean(axis=0).A1
            n = np.linalg.norm(v)
            if n > 0:
                v = v / n
            chap_centroids[ci] = v
    chap_sim = cosine_similarity(chap_centroids)
    chap_sim_mean = float((chap_sim.sum() - np.trace(chap_sim)) /
                          (chap_sim.size - len(chap_sim)))
    entropies = np.array([_shannon_entropy(ch["body"]) for ch in chapters])

    verdict = report.coherence_verdict(float(sims.mean()), float(best_sil))

    artifacts = {}
    if out_dir is not None:
        artifacts["chapter_matrix"] = out_dir / "01_chapter_matrix.png"
        viz.chapter_matrix(
            chap_sim, [c["title"] for c in chapters],
            artifacts["chapter_matrix"],
            title=f"{filepath.stem} — главы (TF-IDF)",
        )
        artifacts["transition"] = out_dir / "02_transition.png"
        viz.transition_smoothness(
            sims, artifacts["transition"],
            title=f"{filepath.stem} — переходы",
        )
        artifacts["pca"] = out_dir / "03_pca.png"
        viz.pca_scatter(
            coords=coords,
            cluster_labels=km.labels_,
            chapter_idx=para_chapter_idx,
            explained_variance=tuple(pca.explained_variance_ratio_),
            out_path=artifacts["pca"],
            title=f"{filepath.stem} — PCA (silhouette={best_sil:.3f})",
        )
        artifacts["entropy"] = out_dir / "04_entropy.png"
        viz.entropy_per_chapter(
            entropies, [c["title"] for c in chapters],
            artifacts["entropy"],
            title=f"{filepath.stem} — entropy",
        )

    metrics = {
        "total_words": int(len(text.split())),
        "total_chapters": len(chapters),
        "total_paragraphs": len(all_paragraphs),
        "neighbor_cosine_mean": float(sims.mean()),
        "neighbor_cosine_std": float(sims.std()),
        "frac_below_0.10": float((sims < 0.10).mean()),
        "frac_above_0.20": float((sims > 0.20).mean()),
        "best_k": int(best_k),
        "silhouette": float(best_sil),
        "pca_explained_variance": float(pca.explained_variance_ratio_.sum()),
        "chap_sim_mean_offdiag": chap_sim_mean,
        "entropy_mean": float(entropies.mean()),
        "entropy_std": float(entropies.std()),
    }

    verdict_lines = report.verdict_block(
        f"Структура: {filepath.name}",
        report.metric_table(
            ["Метрика", "Значение"],
            [
                ["Слов", str(metrics["total_words"])],
                ["Глав", str(metrics["total_chapters"])],
                ["Абзацев", str(metrics["total_paragraphs"])],
                ["Сосед. cosine", f"{metrics['neighbor_cosine_mean']:.3f}"],
                ["Доля <0.10", f"{metrics['frac_below_0.10']:.1%}"],
                ["Доля >0.20", f"{metrics['frac_above_0.20']:.1%}"],
                ["Silhouette", f"{metrics['silhouette']:.3f}"],
                ["PCA expl.var", f"{metrics['pca_explained_variance']:.3f}"],
                ["Cosine глав", f"{metrics['chap_sim_mean_offdiag']:.3f}"],
                ["Entropy", f"{metrics['entropy_mean']:.2f}"],
            ],
        ),
        ["## Вердикт", "", f"**{verdict}**", ""],
    )

    full_report = report.wrap_report(
        task_name="structure",
        engine="sklearn (TF-IDF + KMeans + PCA)",
        input_files=[str(filepath)],
        metrics=metrics,
        output_files={k: str(v) for k, v in artifacts.items()},
        verdict=verdict,
    )

    return {
        "report": full_report,
        "verdict_lines": verdict_lines,
        "artifacts": artifacts,
    }


# ============================================================
# RECIPE: characters
# ============================================================
def recipe_characters(
    filepaths: List[str | Path],
    *,
    names: Optional[List[str]] = None,
    out_dir: Path = None,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Карта персонажей по тексту.
    Использует POLER's grep_search_file для каждого имени.
    Авто-detect: если names не заданы — берёт топ-N знаменательных существительных.
    """
    if len(filepaths) != 1:
        raise ConfigurationError("characters expects exactly 1 file")
    filepath = Path(filepaths[0])
    log.info(f"[characters] file={filepath}, names={names}")

    text, fmt = core.read_any(filepath)

    # Авто-detect имён, если не заданы
    if not names:
        # Берём топ-10 знаменательных слов, фильтруем по заглавной букве
        tokens = re.findall(r"\b[A-ZА-ЯІЄЇ][\w’']+\b", text)
        counter = Counter(tokens)
        # Уберём начала предложений (грубая эвристика)
        # Берём те, что встречаются > 5 раз и не в начале абзаца
        candidates = [(w, c) for w, c in counter.most_common(50) if c >= 5]
        # Отфильтровать стоп-слова
        stop = {"The", "And", "But", "This", "That", "These", "Those", "It",
                "He", "She", "They", "We", "You", "I", "Але", "Тоді", "Це",
                "Той", "Та", "Цей", "Ця", "Він", "Вона", "Вони", "Ми", "Ви", "Я"}
        candidates = [(w, c) for w, c in candidates if w not in stop]
        names = [w for w, _ in candidates[:10]]
        log.info(f"  auto-detected names: {names}")

    # grep по каждому имени
    positions = {}
    counts = {}
    for name in names:
        res = core.safe_call(
            "grep_search_file", str(filepath), name,
            ignore_case=False, count_only=False,
        )
        positions[name] = [r.line_number for r in res]
        counts[name] = len(res)
        log.info(f"  {name}: {counts[name]}")

    # Сортировать по убыванию частоты
    sorted_pairs = sorted(counts.items(), key=lambda x: -x[1])
    sorted_names = [n for n, _ in sorted_pairs]

    # Совместная встречаемость (co-occurrence в окне 1000 chars)
    cooc = np.zeros((len(names), len(names)), dtype=int)
    name_idx = {n: i for i, n in enumerate(names)}
    for i, n1 in enumerate(names):
        for pos1 in positions[n1]:
            # Окно ±1000 chars вокруг pos1
            # (используем line_number как прокси для позиции)
            for j, n2 in enumerate(names):
                if i == j:
                    continue
                for pos2 in positions[n2]:
                    if abs(pos2 - pos1) <= 50:  # 50 строк
                        cooc[i, j] += 1
                        break

    # Графики
    artifacts = {}
    if out_dir is not None:
        artifacts["character_map"] = out_dir / "01_character_map.png"
        viz.character_map(
            sorted_names, positions, counts,
            artifacts["character_map"],
            title=f"{filepath.stem} — карта персонажей",
        )
        # Co-occurrence matrix
        artifacts["cooc_matrix"] = out_dir / "02_cooccurrence.png"
        viz.cooccurrence_matrix(
            cooc, sorted_names, artifacts["cooc_matrix"],
            title=f"{filepath.stem} — совместная встречаемость (окно 50 строк)",
        )

    # Главный персонаж
    if sorted_names:
        protagonist = sorted_names[0]
        verdict = (
            f"Главный персонаж: '{protagonist}' ({counts[protagonist]} згадок). "
            f"Всего отслеживается {len(sorted_names)} персонажей. "
            f"Сумма згадок: {sum(counts.values())}."
        )
    else:
        protagonist = None
        verdict = "Персонажи не найдены."

    metrics = {
        "total_words": int(len(text.split())),
        "characters_tracked": len(sorted_names),
        "character_counts": {n: counts[n] for n in sorted_names},
        "protagonist": protagonist,
        "cooccurrence_matrix": cooc.tolist(),
        "cooccurrence_names": sorted_names,
    }

    verdict_lines = report.verdict_block(
        f"Карта персонажей: {filepath.name}",
        report.metric_table(
            ["Персонаж", "Згадок"],
            [[n, str(counts[n])] for n in sorted_names],
        ),
        ["## Вердикт", "", f"**{verdict}**", ""],
    )

    full_report = report.wrap_report(
        task_name="characters",
        engine="POLER v6 (grep_search_file)",
        input_files=[str(filepath)],
        metrics=metrics,
        output_files={k: str(v) for k, v in artifacts.items()},
        verdict=verdict,
    )

    return {
        "report": full_report,
        "verdict_lines": verdict_lines,
        "artifacts": artifacts,
    }

    },
    "smart_diff": {
        "fn": recipe_smart_diff,
        "n_files": None,
        "description": "Fast differential: compare two files or file vs cached version.",
        "options": ["keyword", "cache_dir"],
    },
}


__all__ = [
    "recipe_analyze",
    "recipe_compare",
    "recipe_structure",
    "recipe_characters",
    "recipe_theme_evolution",
    "recipe_themes_alpha",
    "recipe_diff_versions",
    "recipe_batch_directory",
    "recipe_custom_plugin",
    "recipe_smart_read",
    "recipe_smart_search",
    "recipe_smart_batch",
    "recipe_smart_cache",
    "recipe_smart_diff",
    "RECIPES",
]

```
"""
poler_toolkit.report — генерация отчётов.

Два выходных формата:
  - JSON (машинно-читаемый, для downstream обработки)
  - Markdown verdict (человекочитаемый, краткая сводка)

Все отчёты пишутся в одну выходную директорию с timestamp.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Any, Dict, Optional, List

from .errors import OutputError

log = logging.getLogger("poler_toolkit.report")


# ============================================================
# JSON-отчёт
# ============================================================
def save_json_report(
    data: Dict[str, Any],
    out_dir: Path,
    filename: str = "report.json",
) -> Path:
    """
    Сохраняет data как JSON в out_dir/filename.
    Бросает OutputError при падении.
    """
    out_path = out_dir / filename
    try:
        out_path.write_text(
            json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, default=str),
            encoding="utf-8",
        )
        log.info(f"JSON report saved: {out_path}")
        return out_path
    except Exception as e:
        raise OutputError(f"Cannot write {out_path}: {e}")


# ============================================================
# Markdown verdict
# ============================================================
def save_markdown_verdict(
    lines: List[str],
    out_dir: Path,
    filename: str = "verdict.md",
) -> Path:
    """Сохраняет verdict как Markdown."""
    out_path = out_dir / filename
    try:
        out_path.write_text("\n".join(lines), encoding="utf-8")
        log.info(f"Markdown verdict saved: {out_path}")
        return out_path
    except Exception as e:
        raise OutputError(f"Cannot write {out_path}: {e}")


# ============================================================
# Стандартная обёртка для всех отчётов
# ============================================================
def wrap_report(
    task_name: str,
    engine: str,
    input_files: List[str],
    metrics: Dict[str, Any],
    output_files: Dict[str, str],
    verdict: str,
    extra: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Стандартный шаблон отчёта.
    Все recipe-функции возвращают dict этой структуры.
    """
    report = {
        "meta": {
            "task": task_name,
            "engine": engine,
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
            "toolkit_version": "1.0.0",
        },
        "inputs": input_files,
        "metrics": metrics,
        "outputs": output_files,
        "verdict": verdict,
    }
    if extra:
        report["extra"] = extra
    return report


# ============================================================
# Helpers для verdict-ов
# ============================================================
def verdict_block(title: str, *line_groups: List[str]) -> List[str]:
    """
    Форматирует блок verdict-а.
    Принимает title + произвольное количество списков строк,
    склеивает их в один Markdown-блок.
    """
    out = [f"# {title}", ""]
    for lines in line_groups:
        out.extend(lines)
        if not lines or lines[-1] != "":
            out.append("")
    return out


def metric_table(headers: List[str], rows: List[List[str]]) -> List[str]:
    """Markdown-таблица метрик."""
    lines = [
        "| " + " | ".join(headers) + " |",
        "|" + "|".join(["---"] * len(headers)) + "|",
    ]
    for row in rows:
        lines.append("| " + " | ".join(row) + " |")
    lines.append("")
    return lines


def coherence_verdict(mean_cosine: float, silhouette: float) -> str:
    """Авто-вердикт по coherence + silhouette."""
    if mean_cosine < 0.10 and silhouette < 0.05:
        return "КЛАСТЕРИ НЕ СКЛАДАЮТЬСЯ — текст розпадається на iзольованi шматки."
    if mean_cosine < 0.15 and silhouette < 0.15:
        return "СЛАБКА СКЛАДАНIСТЬ — кластери формальнi, але зв'язок ламкий."
    if mean_cosine < 0.25 and silhouette < 0.30:
        return "СЕРЕДНЯ СКЛАДАНIСТЬ — структура ║, але з провалами."
    if mean_cosine >= 0.25 and silhouette >= 0.30:
        return "ВИСОКА СКЛАДАНIСТЬ — кластери тримаються, переходи плавнi."
    return "ЗМIШАНА КАРТИНА — окремi блоки тримаються, iншi розсипаються."


__all__ = [
    "save_json_report",
    "save_markdown_verdict",
    "wrap_report",
    "verdict_block",
    "metric_table",
    "coherence_verdict",
]
```

```
"""
poler_toolkit.runner — оркестратор.

runner.run(recipe_name, filepaths, opts) -> Path (выходная директория)

Шаги:
  1. Проверить recipe_name в RECIPES
  2. Проверить количество файлов
  3. Создать выходную директорию с timestamp
  4. Вызвать recipe-функцию с out_dir
  5. Сохранить report.json + verdict.md
  6. Вернуть путь к выходной директории
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from . import core
from . import report
from . import recipes
from .errors import RecipeError, ConfigurationError

log = logging.getLogger("poler_toolkit.runner")


def list_recipes() -> Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Возвращает словарь доступных рецептов."""
    return recipes.RECIPES


def run(
    recipe_name: str,
    filepaths: List[str | Path],
    *,
    keyword: Optional[str] = None,
    chapter_pattern: Optional[str] = None,
    lang: str = "auto",
    top_n: int = 15,
    names: Optional[List[str]] = None,
    # ---- Stage 2 ext-recipe options (Agents A-E) ----
    plugin_path: Optional[str] = None,
    timeout: int = 300,
    plugin_opts: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
    extensions: Optional[List[str]] = None,
    recursive: bool = True,
    max_locations: int = 20,
    window_size: int = 3000,
    # ---- end ext-recipe options ----
    # ---- smart_toolkit options ----
    mode: str = "summary",
    query: Optional[str] = None,
    max_chars: int = 2000,
    context_chars: int = 500,
    pattern: Optional[str] = None,
    include: Optional[str] = None,
    exclude: Optional[str] = None,
    max_results: int = 50,
    context_lines: int = 2,
    ignore_case: bool = True,
    action: str = "get",
    cache_dir: Optional[str] = None,
    # ---- end smart_toolkit options ----
    output_root: Optional[Path] = None,
    task_label: Optional[str] = None,
) -> Path:
    """
    Запускает recipe на файлах.

    Args:
        recipe_name: 'analyze' / 'compare' / 'structure' / 'characters'
                     / 'theme_evolution' / 'themes_alpha' / 'diff_versions'
                     / 'batch_directory' / 'custom_plugin'
        filepaths: список файлов (1 для analyze/structure/characters/...,
                   2 для compare/diff_versions, 1 dir для batch_directory,
                   переменное число для custom_plugin)
        keyword: keyword для POLER (None = auto-detect)
        chapter_pattern: regex для глав (None = auto)
        lang: язык глав ('auto' / 'ru' / 'ua' / 'en' / 'inline_ru' / 'inline_en')
        top_n: top_n для POLER analyze (default 15)
        names: список персонажей для recipe='characters'
        plugin_path: путь к .py файлу плагина для recipe='custom_plugin'
        timeout: таймаут выполнения плагина в секундах (custom_plugin)
        plugin_opts: dict опций, передаваемых в плагин (custom_plugin)
        extensions: список расширений файлов для batch_directory
                    (default: ['.txt', '.md'])
        recursive: рекурсивный обход каталога для batch_directory
        max_locations: максимум source locations на слово для themes_alpha
        window_size: размер окна POLER для diff_versions
        output_root: корень для выходных файлов (default: env $POLER_TOOLKIT_OUTPUT
                     или ~/.local/share/poler_toolkit/, см. paths.py)
        task_label: метка задачи (default: recipe_name)

    Returns:
        Путь к созданной выходной директории.
    """
    # 1. Проверить recipe
    if recipe_name not in recipes.RECIPES:
        available = ", ".join(recipes.RECIPES.keys())
        raise RecipeError(
            f"unknown recipe '{recipe_name}'",
            hint=f"Available: {available}",
        )
    spec = recipes.RECIPES[recipe_name]
    fn = spec["fn"]
    expected_n = spec["n_files"]

    # 2. Проверить файлы (n_files=None → переменное число, пропуск проверки)
    if expected_n is not None and len(filepaths) != expected_n:
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"recipe '{recipe_name}' expects {expected_n} file(s), got {len(filepaths)}",
            hint=f"Use --help {recipe_name} for usage.",
        )
    if expected_n is None and not filepaths:
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"recipe '{recipe_name}' expects at least 1 file, got 0",
            hint=f"Use --help {recipe_name} for usage.",
        )

    # 3. Выходная директория
    label = task_label or recipe_name
    out_dir = core.make_output_dir(label, output_root=output_root)
    log.info(f"Output dir: {out_dir}")

    # 4. Логирование входа
    log.info(f"=== RUN recipe='{recipe_name}' ===")
    for i, fp in enumerate(filepaths):
        log.info(f"  input[{i}]: {fp}")
    log.info(f"  opts: keyword={keyword}, lang={lang}, top_n={top_n}, names={names}")

    # 5. Вызов recipe-функции
    common_kwargs = {"out_dir": out_dir}
    if "keyword" in spec["options"]:
        common_kwargs["keyword"] = keyword
    if "chapter_pattern" in spec["options"]:
        common_kwargs["chapter_pattern"] = chapter_pattern
    if "lang" in spec["options"]:
        common_kwargs["lang"] = lang
    if "top_n" in spec["options"]:
        common_kwargs["top_n"] = top_n
    if "names" in spec["options"] and names is not None:
        common_kwargs["names"] = names
    # ---- Stage 2 ext-recipe option forwarding ----
    if "plugin_path" in spec["options"] and plugin_path is not None:
        common_kwargs["plugin_path"] = plugin_path
    if "timeout" in spec["options"]:
        common_kwargs["timeout"] = timeout
    if "plugin_opts" in spec["options"] and plugin_opts is not None:
        common_kwargs["plugin_opts"] = plugin_opts
    if "extensions" in spec["options"] and extensions is not None:
        common_kwargs["extensions"] = extensions
    if "recursive" in spec["options"]:
        common_kwargs["recursive"] = recursive
    if "max_locations" in spec["options"]:
        common_kwargs["max_locations"] = max_locations
    if "window_size" in spec["options"]:
        common_kwargs["window_size"] = window_size
    # ---- smart_toolkit option forwarding ----
    if "mode" in spec["options"]:
        common_kwargs["mode"] = mode
    if "query" in spec["options"] and query is not None:
        common_kwargs["query"] = query
    if "max_chars" in spec["options"]:
        common_kwargs["max_chars"] = max_chars
    if "context_chars" in spec["options"]:
        common_kwargs["context_chars"] = context_chars
    if "pattern" in spec["options"] and pattern is not None:
        common_kwargs["pattern"] = pattern
    if "include" in spec["options"] and include is not None:
        common_kwargs["include"] = include
    if "exclude" in spec["options"] and exclude is not None:
        common_kwargs["exclude"] = exclude
    if "max_results" in spec["options"]:
        common_kwargs["max_results"] = max_results
    if "context_lines" in spec["options"]:
        common_kwargs["context_lines"] = context_lines
    if "ignore_case" in spec["options"]:
        common_kwargs["ignore_case"] = ignore_case
    if "action" in spec["options"]:
        common_kwargs["action"] = action
    if "cache_dir" in spec["options"] and cache_dir is not None:
        common_kwargs["cache_dir"] = cache_dir

    bundle = fn(filepaths, **common_kwargs)

    # 6. Сохранить JSON-отчёт
    json_path = report.save_json_report(
        bundle["report"], out_dir, filename="report.json",
    )
    log.info(f"JSON: {json_path}")

    # 7. Сохранить Markdown verdict
    md_path = report.save_markdown_verdict(
        bundle["verdict_lines"], out_dir, filename="verdict.md",
    )
    log.info(f"Markdown: {md_path}")

    # 8. Финальный лог
    log.info(f"=== DONE: {recipe_name} ===")
    log.info(f"  artifacts: {len(bundle['artifacts'])} PNG files")
    log.info(f"  verdict: {bundle['report']['verdict'][:120]}...")

    return out_dir


__all__ = ["run", "list_recipes"]
```
