"""
poler_toolkit.recipes_ext.diff_versions
=======================================

Recipe: compare two versions of the same narrative text (v1 vs v2) and
surface both **POLER's semantic delta** (ε / fragment-count drift around a
shared keyword) and a **chapter-level structural + semantic diff** with
four visualizations.

What the recipe does
--------------------
1. Validate that exactly two files were supplied.
2. Read both files via ``core.read_any`` (supports .txt / .md / .epub).
3. Pick a shared keyword:
   - If ``keyword`` argument is given — use it.
   - Otherwise — auto-detect the most frequent content token across the
     *combined* text of both files via ``core.pick_keyword`` so the chosen
     keyword actually appears in both versions. If that fails (degenerate
     input) fall back to an empty string, which POLER accepts.
4. Call ``poler_v6.diff_files(file1, file2, keyword, window_size)`` via
   ``core.safe_call``. This is the canonical POLER delta — it returns
   ``file1_windows``, ``file2_windows``, ``delta_windows``,
   ``file1_epsilon``, ``file2_epsilon``, ``delta_epsilon`` and the top-3
   fragments per file.
5. Call ``poler_v6.format_diff_markdown(result)`` via ``core.safe_call``
   and save it verbatim as ``diff.md``.
6. Split each file into chapters via ``core.split_chapters`` and build a
   TF-IDF + cosine-similarity matrix ``M[i, j] = cos(v1_chap_i, v2_chap_j)``.
7. For every v1 chapter find its best-matching v2 chapter (argmax over
   ``M``); symmetrically for v2 chapters. Classify each chapter as:

   * **same**      — best-match similarity ≥ ``SAME_THRESHOLD`` (0.95)
   * **changed**   — best-match similarity in (``MATCH_THRESHOLD``,
                     ``SAME_THRESHOLD``)  → run ``difflib`` on tokens to
                     count added/removed tokens within the pair.
   * **removed**   — v1 chapter whose best-match similarity <
                     ``MATCH_THRESHOLD`` (0.30) → the chapter was
                     deleted in v2.
   * **added**     — v2 chapter whose best-match similarity <
                     ``MATCH_THRESHOLD`` → new chapter in v2.

8. Render four PNG artifacts:
   * ``01_diff_heatmap.png``        — v1 × v2 chapter similarity matrix.
   * ``02_changes_per_chapter.png`` — grouped bar chart of added /
                                      removed / changed token counts per
                                      chapter pair (and standalone
                                      removed / added chapters).
   * ``03_wordcount_comparison.png``— side-by-side word counts per
                                      chapter (v1 blue, v2 orange).
   * ``04_semantic_drift.png``      — line chart of best-match cosine
                                      similarity per v1 chapter (1.0 =
                                      identical, 0.0 = completely
                                      different) — the "drift curve".

9. Emit the standard poler_toolkit bundle:

   * ``report.json`` — via ``report.wrap_report`` (meta + inputs +
     metrics + outputs + verdict).
   * ``verdict.md``  — via ``report.verdict_block`` + ``metric_table``.
   * ``diff.md``     — raw output of ``poler_v6.format_diff_markdown``.
   * 4 PNG artifacts listed in ``bundle["artifacts"]``.

The function signature matches the Stage 2 contract so the main agent
can register it in ``recipes.RECIPES`` directly:

    "diff_versions": {
        "fn": recipe_diff_versions,
        "n_files": 2,
        "description": "Compare two versions of a text: POLER diff + chapter-level semantic + structural delta",
        "options": ["keyword", "chapter_pattern", "lang"],
    }

(Runner support: ``runner.run()`` already forwards ``keyword``,
``chapter_pattern`` and ``lang``. The ``window_size`` argument is *not*
in the runner's option list, so the default (3000) is used when invoked
through the runner. Call the recipe directly to override ``window_size``.)
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import difflib
import json
import logging
import re
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

import numpy as np
import matplotlib
matplotlib.use("Agg")
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from sklearn.feature_extraction.text import TfidfVectorizer
from sklearn.metrics.pairwise import cosine_similarity

# Recipe lives in poler_toolkit.recipes_ext, so parent-of-parent is
# poler_toolkit itself. Primary path: relative imports (matches Stage 2
# integration contract). Fallback: absolute imports, so the file can also
# be invoked directly as `python3 .../diff_versions.py FILE1 FILE2` for
# testing.
try:
    from .. import core
    from .. import report
    from .. import viz
    from ..errors import RecipeError, ConfigurationError, PolerEngineError
except ImportError:  # pragma: no cover - script-mode fallback
    import os as _os
    import sys as _sys
    _here = _os.path.dirname(_os.path.abspath(__file__))
    _scripts = _os.path.dirname(_os.path.dirname(_here))
    if _scripts not in _sys.path:
        _sys.path.insert(0, _scripts)
    from poler_toolkit import core  # type: ignore
    from poler_toolkit import report  # type: ignore
    from poler_toolkit import viz  # type: ignore
    from poler_toolkit.errors import (  # type: ignore
        RecipeError, ConfigurationError, PolerEngineError,
    )

log = logging.getLogger("poler_toolkit.recipes_ext.diff_versions")


# ============================================================
# Tunable constants
# ------------------------------------------------------------
# These are structural defaults — not function arguments — because they
# encode what "a meaningful match" means for chapter-level diffing.
# ============================================================
MATCH_THRESHOLD = 0.30
"""Below this best-match cosine similarity a chapter is considered
*removed* (v1) or *added* (v2) rather than modified."""

SAME_THRESHOLD = 0.95
"""At or above this best-match cosine similarity a chapter pair is
considered identical (no edits worth reporting)."""

TOKEN_RE = re.compile(r"[\w’']+")
"""Tokenizer used for chapter-level diffing. Same family as
``core.auto_detect_keyword`` and the other recipes_ext modules so the
counts are directly comparable."""

# Cap on TF-IDF vocabulary so the cosine-similarity matrix builds fast
# even on a 600 KB novel with 35 chapters.
_TFIDF_MAX_FEATURES = 8000


# ============================================================
# Local visualization helpers
# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Stage 2 constraint: do NOT modify viz.py. The existing viz.chapter_matrix
# builds a *square* N×N heatmap of one text's chapters, but we need a
# rectangular v1×v2 matrix. The other three charts (grouped changes bar,
# side-by-side word counts, drift line) don't exist in viz.py at all.
# Implement them locally so viz.py stays untouched for the main agent.
# ============================================================
def _short_label(title: str) -> str:
    """Collapse 'Глава 12 (Робоча назва): Уламок' -> '12'."""
    m = re.search(r"\d+", title)
    return m.group(0) if m else title[:10]


def _safe_savefig(fig, path: Path) -> None:
    """Save + close. Honours toolkit DPI."""
    try:
        fig.savefig(path, dpi=viz.DPI)
        log.info(f"Saved: {path}")
    except Exception as e:
        raise viz.VisualizationError(f"savefig failed: {e}") from e
    finally:
        plt.close(fig)


def _diff_heatmap(
    sim_matrix: np.ndarray,
    v1_titles: List[str],
    v2_titles: List[str],
    out_path: Path,
    title: str = "v1 × v2 chapter similarity",
) -> None:
    """Rectangular heatmap: rows = v1 chapters, cols = v2 chapters.

    Colour scale 0..1 (cosine similarity). The diagonal (where it exists)
    is highlighted with a thin red outline so the eye can find it on
    roughly-aligned versions.
    """
    n1, n2 = sim_matrix.shape
    fig_w = max(8, n2 * 0.55 + 2)
    fig_h = max(7, n1 * 0.55 + 2)
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(fig_w, fig_h), constrained_layout=True)
    im = ax.imshow(sim_matrix, cmap="viridis", vmin=0, vmax=1, aspect="auto")
    ax.set_title(title, fontsize=13, pad=10)
    x_labels = [_short_label(t) for t in v2_titles]
    y_labels = [_short_label(t) for t in v1_titles]
    ax.set_xticks(range(n2))
    ax.set_yticks(range(n1))
    ax.set_xticklabels(x_labels, rotation=90, fontsize=max(5, 10 - n2 // 15))
    ax.set_yticklabels(y_labels, fontsize=max(5, 10 - n1 // 15))
    ax.set_xlabel("v2 chapter")
    ax.set_ylabel("v1 chapter")
    fig.colorbar(im, ax=ax, fraction=0.046, pad=0.02, label="cosine sim")
    # Annotate cells with the similarity value when the matrix isn't too big
    if n1 * n2 <= 200:
        for i in range(n1):
            for j in range(n2):
                v = sim_matrix[i, j]
                color = "white" if v < 0.55 else "black"
                ax.text(j, i, f"{v:.2f}", ha="center", va="center",
                        fontsize=max(5, 9 - n2 // 10), color=color)
    _safe_savefig(fig, out_path)


def _changes_per_chapter(
    rows: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    out_path: Path,
    title: str = "Added / Removed / Changed tokens per chapter",
) -> None:
    """Grouped bar chart of added / removed / changed token counts.

    Each entry in ``rows`` describes one chapter pair (or a standalone
    removed/added chapter) and must have the keys:
        ``label``, ``added``, ``removed``, ``changed``.
    """
    n = len(rows)
    if n == 0:
        rows = [{"label": "(none)", "added": 0, "removed": 0, "changed": 0}]
        n = 1
    labels = [r["label"] for r in rows]
    added = np.array([r["added"] for r in rows], dtype=np.float64)
    removed = np.array([r["removed"] for r in rows], dtype=np.float64)
    changed = np.array([r["changed"] for r in rows], dtype=np.float64)
    fig_w = max(10, n * 1.4 + 2)
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(fig_w, 6), constrained_layout=True)
    x = np.arange(n)
    w = 0.27
    b1 = ax.bar(x - w, added, w, color=viz.COLOR_OK, alpha=0.85,
                edgecolor="black", linewidth=0.4, label="Added")
    b2 = ax.bar(x, removed, w, color=viz.COLOR_BAD, alpha=0.85,
                edgecolor="black", linewidth=0.4, label="Removed")
    b3 = ax.bar(x + w, changed, w, color=viz.COLOR_WARN, alpha=0.85,
                edgecolor="black", linewidth=0.4, label="Changed (in pair)")
    # Annotate nonzero bars
    for bars, vals in [(b1, added), (b2, removed), (b3, changed)]:
        for bar, v in zip(bars, vals):
            if v > 0:
                ax.text(bar.get_x() + bar.get_width() / 2, v + max(added.max(),
                        removed.max(), changed.max(), 1) * 0.01, str(int(v)),
                        ha="center", va="bottom", fontsize=7)
    ax.set_xticks(x)
    ax.set_xticklabels(labels, rotation=45, ha="right", fontsize=8)
    ax.set_xlabel("Chapter pair (or standalone chapter)")
    ax.set_ylabel("Token count")
    ax.set_title(title, fontsize=13, pad=10)
    ax.legend(loc="upper right", fontsize=9)
    ax.grid(alpha=0.25, axis="y")
    _safe_savefig(fig, out_path)


def _wordcount_comparison(
    v1_titles: List[str],
    v1_counts: List[int],
    v2_titles: List[str],
    v2_counts: List[int],
    out_path: Path,
    label_a: str = "v1",
    label_b: str = "v2",
    title: str = "Word count per chapter",
) -> None:
    """Side-by-side bars: v1 chapters (left) vs v2 chapters (right).

    Two separate bar groups (v1 in blue, v2 in orange) plotted on the
    same x-axis (chapter index, 1-based). When the chapter counts differ,
    the missing side is simply zero — which makes added/removed chapters
    visually obvious.
    """
    n = max(len(v1_titles), len(v2_titles), 1)
    v1_padded = v1_counts + [0] * (n - len(v1_counts))
    v2_padded = v2_counts + [0] * (n - len(v2_counts))
    v1_labels = [_short_label(t) for t in v1_titles] + [""] * (n - len(v1_titles))
    v2_labels = [_short_label(t) for t in v2_titles] + [""] * (n - len(v2_titles))
    # Use combined labels: prefer v1 label, fallback to v2 label
    labels = []
    for i in range(n):
        lbl = v1_labels[i] if v1_labels[i] else v2_labels[i]
        labels.append(lbl if lbl else str(i + 1))

    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(max(10, n * 1.2 + 2), 6),
                           constrained_layout=True)
    x = np.arange(n)
    w = 0.4
    ax.bar(x - w / 2, v1_padded, w, color=viz.COLOR_SECONDARY, alpha=0.85,
           edgecolor="black", linewidth=0.4, label=label_a)
    ax.bar(x + w / 2, v2_padded, w, color=viz.COLOR_WARN, alpha=0.85,
           edgecolor="black", linewidth=0.4, label=label_b)
    # Annotate nonzero bars
    for i, (a, b) in enumerate(zip(v1_padded, v2_padded)):
        if a > 0:
            ax.text(i - w / 2, a + max(max(v1_padded + v2_padded), 1) * 0.01,
                    str(int(a)), ha="center", va="bottom", fontsize=7)
        if b > 0:
            ax.text(i + w / 2, b + max(max(v1_padded + v2_padded), 1) * 0.01,
                    str(int(b)), ha="center", va="bottom", fontsize=7)
    ax.set_xticks(x)
    ax.set_xticklabels(labels, rotation=45, ha="right", fontsize=8)
    ax.set_xlabel("Chapter index")
    ax.set_ylabel("Word count (tokens)")
    ax.set_title(title, fontsize=13, pad=10)
    ax.legend(loc="upper right", fontsize=9)
    ax.grid(alpha=0.25, axis="y")
    _safe_savefig(fig, out_path)


def _semantic_drift(
    drift_points: List[Tuple[str, float]],
    out_path: Path,
    threshold_same: float = SAME_THRESHOLD,
    threshold_match: float = MATCH_THRESHOLD,
    title: str = "Semantic drift per v1 chapter (cosine sim of best match)",
) -> None:
    """Line chart of best-match cosine similarity per v1 chapter.

    ``drift_points`` is a list of ``(label, similarity)`` tuples, one per
    v1 chapter. The chart shows two reference lines:
        * ``SAME_THRESHOLD``   (chapters above this are essentially unchanged)
        * ``MATCH_THRESHOLD``  (chapters below this are *removed* in v2)
    Between the two lines = edited.
    """
    n = len(drift_points)
    if n == 0:
        drift_points = [("(none)", 0.0)]
        n = 1
    labels = [lbl for lbl, _ in drift_points]
    sims = np.array([s for _, s in drift_points], dtype=np.float64)
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(max(10, n * 1.0 + 2), 6),
                           constrained_layout=True)
    xs = np.arange(n)
    ax.plot(xs, sims, color=viz.COLOR_PRIMARY, marker="o", markersize=8,
            linewidth=2, label="best-match cosine sim", zorder=3)
    ax.fill_between(xs, sims, 0, color=viz.COLOR_PRIMARY, alpha=0.10, zorder=1)
    ax.axhline(threshold_same, color=viz.COLOR_OK, linestyle="--",
               linewidth=1.2, alpha=0.8,
               label=f"same (≥{threshold_same:.2f})")
    ax.axhline(threshold_match, color=viz.COLOR_BAD, linestyle="--",
               linewidth=1.2, alpha=0.8,
               label=f"removed (<{threshold_match:.2f})")
    ax.axhspan(threshold_match, threshold_same, color=viz.COLOR_WARN,
               alpha=0.10, zorder=0, label="edited zone")
    # Annotate each point
    for x, s, lbl in zip(xs, sims, labels):
        ax.text(x, s + 0.025, f"{s:.2f}", ha="center", va="bottom",
                fontsize=8, fontweight="bold")
    ax.set_xticks(xs)
    ax.set_xticklabels(labels, rotation=45, ha="right", fontsize=8)
    ax.set_xlabel("v1 chapter")
    ax.set_ylabel("cosine similarity (v1 chapter ↔ best v2 chapter)")
    ax.set_ylim(-0.05, 1.08)
    ax.set_title(title, fontsize=13, pad=10)
    ax.legend(loc="lower right", fontsize=8)
    ax.grid(alpha=0.25)
    _safe_savefig(fig, out_path)


# ============================================================
# Token-level diff (within a matched chapter pair)
# ============================================================
def _tokenize_local(text: str) -> List[str]:
    return TOKEN_RE.findall(text.lower())


def _pair_token_diff(v1_text: str, v2_text: str) -> Dict[str, int]:
    """
    Use difflib.SequenceMatcher on tokens to count:

    * ``added``   — tokens present in v2 but not aligned with v1
    * ``removed`` — tokens present in v1 but not aligned with v2
    * ``changed`` — min(added, removed): tokens that *probably* changed
                    (rather than purely added/removed); we count this
                    separately so the bar chart can show three series.
                    The remainder (|added − removed|) stays in the
                    larger of the two buckets.

    The mapping is conservative: ``changed = min(added, removed)`` and
    the remainder is subtracted from the larger bucket so the totals add
    up to ``max(added_raw, removed_raw)``.
    """
    a = _tokenize_local(v1_text)
    b = _tokenize_local(v2_text)
    sm = difflib.SequenceMatcher(a=a, b=b, autojunk=False)
    added_raw = 0
    removed_raw = 0
    for tag, i1, i2, j1, j2 in sm.get_opcodes():
        if tag == "replace":
            removed_raw += (i2 - i1)
            added_raw += (j2 - j1)
        elif tag == "delete":
            removed_raw += (i2 - i1)
        elif tag == "insert":
            added_raw += (j2 - j1)
        # "equal" contributes to neither
    changed = min(added_raw, removed_raw)
    added = added_raw - changed
    removed = removed_raw - changed
    return {"added": added, "removed": removed, "changed": changed,
            "added_raw": added_raw, "removed_raw": removed_raw}


# ============================================================
# Main recipe
# ============================================================
def recipe_diff_versions(
    filepaths: list,
    *,
    keyword: Optional[str] = None,
    window_size: int = 3000,
    chapter_pattern: Optional[str] = None,
    lang: str = "auto",
    out_dir: Path = None,
) -> dict:
    """Returns {"report": dict, "verdict_lines": list, "artifacts": dict}.

    See module docstring for the full description.
    """
    # ----- 1. validate inputs ------------------------------------------
    if not isinstance(filepaths, (list, tuple)) or len(filepaths) != 2:
        raise ConfigurationError(
            "diff_versions expects exactly 2 files",
            hint="Supply [v1, v2] — the two versions of the same text.",
        )
    fp1 = Path(filepaths[0])
    fp2 = Path(filepaths[1])
    if not fp1.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"v1 file not found: {fp1}")
    if not fp2.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"v2 file not found: {fp2}")
    log.info(f"[diff_versions] v1={fp1.name}  v2={fp2.name}")

    # ----- 2. read both files ------------------------------------------
    text1, fmt1 = core.read_any(fp1)
    text2, fmt2 = core.read_any(fp2)
    log.info(f"  v1: {len(text1):,} chars ({fmt1})")
    log.info(f"  v2: {len(text2):,} chars ({fmt2})")

    # ----- 3. pick a shared keyword ------------------------------------
    chosen_keyword = keyword
    if not chosen_keyword:
        combined = text1 + "\n" + text2
        try:
            chosen_keyword = core.pick_keyword(combined, hint=None)
            log.info(f"  auto-detected keyword: '{chosen_keyword}'")
        except Exception as e:
            log.warning(f"  auto-detect keyword failed ({e}); falling back to ''")
            chosen_keyword = ""

    # ----- 4. POLER diff_files -----------------------------------------
    try:
        poler_diff = core.safe_call(
            "diff_files",
            str(fp1), str(fp2), chosen_keyword, int(window_size),
        )
    except PolerEngineError as e:
        log.warning(f"POLER diff_files failed: {e}")
        # Don't fail the whole recipe — fall back to a minimal stub so the
        # chapter-level diff still works.
        poler_diff = {
            "keyword": chosen_keyword,
            "file1": str(fp1),
            "file2": str(fp2),
            "file1_windows": 0,
            "file2_windows": 0,
            "file1_epsilon": 0.0,
            "file2_epsilon": 0.0,
            "delta_windows": 0,
            "delta_epsilon": 0.0,
            "file1_top": [],
            "file2_top": [],
            "_error": str(e),
        }

    # ----- 5. POLER format_diff_markdown ------------------------------
    try:
        diff_md = core.safe_call("format_diff_markdown", poler_diff)
    except PolerEngineError as e:
        log.warning(f"format_diff_markdown failed: {e}")
        diff_md = f"<!-- format_diff_markdown failed: {e} -->\n"

    # ----- 6. chapter split + TF-IDF cosine matrix ---------------------
    chapters_v1 = core.split_chapters(text1, pattern=chapter_pattern, lang=lang)
    chapters_v2 = core.split_chapters(text2, pattern=chapter_pattern, lang=lang)

    v1_titles = [c["title"] for c in chapters_v1]
    v2_titles = [c["title"] for c in chapters_v2]
    v1_bodies = [c["body"] for c in chapters_v1]
    v2_bodies = [c["body"] for c in chapters_v2]

    n1, n2 = len(v1_bodies), len(v2_bodies)
    log.info(f"  chapters: v1={n1}, v2={n2}")

    # Edge case: one or both sides have 0 chapters
    sim_matrix = np.zeros((max(n1, 1), max(n2, 1)), dtype=np.float64)
    if n1 > 0 and n2 > 0:
        try:
            vec = TfidfVectorizer(
                max_features=_TFIDF_MAX_FEATURES,
                token_pattern=r"(?u)\b[\w’']+\b",
                lowercase=True,
            )
            X = vec.fit_transform(v1_bodies + v2_bodies)
            X1 = X[:n1]
            X2 = X[n1:]
            sim_matrix = cosine_similarity(X1, X2)
        except Exception as e:
            log.warning(f"TF-IDF cosine similarity failed: {e}")
            sim_matrix = np.zeros((n1, n2), dtype=np.float64)

    # ----- 7. chapter matching + classification ------------------------
    # best_v1[i] = index of v2 chapter best matching v1 chapter i
    best_v1 = sim_matrix.argmax(axis=1) if n2 > 0 else np.zeros(n1, dtype=int)
    best_v1_sim = sim_matrix.max(axis=1) if n2 > 0 else np.zeros(n1)
    # best_v2[j] = index of v1 chapter best matching v2 chapter j
    best_v2 = sim_matrix.argmax(axis=0) if n1 > 0 else np.zeros(n2, dtype=int)
    best_v2_sim = sim_matrix.max(axis=0) if n1 > 0 else np.zeros(n2)

    # Per-chapter token counts (for word-count chart + standalone add/rm)
    v1_word_counts = [len(_tokenize_local(b)) for b in v1_bodies]
    v2_word_counts = [len(_tokenize_local(b)) for b in v2_bodies]

    # Build the per-chapter-pair changes table
    # Each row: {label, kind, added, removed, changed, v1_idx, v2_idx, sim}
    change_rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    drift_points: List[Tuple[str, float]] = []
    matched_pairs: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    removed_chapters_v1: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    added_chapters_v2: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    same_count = 0
    changed_count = 0

    # Track which v2 chapters got claimed by a v1 best-match (mutual)
    v2_claimed: set = set()

    for i in range(n1):
        lbl = _short_label(v1_titles[i]) if v1_titles else str(i + 1)
        sim = float(best_v1_sim[i]) if n2 > 0 else 0.0
        drift_points.append((lbl, sim))
        if n2 == 0:
            # All v1 chapters are "removed" since v2 has nothing
            removed_chapters_v1.append({
                "v1_idx": i, "label": lbl,
                "tokens": v1_word_counts[i], "best_sim": 0.0,
            })
            change_rows.append({
                "label": f"v1.C{lbl} (removed)",
                "kind": "removed",
                "added": 0,
                "removed": v1_word_counts[i],
                "changed": 0,
                "v1_idx": i, "v2_idx": None, "sim": 0.0,
            })
            continue
        j = int(best_v1[i])
        # Decide classification based on best-match similarity.
        if sim < MATCH_THRESHOLD:
            removed_chapters_v1.append({
                "v1_idx": i, "label": lbl,
                "tokens": v1_word_counts[i], "best_sim": sim,
            })
            change_rows.append({
                "label": f"v1.C{lbl} (removed)",
                "kind": "removed",
                "added": 0,
                "removed": v1_word_counts[i],
                "changed": 0,
                "v1_idx": i, "v2_idx": None, "sim": sim,
            })
        else:
            # This is a real pair — claim the v2 chapter
            v2_claimed.add(j)
            v2_lbl = _short_label(v2_titles[j]) if v2_titles else str(j + 1)
            if sim >= SAME_THRESHOLD:
                same_count += 1
                change_rows.append({
                    "label": f"v1.C{lbl}→v2.C{v2_lbl} (same)",
                    "kind": "same",
                    "added": 0, "removed": 0, "changed": 0,
                    "v1_idx": i, "v2_idx": j, "sim": sim,
                })
            else:
                changed_count += 1
                d = _pair_token_diff(v1_bodies[i], v2_bodies[j])
                change_rows.append({
                    "label": f"v1.C{lbl}→v2.C{v2_lbl} (changed)",
                    "kind": "changed",
                    "added": d["added"],
                    "removed": d["removed"],
                    "changed": d["changed"],
                    "v1_idx": i, "v2_idx": j, "sim": sim,
                })
            matched_pairs.append({
                "v1_idx": i, "v2_idx": j,
                "v1_label": lbl, "v2_label": v2_lbl,
                "similarity": sim,
                "v1_tokens": v1_word_counts[i],
                "v2_tokens": v2_word_counts[j],
            })

    # Any v2 chapter not claimed by a v1 best-match is "added" — but only
    # if its own best-match similarity is also below threshold (otherwise
    # it's just a secondary match for a v1 chapter that already paired
    # elsewhere, which can happen when v1 has duplicates).
    for j in range(n2):
        sim = float(best_v2_sim[j]) if n1 > 0 else 0.0
        v2_lbl = _short_label(v2_titles[j]) if v2_titles else str(j + 1)
        if j in v2_claimed:
            continue
        if sim < MATCH_THRESHOLD or n1 == 0:
            added_chapters_v2.append({
                "v2_idx": j, "label": v2_lbl,
                "tokens": v2_word_counts[j], "best_sim": sim,
            })
            change_rows.append({
                "label": f"v2.C{v2_lbl} (added)",
                "kind": "added",
                "added": v2_word_counts[j],
                "removed": 0,
                "changed": 0,
                "v1_idx": None, "v2_idx": j, "sim": sim,
            })

    # ----- 8. metrics dict (for report.json) ---------------------------
    total_added = sum(r["added"] for r in change_rows)
    total_removed = sum(r["removed"] for r in change_rows)
    total_changed = sum(r["changed"] for r in change_rows)
    mean_drift_sim = float(np.mean([s for _, s in drift_points])) if drift_points else 0.0
    min_drift_sim = float(np.min([s for _, s in drift_points])) if drift_points else 0.0
    max_drift_sim = float(np.max([s for _, s in drift_points])) if drift_points else 0.0

    metrics: Dict[str, Any] = {
        # inputs
        "file1": str(fp1),
        "file2": str(fp2),
        "file1_chars": int(len(text1)),
        "file2_chars": int(len(text2)),
        "file1_format": fmt1,
        "file2_format": fmt2,
        # POLER diff_files result (raw)
        "poler_diff": {
            "keyword": poler_diff.get("keyword", chosen_keyword),
            "file1_windows": int(poler_diff.get("file1_windows", 0)),
            "file2_windows": int(poler_diff.get("file2_windows", 0)),
            "delta_windows": int(poler_diff.get("delta_windows", 0)),
            "file1_epsilon": float(poler_diff.get("file1_epsilon", 0.0)),
            "file2_epsilon": float(poler_diff.get("file2_epsilon", 0.0)),
            "delta_epsilon": float(poler_diff.get("delta_epsilon", 0.0)),
            "file1_top_count": len(poler_diff.get("file1_top", [])),
            "file2_top_count": len(poler_diff.get("file2_top", [])),
            "_error": poler_diff.get("_error"),
        },
        # chapter-level structural diff
        "chapters": {
            "v1_count": n1,
            "v2_count": n2,
            "v1_titles": v1_titles,
            "v2_titles": v2_titles,
        },
        "classification": {
            "same": same_count,
            "changed": changed_count,
            "removed": len(removed_chapters_v1),
            "added": len(added_chapters_v2),
        },
        "token_diff_totals": {
            "added": int(total_added),
            "removed": int(total_removed),
            "changed": int(total_changed),
        },
        "drift": {
            "mean_best_match_similarity": round(mean_drift_sim, 4),
            "min_best_match_similarity": round(min_drift_sim, 4),
            "max_best_match_similarity": round(max_drift_sim, 4),
            "match_threshold": MATCH_THRESHOLD,
            "same_threshold": SAME_THRESHOLD,
        },
        "similarity_matrix_shape": [int(n1), int(n2)],
        # detailed tables (for downstream tooling)
        "change_rows": change_rows,
        "matched_pairs": matched_pairs,
        "removed_chapters_v1": removed_chapters_v1,
        "added_chapters_v2": added_chapters_v2,
        "word_counts_v1": v1_word_counts,
        "word_counts_v2": v2_word_counts,
        # config
        "config": {
            "window_size": int(window_size),
            "chapter_pattern": chapter_pattern,
            "lang": lang,
            "keyword": chosen_keyword,
            "match_threshold": MATCH_THRESHOLD,
            "same_threshold": SAME_THRESHOLD,
        },
    }

    # ----- 9. write outputs (only if out_dir given) --------------------
    artifacts: Dict[str, Path] = {}
    written_files: List[Path] = []
    diff_md_path: Optional[Path] = None
    if out_dir is not None:
        out_dir = Path(out_dir)
        out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

        # 9a. diff.md (raw POLER markdown)
        diff_md_path = out_dir / "diff.md"
        diff_md_path.write_text(diff_md, encoding="utf-8")
        written_files.append(diff_md_path)
        log.info(f"  wrote {diff_md_path}")

        # 9b. PNG artifacts — only render if there are chapters on both sides
        if n1 > 0 and n2 > 0:
            artifacts["diff_heatmap"] = out_dir / "01_diff_heatmap.png"
            _diff_heatmap(
                sim_matrix,
                v1_titles, v2_titles,
                artifacts["diff_heatmap"],
                title=f"v1 × v2 chapter similarity — {fp1.stem} vs {fp2.stem}",
            )
            written_files.append(artifacts["diff_heatmap"])

            artifacts["changes_per_chapter"] = out_dir / "02_changes_per_chapter.png"
            _changes_per_chapter(
                change_rows,
                artifacts["changes_per_chapter"],
                title=f"Added / Removed / Changed tokens per chapter — {fp1.stem} vs {fp2.stem}",
            )
            written_files.append(artifacts["changes_per_chapter"])

            artifacts["wordcount_comparison"] = out_dir / "03_wordcount_comparison.png"
            _wordcount_comparison(
                v1_titles, v1_word_counts,
                v2_titles, v2_word_counts,
                artifacts["wordcount_comparison"],
                label_a=fp1.stem[:15] or "v1",
                label_b=fp2.stem[:15] or "v2",
                title=f"Word count per chapter — {fp1.stem} vs {fp2.stem}",
            )
            written_files.append(artifacts["wordcount_comparison"])

            artifacts["semantic_drift"] = out_dir / "04_semantic_drift.png"
            _semantic_drift(
                drift_points,
                artifacts["semantic_drift"],
                title=f"Semantic drift per v1 chapter — {fp1.stem} vs {fp2.stem}",
            )
            written_files.append(artifacts["semantic_drift"])
        else:
            log.warning(
                f"Skipping PNG artifacts: chapters v1={n1}, v2={n2} "
                "(need at least 1 chapter on each side)",
            )

    # ----- 10. verdict -------------------------------------------------
    # Auto-verdict text
    cls = metrics["classification"]
    if n1 == 0 and n2 == 0:
        verdict_text = "ОБА ФАЙЛА ПУСТЫ — дифф не построен."
    elif n2 == 0:
        verdict_text = (
            f"v2 ПУСТОЙ: v1 содержит {n1} глав, v2 — 0. "
            f"Все главы удалены."
        )
    elif n1 == 0:
        verdict_text = (
            f"v1 ПУСТОЙ: v1 — 0 глав, v2 содержит {n2} глав. "
            f"Все главы добавлены."
        )
    else:
        verdict_text = (
            f"Глав: v1={n1}, v2={n2}. "
            f"Совпадают: {cls['same']}, изменены: {cls['changed']}, "
            f"удалено: {cls['removed']}, добавлено: {cls['added']}. "
            f"Токенов: +{total_added}/−{total_removed}/~{total_changed}. "
            f"Средняя схожесть лучших пар: {mean_drift_sim:.3f}. "
            f"POLER δ(windows)={poler_diff.get('delta_windows', 0):+d}, "
            f"δ(ε)={poler_diff.get('delta_epsilon', 0.0):+.1f} по «{chosen_keyword}»."
        )

    # Detailed verdict table
    verdict_lines = report.verdict_block(
        f"Diff Versions: {fp1.name} vs {fp2.name}",
        report.metric_table(
            ["Метрика", "v1", "v2", "Δ"],
            [
                ["Файл", fp1.name, fp2.name, ""],
                ["Формат", fmt1, fmt2, ""],
                ["Символов", str(len(text1)), str(len(text2)),
                 f"{len(text2) - len(text1):+d}"],
                ["Глав", str(n1), str(n2), f"{n2 - n1:+d}"],
                ["POLER windows",
                 str(poler_diff.get("file1_windows", 0)),
                 str(poler_diff.get("file2_windows", 0)),
                 f"{poler_diff.get('delta_windows', 0):+d}"],
                ["POLER Σε",
                 f"{poler_diff.get('file1_epsilon', 0.0):.1f}",
                 f"{poler_diff.get('file2_epsilon', 0.0):.1f}",
                 f"{poler_diff.get('delta_epsilon', 0.0):+.1f}"],
                ["Токенов (всего)",
                 str(sum(v1_word_counts)),
                 str(sum(v2_word_counts)),
                 f"{sum(v2_word_counts) - sum(v1_word_counts):+d}"],
                ["Совпадающих глав", str(cls["same"]), "", ""],
                ["Изменённых глав", str(cls["changed"]), "", ""],
                ["Удалённых глав", str(cls["removed"]), "", ""],
                ["Добавленных глав", str(cls["added"]), "", ""],
                ["Токенов добавлено", "", "", f"+{total_added}"],
                ["Токенов удалено", "", "", f"−{total_removed}"],
                ["Токенов изменено", "", "", f"~{total_changed}"],
                ["Средняя схожесть пар", "", "", f"{mean_drift_sim:.3f}"],
                ["Мин. схожесть пары", "", "", f"{min_drift_sim:.3f}"],
                ["Макс. схожесть пары", "", "", f"{max_drift_sim:.3f}"],
                ["Keyword", chosen_keyword or "(пусто)", "", ""],
                ["Window size", str(window_size), "", ""],
            ],
        ),
        [
            "## Классификация глав",
            "",
            "| v1 глава | → | v2 глава | sim | статус |",
            "|---|---|---|---|---|",
            *(
                f"| C{r.get('v1_idx') + 1 if r.get('v1_idx') is not None else '—'} "
                f"| → | "
                f"C{r.get('v2_idx') + 1 if r.get('v2_idx') is not None else '—'} "
                f"| {r['sim']:.3f} | {r['kind']} |"
                for r in change_rows
            ),
            "",
        ],
        ["## Вердикт", "", f"**{verdict_text}**", ""],
    )

    # ----- 11. wrap into standard report bundle ------------------------
    full_report = report.wrap_report(
        task_name="diff_versions",
        engine="POLER v6 diff_files + TF-IDF cosine + difflib",
        input_files=[str(fp1), str(fp2)],
        metrics=metrics,
        output_files={k: str(v) for k, v in artifacts.items()},
        verdict=verdict_text,
        extra={
            "diff_md_path": str(diff_md_path) if diff_md_path else None,
            "poler_diff_raw": {
                k: v for k, v in poler_diff.items()
                if k not in ("file1_top", "file2_top")
            },
            "n_chapters_v1": n1,
            "n_chapters_v2": n2,
            "files_written": [str(p) for p in written_files],
        },
    )

    return {
        "report": full_report,
        "verdict_lines": verdict_lines,
        "artifacts": artifacts,
    }


__all__ = ["recipe_diff_versions"]


# ============================================================
# Standalone test entry-point
# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Run:  python3 poler_toolkit/recipes_ext/diff_versions.py [FILE1 FILE2]
# If no files given, defaults to /tmp/diff_test/{v1,v2}.txt.
# Output: $POLER_TOOLKIT_OUTPUT/test_diff_versions_<ts>/ with diff.md,
# 4 PNG artifacts, report.json, verdict.md.
# ============================================================
if __name__ == "__main__":
    import sys
    import os
    from datetime import datetime

    logging.basicConfig(
        level=logging.INFO,
        format="%(asctime)s [%(name)s] %(levelname)s: %(message)s",
    )

    if len(sys.argv) >= 3:
        test_file1, test_file2 = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
    else:
        # Default: use the synthetic v1/v2 in /tmp/diff_test/, auto-create
        # them if missing (mirrors the spec's test scenario).
        test_file1 = "/tmp/diff_test/v1.txt"
        test_file2 = "/tmp/diff_test/v2.txt"
        if not (os.path.exists(test_file1) and os.path.exists(test_file2)):
            print("Default test files not found — please create them first.")
            print(f"  expected: {test_file1}")
            print(f"  expected: {test_file2}")
            sys.exit(1)

    # Output dir via paths.py (env-aware)
    from .. import paths as _paths
    ts = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
    out_dir = _paths.get_output_root() / f"test_diff_versions_{ts}"
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    print(f"Running diff_versions on:")
    print(f"  v1: {test_file1}")
    print(f"  v2: {test_file2}")
    print(f"Output dir: {out_dir}")

    bundle = recipe_diff_versions(
        [test_file1, test_file2],
        keyword=None,
        window_size=3000,
        chapter_pattern=None,
        lang="auto",
        out_dir=out_dir,
    )

    # Write report.json + verdict.md (mirrors what runner.run() does)
    (out_dir / "report.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(
            bundle["report"], ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, default=str,
        ),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    (out_dir / "verdict.md").write_text(
        "\n".join(bundle["verdict_lines"]), encoding="utf-8",
    )

    print("\n=== DONE ===")
    print(f"Artifacts ({len(bundle['artifacts'])}):")
    for name, p in bundle["artifacts"].items():
        print(f"  {name:24s} -> {p}  (exists={p.exists()})")
    print(f"\ndiff.md:     {out_dir / 'diff.md'}")
    print(f"report.json: {out_dir / 'report.json'}")
    print(f"verdict.md:  {out_dir / 'verdict.md'}")
    print(f"\nVerdict: {bundle['report']['verdict']}")

    # Print classification summary
    cls = bundle["report"]["metrics"]["classification"]
    print(f"\nClassification:")
    print(f"  same:     {cls['same']}")
    print(f"  changed:  {cls['changed']}")
    print(f"  removed:  {cls['removed']}")
    print(f"  added:    {cls['added']}")

```
"""
poler_toolkit.recipes_ext.smart_toolkit
========================================

Token-efficient data processing recipes that replace standard tools
(read / grep / glob) with compact, context-aware output.

5 recipes:
  - smart_read    — read file with summary / context / streaming modes
  - smart_search  — unified search: regex + full-text + file discovery
  - smart_batch   — batch process N files into compact stats
  - smart_cache   — persistent analysis cache (store / get / list)
  - smart_diff    — fast differential between files or vs cache

Design principle: never dump full file contents into output.
Every recipe returns compact JSON metrics only.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import math
import os
import re
import time
from collections import Counter
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

from .. import core, report
from ..errors import RecipeError, ConfigurationError, FileError

try:
    import poler_v6 as P
except ImportError:
    import sys
    sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent))
    import poler_v6 as P


# ============================================================
# Shared helpers
# ============================================================

def _file_stats(text: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    words = text.split()
    total = len(words)
    counts = Counter(w.lower() for w in words if len(w) > 2)
    entropy = 0.0
    if counts:
        total_tok = sum(counts.values())
        entropy = -sum((c / total_tok) * math.log2(c / total_tok)
                       for c in counts.values())
    top_words = counts.most_common(20)
    return {
        "chars": len(text),
        "words": total,
        "lines": text.count("\n") + 1,
        "unique_words": len(counts),
        "entropy": round(entropy, 3),
        "top_words": [{"word": w, "count": c} for w, c in top_words],
    }


def _file_meta(filepath: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    p = Path(filepath)
    stat = p.stat()
    return {
        "path": str(p),
        "name": p.name,
        "extension": p.suffix.lower(),
        "size_bytes": stat.st_size,
        "modified": stat.st_mtime,
    }


def _quick_hash(filepath: str) -> str:
    p = Path(filepath)
    stat = p.stat()
    return hashlib.md5(f"{p}:{stat.st_size}:{stat.st_mtime}".encode()).hexdigest()[:12]


# ============================================================
# RECIPE: smart_read
# ============================================================

def recipe_smart_read(
    filepaths: List[str | Path],
    *,
    mode: str = "summary",
    query: Optional[str] = None,
    max_chars: int = 2000,
    top_n: int = 20,
    context_chars: int = 500,
    out_dir: Path = None,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Token-efficient file reading.

    Modes:
      summary   — stats + top words + sections (no file content)
      context   — only text around query matches
      full      — first/last N chars + stats (for small files)
      streaming — chunk summary for huge files
    """
    if len(filepaths) != 1:
        raise ConfigurationError("smart_read expects exactly 1 file")
    filepath = str(filepaths[0])
    if not os.path.exists(filepath):
        raise FileError(filepath, "file not found")

    meta = _file_meta(filepath)
    text, fmt = core.read_any(filepath)

    if mode == "summary":
        stats = _file_stats(text)
        sections = P.detect_sections(text)
        section_list = [{"title": s["title"], "position": s["position"]} for s in sections]
        metrics = {
            "mode": "summary",
            "file": meta,
            "format": fmt,
            "stats": stats,
            "sections": section_list[:30],
            "section_count": len(section_list),
        }
        verdict = (
            f"File: {meta['name']} ({meta['size_bytes']:,} bytes) — "
            f"{stats['words']:,} words, {stats['lines']:,} lines, "
            f"entropy={stats['entropy']:.2f}, "
            f"{len(section_list)} sections detected."
        )

    elif mode == "context":
        if not query:
            raise ConfigurationError("context mode requires --query")
        index = P.build_search_index(text)
        results = P.search_in_text(text, query, index, max_results=top_n,
                                    context_chars=context_chars)
        compact_results = []
        for r in results:
            compact_results.append({
                "position": r["position"],
                "score": r["score"],
                "matched_words": r["matched_words"],
                "context": r["context"][:300],
            })
        stats = _file_stats(text)
        metrics = {
            "mode": "context",
            "file": meta,
            "query": query,
            "total_matches": len(results),
            "results": compact_results,
            "file_stats": {
                "words": stats["words"],
                "entropy": stats["entropy"],
            },
        }
        verdict = (
            f"Query '{query}' in {meta['name']}: "
            f"{len(results)} context windows found."
        )

    elif mode == "full":
        preview_head = text[:max_chars // 2]
        preview_tail = text[-max_chars // 2:] if len(text) > max_chars // 2 else ""
        stats = _file_stats(text)
        sections = P.detect_sections(text)
        metrics = {
            "mode": "full",
            "file": meta,
            "format": fmt,
            "stats": stats,
            "sections": [{"title": s["title"], "position": s["position"]}
                         for s in sections[:30]],
            "preview_head": preview_head[:1000],
            "preview_tail": preview_tail[:1000],
            "truncated": len(text) > max_chars,
        }
        verdict = (
            f"File: {meta['name']} — showing first/last {max_chars} chars of "
            f"{stats['chars']:,} total."
        )

    elif mode == "streaming":
        chunk_size = 100000
        chunks = []
        offset = 0
        with open(filepath, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as f:
            while True:
                data = f.read(chunk_size)
                if not data:
                    break
                chunk_stats = _file_stats(data)
                chunks.append({
                    "offset": offset,
                    "chars": len(data),
                    "words": chunk_stats["words"],
                    "entropy": chunk_stats["entropy"],
                    "top3": chunk_stats["top_words"][:3],
                })
                offset += len(data)
        metrics = {
            "mode": "streaming",
            "file": meta,
            "chunks": chunks,
            "total_chunks": len(chunks),
            "total_chars": sum(c["chars"] for c in chunks),
        }
        verdict = (
            f"File {meta['name']} split into {len(chunks)} chunks "
            f"({sum(c['chars'] for c in chunks):,} chars total)."
        )
    else:
        raise ConfigurationError(f"Unknown mode '{mode}'. Use: summary, context, full, streaming")

    full_report = report.wrap_report(
        task_name="smart_read",
        engine="smart_toolkit",
        input_files=[filepath],
        metrics=metrics,
        output_files={},
        verdict=verdict,
    )
    return {
        "report": full_report,
        "verdict_lines": report.verdict_block(
            f"Smart Read: {meta['name']}",
            report.metric_table(
                ["Parameter", "Value"],
                [
                    ["Mode", mode],
                    ["File", str(filepath)],
                    ["Size", f"{meta['size_bytes']:,} bytes"],
                    ["Words", str(metrics.get("stats", metrics.get("file_stats", {})).get("words", "N/A"))],
                ],
            ),
            [f"## Result", "", f"**{verdict}**", ""],
        ),
        "artifacts": {},
    }


# ============================================================
# RECIPE: smart_search
# ============================================================

def recipe_smart_search(
    filepaths: List[str | Path],
    *,
    pattern: Optional[str] = None,
    keyword: Optional[str] = None,
    include: Optional[str] = None,
    exclude: Optional[str] = None,
    max_results: int = 50,
    context_lines: int = 2,
    ignore_case: bool = True,
    recursive: bool = True,
    out_dir: Path = None,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Unified search: regex + full-text + file discovery in one call.
    Returns ranked results — never full file contents.
    """
    search_pattern = pattern or keyword
    if not search_pattern:
        raise ConfigurationError("Either --pattern or --keyword is required")

    all_results = []
    files_scanned = 0
    files_with_hits = 0

    for fp in filepaths:
        p = Path(str(fp))
        if p.is_file():
            files_to_search = [str(p)]
        elif p.is_dir():
            files_to_search = P.scan_directory(str(p))
        else:
            continue

        for fpath in files_to_search:
            fname = Path(fpath).name
            if include and not Path(fname).match(include):
                continue
            if exclude and Path(fname).match(exclude):
                continue

            try:
                size = os.path.getsize(fpath)
                if size > 10 * 1024 * 1024 or size == 0:
                    continue
            except OSError:
                continue

            grep_results = P.grep_search_file(
                fpath, search_pattern,
                ignore_case=ignore_case,
                context_lines=context_lines,
                max_matches=max_results,
            )

            files_scanned += 1
            if grep_results:
                files_with_hits += 1
                for r in grep_results:
                    all_results.append({
                        "file": fpath,
                        "line_number": r.line_number,
                        "line": r.line[:200],
                        "before": r.before_context[:1],
                        "after": r.after_context[:1],
                    })

    all_results.sort(key=lambda x: x["line_number"])
    if len(all_results) > max_results:
        all_results = all_results[:max_results]

    metrics = {
        "pattern": search_pattern,
        "files_scanned": files_scanned,
        "files_with_hits": files_with_hits,
        "total_matches": len(all_results),
        "results": all_results,
    }
    verdict = (
        f"Pattern '{search_pattern}': {len(all_results)} matches "
        f"in {files_with_hits}/{files_scanned} files."
    )

    full_report = report.wrap_report(
        task_name="smart_search",
        engine="smart_toolkit + poler_v6 grep",
        input_files=[str(f) for f in filepaths],
        metrics=metrics,
        output_files={},
        verdict=verdict,
    )
    return {
        "report": full_report,
        "verdict_lines": report.verdict_block(
            f"Smart Search: '{search_pattern}'",
            report.metric_table(
                ["Parameter", "Value"],
                [
                    ["Pattern", search_pattern],
                    ["Files scanned", str(files_scanned)],
                    ["Files with hits", str(files_with_hits)],
                    ["Total matches", str(len(all_results))],
                ],
            ),
            [f"## Result", "", f"**{verdict}**", ""],
        ),
        "artifacts": {},
    }


# ============================================================
# RECIPE: smart_batch
# ============================================================

def recipe_smart_batch(
    filepaths: List[str | Path],
    *,
    keyword: Optional[str] = None,
    extensions: Optional[List[str]] = None,
    recursive: bool = True,
    top_n: int = 10,
    out_dir: Path = None,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Batch process N files into compact stats.
    No full file content in output — only metrics.
    """
    target_path = Path(str(filepaths[0]))
    if target_path.is_file():
        file_list = [str(target_path)]
    elif target_path.is_dir():
        file_list = P.scan_directory(str(target_path), extensions)
    else:
        raise FileError(str(target_path), "not a file or directory")

    if not file_list:
        raise RecipeError("no matching files found")

    per_file = []
    total_words = 0
    total_chars = 0
    for fpath in file_list:
        try:
            meta = _file_meta(fpath)
            text, fmt = core.read_any(fpath)
            stats = _file_stats(text)
            per_file.append({
                "file": meta["name"],
                "path": fpath,
                "extension": meta["extension"],
                "size_bytes": meta["size_bytes"],
                "words": stats["words"],
                "chars": stats["chars"],
                "lines": stats["lines"],
                "entropy": stats["entropy"],
                "top5": stats["top_words"][:5],
            })
            total_words += stats["words"]
            total_chars += stats["chars"]
        except Exception as e:
            per_file.append({
                "file": Path(fpath).name,
                "path": fpath,
                "error": str(e),
            })

    per_file.sort(key=lambda x: x.get("words", 0), reverse=True)
    top_files = per_file[:top_n]

    word_extremes = {
        "largest": per_file[0]["file"] if per_file else None,
        "most_entropy": max((f for f in per_file if "entropy" in f),
                            key=lambda x: x["entropy"], default=None),
        "least_entropy": min((f for f in per_file if "entropy" in f),
                             key=lambda x: x["entropy"], default=None),
    }

    metrics = {
        "total_files": len(per_file),
        "total_words": total_words,
        "total_chars": total_chars,
        "avg_words_per_file": total_words // max(len(per_file), 1),
        "top_files": top_files,
        "extremes": word_extremes,
        "by_extension": _group_by_ext(per_file),
    }
    verdict = (
        f"Batch: {len(per_file)} files, {total_words:,} words total, "
        f"{total_chars:,} chars. "
        f"Largest: {word_extremes['largest'] or 'N/A'}."
    )

    full_report = report.wrap_report(
        task_name="smart_batch",
        engine="smart_toolkit",
        input_files=[str(target_path)],
        metrics=metrics,
        output_files={},
        verdict=verdict,
    )
    return {
        "report": full_report,
        "verdict_lines": report.verdict_block(
            f"Smart Batch: {target_path.name}",
            report.metric_table(
                ["Parameter", "Value"],
                [
                    ["Files", str(len(per_file))],
                    ["Total words", f"{total_words:,}"],
                    ["Total chars", f"{total_chars:,}"],
                    ["Avg words/file", f"{total_words // max(len(per_file), 1):,}"],
                ],
            ),
            [f"## Result", "", f"**{verdict}**", ""],
        ),
        "artifacts": {},
    }


def _group_by_ext(per_file: List[Dict]) -> Dict[str, Dict]:
    groups: Dict[str, Dict] = {}
    for f in per_file:
        ext = f.get("extension", "?")
        if ext not in groups:
            groups[ext] = {"count": 0, "total_words": 0, "total_chars": 0}
        groups[ext]["count"] += 1
        groups[ext]["total_words"] += f.get("words", 0)
        groups[ext]["total_chars"] += f.get("chars", 0)
    return groups


# ============================================================
# RECIPE: smart_cache
# ============================================================

# Cache dir resolved via paths.py (env-aware: $POLER_TOOLKIT_CACHE / XDG)
try:
    from ..paths import get_cache_root as _get_cache_root
    _CACHE_DIR = _get_cache_root()
except ImportError:
    _CACHE_DIR = Path.home() / ".poler_cache"


def recipe_smart_cache(
    filepaths: List[str | Path],
    *,
    action: str = "get",
    cache_dir: Optional[str] = None,
    keyword: Optional[str] = None,
    out_dir: Path = None,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Persistent analysis cache.
      store — analyze file + save to cache
      get   — return cached result (or analyze if miss)
      list  — list all cached entries
      clear — remove all cache entries
    """
    cache_path = Path(cache_dir) if cache_dir else _CACHE_DIR
    cache_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    if action == "list":
        entries = []
        for cf in sorted(cache_path.glob("*.json")):
            try:
                data = json.loads(cf.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
                entries.append({
                    "cache_file": cf.name,
                    "source": data.get("source_file", "?"),
                    "cached_at": data.get("cached_at", "?"),
                    "words": data.get("stats", {}).get("words", 0),
                })
            except Exception:
                pass
        metrics = {"action": "list", "entries": entries, "count": len(entries)}
        verdict = f"Cache contains {len(entries)} entries."
        full_report = report.wrap_report(
            task_name="smart_cache", engine="smart_toolkit",
            input_files=[], metrics=metrics, output_files={}, verdict=verdict,
        )
        return {
            "report": full_report,
            "verdict_lines": report.verdict_block(
                "Smart Cache — List", [], [f"**{verdict}**"],
            ),
            "artifacts": {},
        }

    if action == "clear":
        count = 0
        for cf in cache_path.glob("*.json"):
            cf.unlink()
            count += 1
        metrics = {"action": "clear", "removed": count}
        verdict = f"Cleared {count} cache entries."
        full_report = report.wrap_report(
            task_name="smart_cache", engine="smart_toolkit",
            input_files=[], metrics=metrics, output_files={}, verdict=verdict,
        )
        return {
            "report": full_report,
            "verdict_lines": report.verdict_block(
                "Smart Cache — Clear", [], [f"**{verdict}**"],
            ),
            "artifacts": {},
        }

    if len(filepaths) < 1:
        raise ConfigurationError("smart_cache requires at least 1 file for store/get")
    filepath = str(filepaths[0])
    if not os.path.exists(filepath):
        raise FileError(filepath, "file not found")

    file_hash = _quick_hash(filepath)
    cache_file = cache_path / f"{file_hash}.json"

    if action == "get" and cache_file.exists():
        data = json.loads(cache_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        data["cache_hit"] = True
        metrics = data
        verdict = f"Cache HIT for {Path(filepath).name} (cached at {data.get('cached_at', '?')})."
        full_report = report.wrap_report(
            task_name="smart_cache", engine="smart_toolkit",
            input_files=[filepath], metrics=metrics, output_files={}, verdict=verdict,
        )
        return {
            "report": full_report,
            "verdict_lines": report.verdict_block(
                f"Smart Cache — {Path(filepath).name}",
                [], [f"**{verdict}**"],
            ),
            "artifacts": {},
        }

    if action == "get" and not cache_file.exists():
        action = "store"

    if action == "store":
        text, fmt = core.read_any(filepath)
        stats = _file_stats(text)
        sections = P.detect_sections(text)
        keyword_val = keyword or core.pick_keyword(text)
        poler_result = P.analyze_text(text, keyword_val, top_n=5, source_file=filepath)

        cache_data = {
            "source_file": filepath,
            "cached_at": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S"),
            "file_hash": file_hash,
            "format": fmt,
            "stats": stats,
            "keyword": keyword_val,
            "sections": [{"title": s["title"], "position": s["position"]}
                         for s in sections[:50]],
            "top_fragments": poler_result.get("top_fragments", [])[:5],
            "total_occurrences": poler_result.get("total_occurrences", 0),
        }
        cache_file.write_text(
            json.dumps(cache_data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, default=str),
            encoding="utf-8",
        )
        cache_data["cache_hit"] = False
        metrics = cache_data
        verdict = f"Cached {Path(filepath).name} ({stats['words']:,} words, keyword='{keyword_val}')."
        full_report = report.wrap_report(
            task_name="smart_cache", engine="smart_toolkit",
            input_files=[filepath], metrics=metrics, output_files={}, verdict=verdict,
        )
        return {
            "report": full_report,
            "verdict_lines": report.verdict_block(
                f"Smart Cache — Store {Path(filepath).name}",
                report.metric_table(
                    ["Parameter", "Value"],
                    [
                        ["File", filepath],
                        ["Hash", file_hash],
                        ["Words", f"{stats['words']:,}"],
                        ["Keyword", keyword_val],
                        ["Cache", str(cache_file)],
                    ],
                ),
                [f"**{verdict}**"],
            ),
            "artifacts": {},
        }

    raise ConfigurationError(f"Unknown action '{action}'. Use: store, get, list, clear")


# ============================================================
# RECIPE: smart_diff
# ============================================================

def recipe_smart_diff(
    filepaths: List[str | Path],
    *,
    keyword: Optional[str] = None,
    cache_dir: Optional[str] = None,
    out_dir: Path = None,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Fast differential: compare two files OR file vs cached version.
    Returns ONLY the delta — no full file dumps.
    """
    cache_path = Path(cache_dir) if cache_dir else _CACHE_DIR

    if len(filepaths) == 1:
        filepath = str(filepaths[0])
        file_hash = _quick_hash(filepath)
        cache_file = cache_path / f"{file_hash}.json"
        if not cache_file.exists():
            raise RecipeError(
                f"no cache entry for {Path(filepath).name}",
                hint="Run 'smart_cache store' first, or pass 2 files.",
            )
        cached = json.loads(cache_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        text_a = json.dumps(cached.get("stats", {}))
        label_a = f"cached({Path(filepath).name})"
        text_b = cached.get("stats", {}).get("words", 0)
        source_file = filepath
    elif len(filepaths) == 2:
        fp_a, fp_b = str(filepaths[0]), str(filepaths[1])
        text_a, _ = core.read_any(fp_a)
        text_b, _ = core.read_any(fp_b)
        label_a = Path(fp_a).name
        source_file = f"{fp_a} vs {fp_b}"
    else:
        raise ConfigurationError("smart_diff expects 1 or 2 files")

    if len(filepaths) == 2:
        stats_a = _file_stats(text_a)
        stats_b = _file_stats(text_b)

        words_a = set(w.lower() for w in text_a.split() if len(w) > 2)
        words_b = set(w.lower() for w in text_b.split() if len(w) > 2)
        common = words_a & words_b
        only_a = words_a - words_b
        only_b = words_b - words_a

        keyword_val = keyword or core.pick_keyword(text_a + " " + text_b)
        r1 = P.analyze_text(text_a, keyword_val, top_n=3, source_file=fp_a)
        r2 = P.analyze_text(text_b, keyword_val, top_n=3, source_file=fp_b)

        metrics = {
            "file_a": fp_a,
            "file_b": fp_b,
            "stats_a": stats_a,
            "stats_b": stats_b,
            "delta": {
                "words": stats_b["words"] - stats_a["words"],
                "chars": stats_b["chars"] - stats_a["chars"],
                "lines": stats_b["lines"] - stats_a["lines"],
                "entropy": round(stats_b["entropy"] - stats_a["entropy"], 3),
            },
            "vocabulary": {
                "common_words": len(common),
                "only_in_a": len(only_a),
                "only_in_b": len(only_b),
                "top_only_a": sorted(list(only_a))[:20],
                "top_only_b": sorted(list(only_b))[:20],
            },
            "keyword": keyword_val,
            "occurrences_a": r1.get("total_occurrences", 0),
            "occurrences_b": r2.get("total_occurrences", 0),
        }
        verdict = (
            f"Diff: {label_a} vs {Path(fp_b).name}: "
            f"Δ words={metrics['delta']['words']:+d}, "
            f"Δ entropy={metrics['delta']['entropy']:+.3f}, "
            f"{len(only_a)} words only in A, {len(only_b)} only in B."
        )
    else:
        stats_a = cached.get("stats", {})
        metrics = {
            "file": source_file,
            "cached_stats": stats_a,
            "keyword": cached.get("keyword", "?"),
            "cache_hit": True,
        }
        verdict = f"Diff vs cache for {Path(source_file).name}: stats loaded from cache."

    full_report = report.wrap_report(
        task_name="smart_diff",
        engine="smart_toolkit",
        input_files=[source_file] if len(filepaths) == 1 else [fp_a, fp_b],
        metrics=metrics,
        output_files={},
        verdict=verdict,
    )
    return {
        "report": full_report,
        "verdict_lines": report.verdict_block(
            f"Smart Diff: {label_a}",
            [f"## Result", "", f"**{verdict}**", ""],
        ),
        "artifacts": {},
    }


__all__ = [
    "recipe_smart_read",
    "recipe_smart_search",
    "recipe_smart_batch",
    "recipe_smart_cache",
    "recipe_smart_diff",
]
