```
"""
poler_toolkit.recipes_ext.theme_evolution
=========================================

Recipe: track how ``EMOTIONAL_MARKERS`` from POLER v6 evolve across chapters
of a narrative text.

What the recipe does
--------------------
1. Read the file via ``core.read_any`` (supports .txt / .md / .epub).
2. Split the text into chapters via ``core.split_chapters``.
3. For each chapter, count occurrences of every marker in
   ``poler_v6.EMOTIONAL_MARKERS`` (a set of 60 multi-lingual terms:
   RU / UK / EN).
4. Normalize counts by chapter length (markers per 1000 tokens) so that
   long and short chapters are directly comparable.
5. Rank markers by total raw frequency and pick top-N (default 20) for the
   visualizations.
6. Render three PNG artifacts:
   - **Stacked area chart** — top-N marker *proportions* per chapter.
   - **Heatmap** — chapters (rows) × top-N markers (cols), normalized
     density on a ``magma`` colour scale.
   - **Line chart** — top-5 markers' evolution across chapters.
7. Emit a JSON ``report`` with the full chapters × markers matrix, the
   per-chapter dominant marker, and variance statistics.
8. Emit a Markdown ``verdict`` identifying:
   - Most stable marker (lowest variance of normalized density).
   - Most volatile marker (highest variance of normalized density).
   - Chapter with the highest emotional density.
   - Chapter with the lowest emotional density.

The function signature matches the Stage 2 contract so the main agent can
register it in ``recipes.RECIPES`` directly:

    "theme_evolution": {
        "fn": recipe_theme_evolution,
        "n_files": 1,
        "description": "Evolution of EMOTIONAL_MARKERS across chapters",
        "options": ["chapter_pattern", "lang", "top_n"],
    }
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
import logging
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional
from collections import Counter

import numpy as np
import matplotlib
matplotlib.use("Agg")
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

# Recipe lives in poler_toolkit.recipes_ext, so parent-of-parent is
# poler_toolkit itself. Primary path: relative imports (matches Stage 2
# integration contract). Fallback: absolute imports, so the file can also
# be invoked directly as `python3 .../theme_evolution.py FILE` for testing.
try:
    from .. import core
    from .. import report
    from .. import viz
    from ..errors import RecipeError, ConfigurationError
except ImportError:  # pragma: no cover - script-mode fallback
    import os as _os
    import sys as _sys
    _here = _os.path.dirname(_os.path.abspath(__file__))
    _scripts = _os.path.dirname(_os.path.dirname(_here))
    if _scripts not in _sys.path:
        _sys.path.insert(0, _scripts)
    from poler_toolkit import core  # type: ignore
    from poler_toolkit import report  # type: ignore
    from poler_toolkit import viz  # type: ignore
    from poler_toolkit.errors import RecipeError, ConfigurationError  # type: ignore

log = logging.getLogger("poler_toolkit.recipes_ext.theme_evolution")


# ============================================================
# Local visualization helpers
# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Stage 2 constraint: do NOT modify viz.py. The existing viz.* functions
# cover heatmaps for cosine similarity (square matrix) and stacked BAR
# charts, but the task explicitly asks for stacked AREA + a non-square
# chapter×marker heatmap + a multi-series line chart. We implement those
# three locally so viz.py stays untouched for the main agent.
# ============================================================
def _short_label(title: str) -> str:
    """Collapse 'Глава 12 (Робоча назва): Уламок' -> '12'."""
    m = re.search(r"\d+", title)
    return m.group(0) if m else title[:10]


def _safe_savefig(fig, path: Path) -> None:
    """Save + close. Honours toolkit DPI."""
    try:
        fig.savefig(path, dpi=viz.DPI)
        log.info(f"Saved: {path}")
    except Exception as e:
        raise viz.VisualizationError(f"savefig failed: {e}") from e
    finally:
        plt.close(fig)


def _stacked_area(
    matrix: np.ndarray,           # shape (chapters, n_markers)
    markers: List[str],
    chapter_titles: List[str],
    out_path: Path,
    title: str = "Marker proportions per chapter",
) -> None:
    """
    Stacked area chart of top-N marker *proportions* per chapter.

    Each chapter's row is normalized so that the visible top-N markers sum
    to 1.0 (residual 'other' mass, if any, is dropped — it would always be
    zero here because we sum over the same top-N subset).
    """
    n_chap, n_m = matrix.shape
    row_sums = matrix.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)
    row_sums[row_sums == 0] = 1.0
    prop = matrix / row_sums

    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(13, 6), constrained_layout=True)
    x = np.arange(n_chap)
    colors = plt.cm.tab20(np.linspace(0, 1, max(n_m, 1)))
    stack = np.zeros(n_chap)
    for k in range(n_m):
        ax.fill_between(
            x, stack, stack + prop[:, k],
            color=colors[k], alpha=0.85, linewidth=0.4,
            label=markers[k],
        )
        stack = stack + prop[:, k]

    labels = [_short_label(t) for t in chapter_titles]
    ax.set_xticks(x)
    ax.set_xticklabels(
        labels, rotation=90, fontsize=max(5, 8 - n_chap // 30),
    )
    ax.set_xlabel("Глава")
    ax.set_ylabel("Доля маркера (в top-N)")
    ax.set_ylim(0, 1.0)
    ax.set_title(title, fontsize=13, pad=10)
    ax.legend(
        loc="center left", bbox_to_anchor=(1.01, 0.5),
        fontsize=7, ncol=1, framealpha=0.9,
    )
    ax.grid(alpha=0.25, axis="y")
    _safe_savefig(fig, out_path)


def _marker_heatmap(
    matrix: np.ndarray,           # shape (chapters, n_markers)
    markers: List[str],
    chapter_titles: List[str],
    out_path: Path,
    title: str = "Marker density heatmap",
    cmap: str = "magma",
) -> None:
    """
    Heatmap chapters × markers, normalized density (markers / 1000 tokens).

    Rows are markers (sorted by total frequency desc), columns are chapters
    in textual order. Cell colour encodes density.
    """
    n_chap, n_m = matrix.shape
    fig_w = max(10, n_chap * 0.32)
    fig_h = max(6, n_m * 0.40 + 2)
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(
        figsize=(fig_w, fig_h), constrained_layout=True,
    )
    display = matrix.T  # rows=markers, cols=chapters
    vmax = float(display.max()) if display.size and display.max() > 0 else 1.0
    im = ax.imshow(
        display, aspect="auto", cmap=cmap, vmin=0, vmax=vmax,
    )
    ax.set_xticks(range(n_chap))
    ax.set_yticks(range(n_m))
    chap_labels = [_short_label(t) for t in chapter_titles]
    ax.set_xticklabels(
        chap_labels, rotation=90, fontsize=max(5, 8 - n_chap // 30),
    )
    ax.set_yticklabels(markers, fontsize=max(7, 10 - n_m // 15))
    ax.set_xlabel("Глава")
    ax.set_ylabel("Маркер")
    ax.set_title(title, fontsize=13, pad=10)
    fig.colorbar(
        im, ax=ax, fraction=0.025, pad=0.02,
        label="маркеров / 1000 токенов",
    )
    _safe_savefig(fig, out_path)


def _top5_line_chart(
    matrix: np.ndarray,           # shape (chapters, k)  (k <= 5)
    markers: List[str],
    chapter_titles: List[str],
    out_path: Path,
    title: str = "Top-5 markers evolution",
) -> None:
    """Line chart of top-5 markers' normalized density across chapters."""
    n_chap, k = matrix.shape
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(13, 6), constrained_layout=True)
    x = np.arange(n_chap)
    colors = plt.cm.tab10(np.linspace(0, 1, max(k, 1)))
    for j in range(k):
        ax.plot(
            x, matrix[:, j],
            marker="o", markersize=3.5, linewidth=1.7,
            color=colors[j], label=markers[j], alpha=0.9,
        )
    labels = [_short_label(t) for t in chapter_titles]
    ax.set_xticks(x)
    ax.set_xticklabels(
        labels, rotation=90, fontsize=max(5, 8 - n_chap // 30),
    )
    ax.set_xlabel("Глава")
    ax.set_ylabel("Маркеров на 1000 токенов")
    ax.set_title(title, fontsize=13, pad=10)
    ax.legend(loc="best", fontsize=9, ncol=min(k, 2), framealpha=0.9)
    ax.grid(alpha=0.3)
    _safe_savefig(fig, out_path)


# ============================================================
# Recipe
# ============================================================
def recipe_theme_evolution(
    filepaths: list,
    *,
    chapter_pattern: Optional[str] = None,
    lang: str = "auto",
    top_n: int = 20,
    out_dir: Path = None,
) -> dict:
    """
    Track how ``poler_v6.EMOTIONAL_MARKERS`` evolve across chapters.

    Args:
        filepaths: length-1 list with the input file path
            (.txt / .md / .epub supported via ``core.read_any``).
        chapter_pattern: optional regex for chapter headings. If ``None``,
            ``core.split_chapters`` auto-detects.
        lang: chapter-pattern language hint (``auto`` / ``ru`` / ``ua`` /
            ``en`` / ``inline_ru`` / ``inline_en``).
        top_n: how many top markers to keep for the heatmap + stacked area
            chart (default 20). Line chart always shows the top 5.
        out_dir: directory for PNG artifacts. If ``None``, no artifacts
            are written (useful when stacking recipes).

    Returns:
        ``{"report": dict, "verdict_lines": list, "artifacts": dict}``.
        The ``artifacts`` mapping contains at most three entries:
        ``stacked_area``, ``heatmap``, ``line_chart``.
    """
    # ----- 0. validate inputs -------------------------------------------
    if len(filepaths) != 1:
        raise ConfigurationError(
            "theme_evolution expects exactly 1 file",
            hint="Pass a single .txt / .md / .epub path.",
        )
    filepath = Path(filepaths[0])
    log.info(f"[theme_evolution] file={filepath}")

    # ----- 1. read + split chapters -------------------------------------
    text, fmt = core.read_any(filepath)
    log.info(f"  read: {len(text)} chars, format={fmt}")

    chapters = core.split_chapters(text, pattern=chapter_pattern, lang=lang)
    n_chap = len(chapters)
    log.info(f"  chapters: {n_chap}")
    if n_chap < 1:
        raise RecipeError(
            "no chapters detected",
            hint="Try --lang inline_ru or pass an explicit chapter_pattern.",
        )

    # ----- 2. load EMOTIONAL_MARKERS (sorted for stable column order) --
    poler = core.get_poler()
    markers = sorted(poler.EMOTIONAL_MARKERS)
    n_markers = len(markers)
    log.info(f"  markers: {n_markers}")

    # ----- 3. per-chapter counts + token counts -------------------------
    # NB: we deliberately use a local regex tokenizer here (matches what
    # core.auto_detect_keyword and recipes.recipe_analyze do) instead of
    # 60×N calls to core.safe_call('grep_search_file', ...) — the latter
    # would re-scan the file 60 times per chapter and is needlessly slow.
    # The pattern is word-aware (Cyrillic-safe via re.UNICODE default).
    token_re = re.compile(r"[\w’']+")
    raw_counts = np.zeros((n_chap, n_markers), dtype=np.int64)
    token_counts = np.zeros(n_chap, dtype=np.int64)
    for ci, ch in enumerate(chapters):
        body_lower = ch["body"].lower()
        tokens = token_re.findall(body_lower)
        token_counts[ci] = len(tokens)
        tc = Counter(tokens)
        for mi, m in enumerate(markers):
            raw_counts[ci, mi] = tc.get(m, 0)
    log.info(
        f"  total markers found: {int(raw_counts.sum())} "
        f"across {int((raw_counts.sum(axis=0) > 0).sum())}/{n_markers} active markers"
    )

    # ----- 4. normalize: markers per 1000 tokens ------------------------
    norm_matrix = np.zeros((n_chap, n_markers), dtype=np.float64)
    for ci in range(n_chap):
        if token_counts[ci] > 0:
            norm_matrix[ci] = raw_counts[ci] / token_counts[ci] * 1000.0

    # ----- 5. rank markers by total raw frequency -----------------------
    total_per_marker = raw_counts.sum(axis=0)
    top_n_actual = max(1, min(top_n, n_markers))
    # indices sorted by total count descending
    top_idx_list = list(np.argsort(-total_per_marker)[:top_n_actual])
    top_markers = [markers[i] for i in top_idx_list]
    top_norm = norm_matrix[:, top_idx_list]
    log.info(f"  top-{top_n_actual} markers: {top_markers[:5]} ...")

    # Top-5 for the line chart
    top5_idx = top_idx_list[:5]
    top5_markers = [markers[i] for i in top5_idx]
    top5_norm = norm_matrix[:, top5_idx]

    # ----- 6. per-chapter dominant marker -------------------------------
    dominant_per_chapter: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    for ci in range(n_chap):
        row = norm_matrix[ci]
        if row.sum() == 0:
            dominant_per_chapter.append({
                "chapter_index": ci,
                "title": chapters[ci]["title"],
                "marker": None,
                "density_per_1k": 0.0,
                "unique_markers": 0,
            })
        else:
            mi = int(np.argmax(row))
            dominant_per_chapter.append({
                "chapter_index": ci,
                "title": chapters[ci]["title"],
                "marker": markers[mi],
                "density_per_1k": float(row[mi]),
                "unique_markers": int((raw_counts[ci] > 0).sum()),
            })

    # ----- 7. variance per marker (on normalized values) ----------------
    # Stable = low variance of per-1000-tokens density across chapters.
    # Volatile = high variance.
    #
    # Two filters apply:
    #   - For 'most stable' we additionally require the marker to appear
    #     in >= min(max(2, n_chap // 4), n_chap) chapters. Without this
    #     filter, a marker that appears exactly once in one long chapter
    #     (giving a tiny density value) would have variance ≈ 0 and
    #     win 'most stable' — semantically wrong. We want 'stable' to
    #     mean 'consistently present at a similar rate'.
    #   - For 'most volatile' we only require appearance in >= 1 chapter
    #     (singletons can still spike).
    marker_variance = np.zeros(n_markers, dtype=np.float64)
    chapters_appearing = np.zeros(n_markers, dtype=np.int64)
    for mi in range(n_markers):
        if total_per_marker[mi] > 0:
            marker_variance[mi] = float(np.var(norm_matrix[:, mi]))
            chapters_appearing[mi] = int((raw_counts[:, mi] > 0).sum())
    appears_mask = total_per_marker > 0
    n_appearing = int(appears_mask.sum())

    # threshold for "stable": appear in at least 25% of chapters (>= 2)
    stable_min_chapters = max(2, min(n_chap, n_chap // 4))
    stable_mask = appears_mask & (chapters_appearing >= stable_min_chapters)
    if stable_mask.any():
        stable_idx_pool = np.where(stable_mask)[0]
        var_stable = marker_variance[stable_idx_pool]
        most_stable_idx = int(stable_idx_pool[int(np.argmin(var_stable))])
    elif n_appearing > 0:
        # fall back to all appearing markers if none is "consistent"
        appearing_idx = np.where(appears_mask)[0]
        var_among = marker_variance[appearing_idx]
        most_stable_idx = int(appearing_idx[int(np.argmin(var_among))])
    else:
        most_stable_idx = 0

    if n_appearing > 0:
        appearing_idx = np.where(appears_mask)[0]
        var_among = marker_variance[appearing_idx]
        most_volatile_idx = int(appearing_idx[int(np.argmax(var_among))])
    else:
        most_volatile_idx = 0

    # ----- 8. emotional density per chapter -----------------------------
    density_per_chapter = norm_matrix.sum(axis=1)
    if n_chap > 0 and density_per_chapter.sum() > 0:
        hi_ch = int(np.argmax(density_per_chapter))
        lo_ch = int(np.argmin(density_per_chapter))
    else:
        hi_ch = 0
        lo_ch = 0

    # ----- 9. render artifacts ------------------------------------------
    artifacts: Dict[str, Path] = {}
    if out_dir is not None:
        out_dir = Path(out_dir)
        out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

        chapter_titles = [c["title"] for c in chapters]

        artifacts["stacked_area"] = out_dir / "01_stacked_area.png"
        _stacked_area(
            top_norm, top_markers, chapter_titles,
            artifacts["stacked_area"],
            title=f"{filepath.stem} — эволюция маркеров (stacked area)",
        )

        artifacts["heatmap"] = out_dir / "02_heatmap.png"
        _marker_heatmap(
            top_norm, top_markers, chapter_titles,
            artifacts["heatmap"],
            title=f"{filepath.stem} — плотность маркеров (на 1000 токенов)",
        )

        artifacts["line_chart"] = out_dir / "03_line_top5.png"
        _top5_line_chart(
            top5_norm, top5_markers, chapter_titles,
            artifacts["line_chart"],
            title=f"{filepath.stem} — топ-5 маркеров (на 1000 токенов)",
        )

    # ----- 10. build metrics dict for JSON ------------------------------
    stable = {
        "marker": markers[most_stable_idx],
        "variance": float(marker_variance[most_stable_idx]),
        "mean_density_per_1k": float(norm_matrix[:, most_stable_idx].mean()),
        "total_count": int(total_per_marker[most_stable_idx]),
        "chapters_appearing": int(chapters_appearing[most_stable_idx]),
    }
    volatile = {
        "marker": markers[most_volatile_idx],
        "variance": float(marker_variance[most_volatile_idx]),
        "mean_density_per_1k": float(norm_matrix[:, most_volatile_idx].mean()),
        "total_count": int(total_per_marker[most_volatile_idx]),
        "chapters_appearing": int(chapters_appearing[most_volatile_idx]),
    }
    hi = {
        "chapter_index": hi_ch,
        "title": chapters[hi_ch]["title"],
        "density_per_1k": float(density_per_chapter[hi_ch]),
        "tokens": int(token_counts[hi_ch]),
    }
    lo = {
        "chapter_index": lo_ch,
        "title": chapters[lo_ch]["title"],
        "density_per_1k": float(density_per_chapter[lo_ch]),
        "tokens": int(token_counts[lo_ch]),
    }

    metrics: Dict[str, Any] = {
        # input summary
        "file": str(filepath),
        "format": fmt,
        "total_chars": int(len(text)),
        "total_words": int(len(text.split())),
        "total_tokens_regex": int(token_counts.sum()),
        "total_chapters": int(n_chap),
        # markers summary
        "markers_total": int(n_markers),
        "markers_appearing": int(n_appearing),
        "top_n": int(top_n_actual),
        "top_markers": top_markers,
        "top5_markers": top5_markers,
        # per-chapter info
        "chapter_titles": [c["title"] for c in chapters],
        "chapter_token_counts": token_counts.tolist(),
        "chapter_emotional_density_per_1k": density_per_chapter.tolist(),
        "dominant_marker_per_chapter": dominant_per_chapter,
        # full matrix (chapters × all markers)
        "matrix_markers": markers,
        "matrix_norm_per_1k": norm_matrix.tolist(),
        "matrix_raw_counts": raw_counts.tolist(),
        # top-N matrix (subset, for the 2 charts)
        "top_matrix_markers": top_markers,
        "top_matrix_norm_per_1k": top_norm.tolist(),
        # variance stats
        "marker_variance": marker_variance.tolist(),
        "marker_chapters_appearing": chapters_appearing.tolist(),
        "stable_min_chapters_threshold": int(stable_min_chapters),
        "most_stable_marker": stable,
        "most_volatile_marker": volatile,
        "highest_density_chapter": hi,
        "lowest_density_chapter": lo,
    }

    # ----- 11. Markdown verdict -----------------------------------------
    verdict_text = (
        f"Эволюция маркеров: «{stable['marker']}» — самый стабильный "
        f"(var={stable['variance']:.4f}, mean={stable['mean_density_per_1k']:.2f}/1000, "
        f"в {stable['chapters_appearing']}/{n_chap} главах); "
        f"«{volatile['marker']}» — самый волатильный "
        f"(var={volatile['variance']:.4f}, mean={volatile['mean_density_per_1k']:.2f}/1000, "
        f"в {volatile['chapters_appearing']}/{n_chap} главах). "
        f"Пик эмоциональной плотности — глава «{hi['title']}» "
        f"({hi['density_per_1k']:.2f}/1000); "
        f"минимум — глава «{lo['title']}» ({lo['density_per_1k']:.2f}/1000). "
        f"Активных маркеров: {n_appearing}/{n_markers}."
    )

    verdict_lines = report.verdict_block(
        f"Theme Evolution: {filepath.name}",
        report.metric_table(
            ["Метрика", "Значение"],
            [
                ["Файл", str(filepath)],
                ["Слов (whitespace)", str(metrics["total_words"])],
                ["Токенов (regex)", str(metrics["total_tokens_regex"])],
                ["Глав", str(metrics["total_chapters"])],
                ["Маркеров всего", str(metrics["markers_total"])],
                ["Активных маркеров", str(metrics["markers_appearing"])],
                ["Top-N визуализации", str(metrics["top_n"])],
                ["Порог 'stable' (глав)", str(stable_min_chapters)],
                ["Самый стабильный", stable["marker"]],
                ["  var (stable)", f"{stable['variance']:.6f}"],
                ["  mean / 1000", f"{stable['mean_density_per_1k']:.3f}"],
                ["  глав с маркером", f"{stable['chapters_appearing']}/{n_chap}"],
                ["Самый волатильный", volatile["marker"]],
                ["  var (volatile)", f"{volatile['variance']:.6f}"],
                ["  mean / 1000", f"{volatile['mean_density_per_1k']:.3f}"],
                ["  глав с маркером", f"{volatile['chapters_appearing']}/{n_chap}"],
                ["Глава пик плотности",
                 f"#{hi['chapter_index'] + 1} «{hi['title']}»"],
                ["  density", f"{hi['density_per_1k']:.2f}/1000"],
                ["  tokens", str(hi["tokens"])],
                ["Глава минимум плотности",
                 f"#{lo['chapter_index'] + 1} «{lo['title']}»"],
                ["  density", f"{lo['density_per_1k']:.2f}/1000"],
                ["  tokens", str(lo["tokens"])],
            ],
        ),
        ["## Вердикт", "", f"**{verdict_text}**", ""],
    )

    # ----- 12. wrap into standard report bundle -------------------------
    full_report = report.wrap_report(
        task_name="theme_evolution",
        engine="POLER v6 EMOTIONAL_MARKERS",
        input_files=[str(filepath)],
        metrics=metrics,
        output_files={k: str(v) for k, v in artifacts.items()},
        verdict=verdict_text,
    )

    return {
        "report": full_report,
        "verdict_lines": verdict_lines,
        "artifacts": artifacts,
    }


__all__ = ["recipe_theme_evolution"]


# ============================================================
# Standalone test entry-point
# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Run:  python3 poler_toolkit/recipes_ext/theme_evolution.py [FILE]
# If no FILE is given, defaults to env $POLER_TOOLKIT_TEST_FILE or
# the Cassiopeia canon sample (if available).
# Output: env $POLER_TOOLKIT_OUTPUT/test_theme_evolution_<ts>/
# ============================================================
if __name__ == "__main__":
    import sys
    import os
    import json
    from datetime import datetime

    logging.basicConfig(
        level=logging.INFO,
        format="%(asctime)s [%(name)s] %(levelname)s: %(message)s",
    )

    default_file = os.environ.get("POLER_TOOLKIT_TEST_FILE", "")
    if not default_file or not Path(default_file).exists():
        # Fallback: try common sample locations
        for cand in [
            "upload/Касіопея исп канон главы 1 -58 (1).txt",
            "upload/Касіопея исп канон главы 1 -58 (1).txt",
        ]:
            if Path(cand).exists():
                default_file = cand
                break
    test_file = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else default_file

    # Output dir via paths.py (env-aware)
    from .. import paths as _paths
    output_root = _paths.get_output_root()
    ts = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
    out_dir = output_root / f"test_theme_evolution_{ts}"
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    print(f"Running theme_evolution on: {test_file}")
    print(f"Output dir: {out_dir}")

    bundle = recipe_theme_evolution(
        [test_file],
        chapter_pattern=None,
        lang="auto",
        top_n=20,
        out_dir=out_dir,
    )

    # Write report.json + verdict.md (mirrors what runner.run() does)
    (out_dir / "report.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(
            bundle["report"], ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, default=str,
        ),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    (out_dir / "verdict.md").write_text(
        "\n".join(bundle["verdict_lines"]), encoding="utf-8",
    )

    print("\n=== DONE ===")
    print(f"Artifacts ({len(bundle['artifacts'])}):")
    for name, p in bundle["artifacts"].items():
        print(f"  {name:14s} -> {p}  (exists={p.exists()})")
    print(f"\nreport.json: {out_dir / 'report.json'}")
    print(f"verdict.md:  {out_dir / 'verdict.md'}")
    print(f"\nVerdict: {bundle['report']['verdict']}")

```
"""
poler_toolkit.recipes_ext.themes_alpha
======================================

Recipe: build an **alphabetical inverted index** of every word in a text,
with source tracking (chapter / line / char-offset) and aggressive size
optimization so a 100 K-word novel produces < 5 MB of output instead of
the 10+ MB a naive full index would consume.

What the recipe does
--------------------
1. Read the file via ``core.read_any`` (supports .txt / .md / .epub).
2. Split the text into chapters via ``core.split_chapters`` so we can map
   any character offset to a 1-indexed chapter number (0 = pre-chapter).
3. In a single regex pass over the full text, capture every word and its
   absolute character offset; for each occurrence compute
   ``(chapter_num, line_num, char_offset)`` via ``bisect`` on pre-computed
   line/chapter starts (O(log N) per token).
4. Group occurrences by the lower-cased word form. Apply three size-bounding
   strategies:

   * **Pagination** — split the index alphabetically into one JSON file per
     starting letter (``index/A.json`` … ``index/Z.json`` for Latin,
     ``index/А.json`` … ``index/Я.json`` for Cyrillic, etc.) plus
     ``index/_other.json`` for words that start with a digit / underscore /
     other non-letter. Each file is capped at ``MAX_FILE_BYTES``
     (500 KB) by further sharding if needed (``A.json``, ``A_2.json`` …).

   * **Top-N per word** — for ordinary words keep at most ``max_locations``
     (default 20) source locations.

   * **Sampling common words** — words with more than
     ``SAMPLING_THRESHOLD`` (100) occurrences are stored as a chapter-level
     summary + the first ``SAMPLE_FIRST_N`` (5) exact locations only.

5. Emit:
   * ``summary.json``       — totals, top-N words, alphabet distribution.
   * ``schema.json``        — describes the on-disk layout.
   * ``letter_index.json``  — per-letter totals + top-10 most frequent.
   * ``index/<LETTER>.json`` — the paginated inverted index.
   * 4 PNG artifacts (alphabet histogram, word-length histogram,
     top-100 words, Zipf curve).
   * Standard ``report.json`` + ``verdict.md`` via the report bundle.

The function signature matches the Stage 2 contract so the main agent can
register it in ``recipes.RECIPES`` directly:

    "themes_alpha": {
        "fn": recipe_themes_alpha,
        "n_files": 1,
        "description": "Alphabetical inverted word index with source tracking + size optimization",
        "options": ["chapter_pattern", "lang", "max_locations", "top_n"],
    }
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import bisect
import json
import logging
import re
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
from collections import Counter, defaultdict

import numpy as np
import matplotlib
matplotlib.use("Agg")
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

# Recipe lives in poler_toolkit.recipes_ext, so parent-of-parent is
# poler_toolkit itself. Primary path: relative imports (matches Stage 2
# integration contract). Fallback: absolute imports, so the file can also
# be invoked directly as `python3 .../themes_alpha.py FILE` for testing.
try:
    from .. import core
    from .. import report
    from .. import viz
    from ..errors import RecipeError, ConfigurationError
except ImportError:  # pragma: no cover - script-mode fallback
    import os as _os
    import sys as _sys
    _here = _os.path.dirname(_os.path.abspath(__file__))
    _scripts = _os.path.dirname(_os.path.dirname(_here))
    if _scripts not in _sys.path:
        _sys.path.insert(0, _scripts)
    from poler_toolkit import core  # type: ignore
    from poler_toolkit import report  # type: ignore
    from poler_toolkit import viz  # type: ignore
    from poler_toolkit.errors import RecipeError, ConfigurationError  # type: ignore

log = logging.getLogger("poler_toolkit.recipes_ext.themes_alpha")


# ============================================================
# Tunable size-budget constants
# ------------------------------------------------------------
# These are *module-level* constants (not function arguments) because they
# are structural limits of the format, not user-tunable knobs. The
# user-tunable knobs (max_locations, top_n) are function arguments.
# ============================================================
SAMPLING_THRESHOLD = 100
"""Words with > this many occurrences get the chapter-summary + first-5
exact-locations treatment instead of the full ``max_locations`` list."""

SAMPLE_FIRST_N = 5
"""For sampled (common) words, how many exact ``[chapter, line, offset]``
locations to keep verbatim."""

MAX_FILE_BYTES = 500 * 1024  # 500 KB
"""Hard cap on a single ``index/<LETTER>.json`` file. If a letter's bucket
would exceed this, it is sharded into ``<LETTER>.json``, ``<LETTER>_2.json``,
``<LETTER>_3.json`` … — each shard carries a contiguous slice of the
alphabetically-sorted word list."""

# Tokenizer — same family as core.auto_detect_keyword and
# recipes_ext.theme_evolution. ``\w`` is Unicode-aware in Python 3 so it
# matches Cyrillic letters natively. The apostrophe inclusion lets us keep
# Ukrainian words like ``ібн'я`` and English contractions like ``don't`` as
# single tokens.
_TOKEN_RE = re.compile(r"[\w’']+")


# ============================================================
# Local visualization helpers
# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Stage 2 constraint: do NOT modify viz.py. The existing viz.* functions
# cover heatmaps, stacked bars, scatter plots and dashboards, but not the
# four specific plots this recipe needs (alphabet histogram, word-length
# histogram, top-N horizontal bar, Zipf log-log curve). We implement them
# locally so viz.py stays untouched for the main agent.
# ============================================================
def _safe_savefig(fig, path: Path) -> None:
    """Save + close. Honours toolkit DPI."""
    try:
        fig.savefig(path, dpi=viz.DPI)
        log.info(f"Saved: {path}")
    except Exception as e:
        raise viz.VisualizationError(f"savefig failed: {e}") from e
    finally:
        plt.close(fig)


def _alphabet_histogram(
    letter_counts: List[Tuple[str, int]],
    out_path: Path,
    title: str = "Alphabet distribution",
) -> None:
    """Bar chart of token occurrences per starting letter."""
    if not letter_counts:
        letter_counts = [("(none)", 0)]
    letters = [lc[0] for lc in letter_counts]
    counts = [lc[1] for lc in letter_counts]
    n = len(letters)
    fig_w = max(8, min(28, n * 0.55 + 2))
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(fig_w, 6), constrained_layout=True)
    xs = np.arange(n)
    colors = plt.cm.viridis(np.linspace(0.1, 0.9, n)) if n > 1 else [viz.COLOR_PRIMARY]
    ax.bar(xs, counts, color=colors, alpha=0.9, edgecolor="black", linewidth=0.3)
    ax.set_xticks(xs)
    ax.set_xticklabels(letters, fontsize=max(6, 11 - n // 20))
    ax.set_xlabel("Первая буква")
    ax.set_ylabel("Количество слов (с повторами)")
    ax.set_title(title, fontsize=13, pad=10)
    ax.grid(alpha=0.25, axis="y")
    if counts:
        ymax = max(counts)
        ax.set_ylim(0, ymax * 1.12 + 1)
    _safe_savefig(fig, out_path)


def _wordlength_histogram(
    lengths: np.ndarray,
    out_path: Path,
    title: str = "Word-length histogram",
) -> None:
    """Histogram of word lengths (in characters)."""
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(11, 5), constrained_layout=True)
    if lengths.size == 0:
        ax.text(0.5, 0.5, "no tokens", ha="center", va="center")
    else:
        max_len = int(lengths.max())
        bins = np.arange(1, max_len + 2) - 0.5  # bin edges centered on integers
        ax.hist(lengths, bins=bins, color=viz.COLOR_SECONDARY, alpha=0.85,
                edgecolor="black", linewidth=0.4)
        mean_len = float(lengths.mean())
        ax.axvline(mean_len, color=viz.COLOR_ACCENT, linestyle="--",
                   linewidth=1.5, label=f"среднее={mean_len:.2f}")
        ax.legend(fontsize=9)
        ax.set_xlim(0.5, max_len + 0.5)
    ax.set_xlabel("Длина слова (символов)")
    ax.set_ylabel("Количество слов")
    ax.set_title(title, fontsize=13, pad=10)
    ax.grid(alpha=0.25, axis="y")
    _safe_savefig(fig, out_path)


def _top_words_bar(
    top_words: List[Tuple[str, int]],
    out_path: Path,
    title: str = "Top-N most frequent words",
    max_label_len: int = 22,
) -> None:
    """Horizontal bar chart of the top-N most frequent words."""
    n = len(top_words)
    if n == 0:
        top_words = [("(none)", 0)]
        n = 1
    # Reverse so the largest bar is at the top of the chart
    pairs = list(reversed(top_words))
    words = [w if len(w) <= max_label_len else w[: max_label_len - 1] + "…" for w, _ in pairs]
    counts = [c for _, c in pairs]
    fig_h = max(6, min(34, n * 0.30 + 2))
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(11, fig_h), constrained_layout=True)
    ys = np.arange(n)
    colors = plt.cm.plasma(np.linspace(0.05, 0.85, n)) if n > 1 else [viz.COLOR_PRIMARY]
    ax.barh(ys, counts, color=colors, alpha=0.9, edgecolor="black", linewidth=0.3)
    ax.set_yticks(ys)
    ax.set_yticklabels(words, fontsize=max(5, 9 - n // 25))
    ax.invert_yaxis()  # so the largest is at top after our manual reverse cancels out
    ax.set_xlabel("Количество вхождений")
    ax.set_title(title, fontsize=13, pad=10)
    ax.grid(alpha=0.25, axis="x")
    # Annotate counts on bars (only if not too crowded)
    if n <= 50:
        for y, c in zip(ys, counts):
            ax.text(c + max(counts) * 0.005, y, str(c), va="center", fontsize=7)
    _safe_savefig(fig, out_path)


def _zipf_curve(
    counts_sorted: np.ndarray,
    out_path: Path,
    title: str = "Zipf distribution (log-log)",
) -> None:
    """Log-log Zipf plot: rank vs frequency."""
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(10, 6), constrained_layout=True)
    if counts_sorted.size == 0:
        ax.text(0.5, 0.5, "no tokens", ha="center", va="center")
    else:
        ranks = np.arange(1, counts_sorted.size + 1)
        ax.loglog(ranks, counts_sorted, color=viz.COLOR_PRIMARY,
                  alpha=0.55, linewidth=0, marker="o", markersize=2.5)
        # Reference 1/r line (Zipf's law): intercept = count of #1 word
        c1 = float(counts_sorted[0])
        ref = c1 / ranks
        ax.loglog(ranks, ref, color=viz.COLOR_ACCENT, linestyle="--",
                  linewidth=1.5, alpha=0.8, label="идеал 1/r (Zipf)")
        ax.legend(fontsize=9)
    ax.set_xlabel("Ранг слова (log)")
    ax.set_ylabel("Частота (log)")
    ax.set_title(title, fontsize=13, pad=10)
    ax.grid(alpha=0.25, which="both", linestyle=":")
    _safe_savefig(fig, out_path)


# ============================================================
# Index construction helpers
# ============================================================
def _compute_line_starts(text: str) -> List[int]:
    """
    Build the array of absolute char-offsets where each line begins.

    ``line_starts[i]`` is the offset of line ``i+1``. The sentinel
    ``line_starts[-1]`` is one past the end of the text (so bisect_right
    always returns a valid 1-indexed line number for any offset inside the
    text).
    """
    starts = [0]
    # Use find for speed (str.split makes a copy, which is wasteful for
    # 600 KB of text). We walk the \n positions and add +1 to land on the
    # first char of the next line.
    pos = text.find("\n")
    while pos != -1:
        starts.append(pos + 1)
        pos = text.find("\n", pos + 1)
    starts.append(len(text) + 1)  # sentinel
    return starts


def _classify_letter(ch: str) -> str:
    """
    Return the bucket key for a word starting with character ``ch``.

    * Upper-cased first letter (e.g. ``"А"``, ``"B"``, ``"Z"``) for letter
      starts.
    * ``"_other"`` for any non-letter start (digits, underscores, etc.).
      This becomes the filename ``index/_other.json`` per the Stage 2 spec.
    """
    if not ch:
        return "_other"
    if ch.isalpha():
        return ch.upper()
    return "_other"


def _compact_locations(locs: List[Tuple[int, int, int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
    """Convert list of 3-tuples to list of 3-element lists for JSON."""
    return [[int(c), int(l), int(o)] for c, l, o in locs]


def _build_word_entry(
    word: str,
    locations: List[Tuple[int, int, int]],
    max_locations: int,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Build a single word entry, applying size-bounding rules.

    * count > SAMPLING_THRESHOLD  → sampled format (first SAMPLE_FIRST_N
      exact + per-chapter summary).
    * otherwise                   → up to ``max_locations`` exact locations.
    """
    count = len(locations)
    if count > SAMPLING_THRESHOLD:
        # Per-chapter summary
        per_chapter: Counter = Counter()
        for chap, _line, _off in locations:
            per_chapter[chap] += 1
        by_chapter = [
            {"c": int(c), "n": int(n)}
            for c, n in sorted(per_chapter.items())
        ]
        return {
            "w": word,
            "n": count,
            "sampled": True,
            "first_5": _compact_locations(locations[:SAMPLE_FIRST_N]),
            "by_chapter": by_chapter,
        }
    # Regular word — cap at max_locations
    capped = locations[:max_locations]
    return {
        "w": word,
        "n": count,
        "loc": _compact_locations(capped),
        "truncated": count > max_locations,
    }


def _write_sharded_json(
    bucket: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    base_path: Path,
    max_bytes: int = MAX_FILE_BYTES,
) -> List[Path]:
    """
    Write ``bucket`` (a list of word entries) to JSON, sharding if the
    serialized form exceeds ``max_bytes``.

    Returns the list of files actually written. Always writes at least one
    file (``base_path``); if sharding is needed, subsequent shards are
    named ``base_path_2``, ``base_path_3``, …
    """
    if not bucket:
        # Empty bucket — still emit an (almost) empty file for the schema
        # to be consistent.
        base_path.write_text(
            json.dumps({"words": []}, ensure_ascii=False),
            encoding="utf-8",
        )
        return [base_path]

    # First, sort the bucket alphabetically by word so shard boundaries are
    # stable and meaningful.
    bucket = sorted(bucket, key=lambda e: e["w"])

    # Try to write as one file
    payload = json.dumps({"words": bucket}, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":"))
    encoded = payload.encode("utf-8")
    if len(encoded) <= max_bytes:
        base_path.write_bytes(encoded)
        return [base_path]

    # Need to shard — binary-search for the largest prefix that fits.
    # Each shard needs ~30 bytes of JSON wrapper ({"words":[...]}).
    written: List[Path] = []
    remaining = bucket
    shard_idx = 1
    while remaining:
        # Binary search: find max k such that json of remaining[:k] fits
        lo, hi = 1, len(remaining)
        best_k = 1
        while lo <= hi:
            mid = (lo + hi) // 2
            payload = json.dumps(
                {"words": remaining[:mid]},
                ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":"),
            ).encode("utf-8")
            if len(payload) <= max_bytes:
                best_k = mid
                lo = mid + 1
            else:
                hi = mid - 1
        shard = remaining[:best_k]
        remaining = remaining[best_k:]
        if shard_idx == 1:
            shard_path = base_path
        else:
            shard_path = base_path.with_name(f"{base_path.stem}_{shard_idx}{base_path.suffix}")
        shard_path.write_bytes(
            json.dumps({"words": shard}, ensure_ascii=False,
                       separators=(",", ":")).encode("utf-8")
        )
        written.append(shard_path)
        shard_idx += 1
    return written


# ============================================================
# Recipe
# ============================================================
def recipe_themes_alpha(
    filepaths: list,
    *,
    chapter_pattern: Optional[str] = None,
    lang: str = "auto",
    max_locations: int = 20,
    top_n: int = 100,
    out_dir: Path = None,
) -> dict:
    """
    Build an alphabetical inverted index of every word in the input text,
    with source tracking (chapter / line / char-offset) and aggressive
    size optimization.

    Args:
        filepaths: length-1 list with the input file path
            (.txt / .md / .epub supported via ``core.read_any``).
        chapter_pattern: optional regex for chapter headings. If ``None``,
            ``core.split_chapters`` auto-detects.
        lang: chapter-pattern language hint (``auto`` / ``ru`` / ``ua`` /
            ``en`` / ``inline_ru`` / ``inline_en``).
        max_locations: max number of exact ``[chapter, line, offset]``
            locations stored per ordinary word (default 20). Words with
            more than 100 occurrences bypass this and use a sampled format
            (chapter summary + first 5 exact locations).
        top_n: how many top-frequency words to expose in
            ``summary.json`` and the top-words PNG (default 100).
        out_dir: directory for all artifacts. If ``None``, no artifacts
            are written (useful when stacking recipes).

    Returns:
        ``{"report": dict, "verdict_lines": list, "artifacts": dict}``.
        The ``artifacts`` mapping contains at most four entries:
        ``alphabet_histogram``, ``wordlength_histogram``,
        ``top_words``, ``zipf_curve``.
    """
    # ----- 0. validate inputs -------------------------------------------
    if len(filepaths) != 1:
        raise ConfigurationError(
            "themes_alpha expects exactly 1 file",
            hint="Pass a single .txt / .md / .epub path.",
        )
    if max_locations < 1:
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"max_locations must be >= 1, got {max_locations}",
        )
    if top_n < 1:
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"top_n must be >= 1, got {top_n}",
        )
    filepath = Path(filepaths[0])
    log.info(f"[themes_alpha] file={filepath}")

    # ----- 1. read + split chapters -------------------------------------
    text, fmt = core.read_any(filepath)
    log.info(f"  read: {len(text)} chars, format={fmt}")

    chapters = core.split_chapters(text, pattern=chapter_pattern, lang=lang)
    n_chap = len(chapters)
    # Defensive fallback: ``core.split_chapters`` returns [] when the pattern
    # matched but every chapter was shorter than ``min_chapter_chars`` (500).
    # For the inverted-index recipe we do NOT want to fail in that case —
    # the index is still useful with the whole text treated as one chapter.
    # Every token will then get chapter_num=1 (since chapter_starts=[0]).
    if n_chap < 1:
        log.warning(
            "no chapters passed min_chapter_chars filter; "
            "treating whole text as single chapter",
        )
        chapters = [{
            "title": "Whole text",
            "body": text,
            "start": 0,
            "end": len(text),
            "paragraphs": [text],
        }]
        n_chap = 1
    log.info(f"  chapters: {n_chap}")

    # ----- 2. precompute offset→chapter and offset→line lookup tables --
    # chapter_starts: sorted list of `ch["start"]` (= m.end() of the
    # heading) — i.e. the offset where chapter body starts.
    chapter_starts = [ch["start"] for ch in chapters]
    line_starts = _compute_line_starts(text)
    log.info(
        f"  lookup tables: chapter_starts={len(chapter_starts)}, "
        f"line_starts={len(line_starts)}"
    )

    # ----- 3. single-pass tokenization with location tracking ----------
    word_locations: Dict[str, List[Tuple[int, int, int]]] = defaultdict(list)
    total_tokens = 0
    word_lengths: List[int] = []
    for m in _TOKEN_RE.finditer(text):
        word = m.group(0)
        offset = m.start()
        # chapter number: 0 if before any chapter body, else 1-indexed
        if not chapter_starts:
            chap_num = 0
        else:
            idx = bisect.bisect_right(chapter_starts, offset) - 1
            chap_num = 0 if idx < 0 else idx + 1
        # line number: 1-indexed (bisect_right on line_starts)
        line_num = bisect.bisect_right(line_starts, offset)
        word_locations[word.lower()].append((chap_num, line_num, offset))
        word_lengths.append(len(word))
        total_tokens += 1
    log.info(
        f"  tokenized: {total_tokens} occurrences, "
        f"{len(word_locations)} unique words"
    )

    # ----- 4. frequency ranking + summary stats -------------------------
    word_counts = {w: len(locs) for w, locs in word_locations.items()}
    # sorted (word, count) pairs by count desc, then word asc for stability
    sorted_words = sorted(
        word_counts.items(), key=lambda kv: (-kv[1], kv[0]),
    )
    top_words = sorted_words[:top_n]
    if sorted_words:
        most_frequent_word = sorted_words[0][0]
        # Longest word — pick the longest by character count; ties broken
        # alphabetically for determinism.
        longest_word = max(word_counts.keys(), key=lambda w: (len(w), -ord(w[0]) if w else 0))
    else:
        most_frequent_word = None
        longest_word = ""

    # ----- 5. alphabet distribution + per-letter top-10 ----------------
    letter_total: Counter = Counter()
    letter_unique: Counter = Counter()
    letter_top10: Dict[str, List[Tuple[str, int]]] = defaultdict(list)
    for w, c in sorted_words:
        key = _classify_letter(w[0])
        letter_total[key] += c
        letter_unique[key] += 1
        if len(letter_top10[key]) < 10:
            letter_top10[key].append([w, c])

    # letter_index: every letter that actually appears (no empty entries)
    letter_index: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    for key in sorted(letter_total.keys()):
        letter_index[key] = {
            "total_words_starting": int(letter_total[key]),
            "unique_words": int(letter_unique[key]),
            "top10": letter_top10[key],
        }

    # alphabet distribution sorted by letter (for the histogram + summary)
    alphabet_distribution = [
        (k, letter_total[k]) for k in sorted(letter_total.keys())
    ]

    # ----- 6. build word entries + group by letter ----------------------
    # Each entry is the JSON-ready dict from _build_word_entry.
    entries_by_letter: Dict[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]] = defaultdict(list)
    sampled_count = 0
    truncated_count = 0
    for w, locs in word_locations.items():
        entry = _build_word_entry(w, locs, max_locations)
        if entry.get("sampled"):
            sampled_count += 1
        if entry.get("truncated"):
            truncated_count += 1
        key = _classify_letter(w[0])
        entries_by_letter[key].append(entry)

    log.info(
        f"  size-bounding: sampled={sampled_count} (>{SAMPLING_THRESHOLD} occ.), "
        f"truncated={truncated_count} (>{max_locations} loc.), "
        f"letters={len(entries_by_letter)}"
    )

    # ----- 7. write all artifacts (if out_dir given) --------------------
    artifacts: Dict[str, Path] = {}
    written_files: List[Path] = []
    total_output_bytes = 0
    largest_file: Tuple[str, int] = ("(none)", 0)

    if out_dir is not None:
        out_dir = Path(out_dir)
        out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        index_dir = out_dir / "index"
        index_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

        # --- 7a. paginated index files ---
        # Always write all letter keys we saw + _other.json (which may be empty)
        all_keys = sorted(entries_by_letter.keys())
        if "_other" not in all_keys:
            all_keys.append("_other")
        index_file_paths: Dict[str, List[Path]] = {}
        for key in all_keys:
            bucket = entries_by_letter.get(key, [])
            base_path = index_dir / f"{key}.json"
            paths = _write_sharded_json(bucket, base_path, MAX_FILE_BYTES)
            index_file_paths[key] = paths
            for p in paths:
                written_files.append(p)
                sz = p.stat().st_size
                total_output_bytes += sz
                if sz > largest_file[1]:
                    largest_file = (str(p.relative_to(out_dir)), sz)

        # --- 7b. letter_index.json ---
        letter_index_path = out_dir / "letter_index.json"
        letter_index_path.write_text(
            json.dumps(letter_index, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2),
            encoding="utf-8",
        )
        written_files.append(letter_index_path)
        sz = letter_index_path.stat().st_size
        total_output_bytes += sz
        if sz > largest_file[1]:
            largest_file = (str(letter_index_path.relative_to(out_dir)), sz)

        # --- 7c. summary.json ---
        summary = {
            "file": str(filepath),
            "format": fmt,
            "total_chars": int(len(text)),
            "total_token_occurrences": int(total_tokens),
            "unique_words": int(len(word_locations)),
            "total_chapters": int(n_chap),
            "alphabet_distribution": {k: v for k, v in alphabet_distribution},
            "top_words": [[w, c] for w, c in top_words],
            "most_frequent_word": most_frequent_word,
            "most_frequent_word_count": int(sorted_words[0][1]) if sorted_words else 0,
            "longest_word": longest_word,
            "longest_word_len": len(longest_word),
            "mean_word_length": float(np.mean(word_lengths)) if word_lengths else 0.0,
            "median_word_length": float(np.median(word_lengths)) if word_lengths else 0.0,
            "sampled_words_count": int(sampled_count),
            "truncated_words_count": int(truncated_count),
            "config": {
                "max_locations": int(max_locations),
                "top_n": int(top_n),
                "sampling_threshold": SAMPLING_THRESHOLD,
                "sample_first_n": SAMPLE_FIRST_N,
                "max_file_bytes": MAX_FILE_BYTES,
            },
            "pagination": {
                "letter_files": {
                    k: [str(p.name) for p in ps]
                    for k, ps in index_file_paths.items()
                },
                "total_index_files": sum(len(ps) for ps in index_file_paths.values()),
            },
        }
        summary_path = out_dir / "summary.json"
        summary_path.write_text(
            json.dumps(summary, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2),
            encoding="utf-8",
        )
        written_files.append(summary_path)
        sz = summary_path.stat().st_size
        total_output_bytes += sz
        if sz > largest_file[1]:
            largest_file = (str(summary_path.relative_to(out_dir)), sz)

        # --- 7d. schema.json ---
        schema = {
            "version": "1.0.0",
            "recipe": "themes_alpha",
            "description": (
                "Alphabetical inverted word index with source tracking "
                "(chapter, line, char-offset) and size optimization."
            ),
            "files": {
                "summary.json": "Top-level summary: totals, top-N words, alphabet distribution, config.",
                "schema.json": "This file — describes the on-disk layout.",
                "letter_index.json": "Per-letter summary: total words, unique words, top-10 most frequent.",
                "index/<LETTER>.json": "Inverted index for words starting with <LETTER> (upper-cased).",
                "index/_other.json": "Inverted index for words starting with non-letter characters (digits, underscores).",
                "index/<LETTER>_2.json": "Overflow shard if a letter's bucket exceeds MAX_FILE_BYTES (rare).",
                "01_alphabet_histogram.png": "Bar chart of token occurrences per starting letter.",
                "02_wordlength_histogram.png": "Histogram of word lengths (in characters).",
                "03_top100_words.png": f"Top-{top_n} most frequent words (horizontal bar chart).",
                "04_zipf_curve.png": "Log-log Zipf distribution curve (rank vs frequency).",
                "report.json": "Standard poler_toolkit report bundle (meta + metrics + verdict).",
                "verdict.md": "Markdown verdict summary (human-readable).",
            },
            "word_entry_formats": {
                "regular_word (count <= SAMPLING_THRESHOLD)": {
                    "w": "string — the lower-cased word",
                    "n": "int — total occurrence count",
                    "loc": "list of [chapter_num, line_num, char_offset] tuples (up to max_locations)",
                    "truncated": "bool — true if count > max_locations (some locations were dropped)",
                },
                "sampled_word (count > SAMPLING_THRESHOLD)": {
                    "w": "string — the lower-cased word",
                    "n": "int — total occurrence count",
                    "sampled": "true (marker)",
                    "first_5": "first 5 exact [chapter_num, line_num, char_offset] tuples",
                    "by_chapter": "list of {c: chapter_num, n: count_in_chapter} (sorted by chapter_num)",
                },
            },
            "source_format": (
                "[chapter_num, line_num, char_offset] where "
                "chapter_num is 1-indexed (0 = before any chapter body), "
                "line_num is 1-indexed, char_offset is the absolute byte-"
                "equivalent character position in the source file."
            ),
            "pagination": {
                "rule": "One JSON file per starting letter (upper-cased). Non-letter starts go to _other.json.",
                "max_file_bytes": MAX_FILE_BYTES,
                "shard_naming": "If a letter's bucket exceeds max_file_bytes, shards are named <LETTER>_2.json, <LETTER>_3.json, ...",
            },
            "size_bounding": {
                "max_locations_per_word": "configurable via max_locations (default 20)",
                "sampling_threshold": SAMPLING_THRESHOLD,
                "sample_first_n": SAMPLE_FIRST_N,
            },
            "tokenizer": r"re.compile(r\"[\w’']+\")  — Unicode-aware, includes apostrophes",
            "notes": [
                "Words are lower-cased before grouping (case-insensitive index).",
                "Apostrophes are preserved inside words (e.g. Ukrainian ібн'я, English don't).",
                "Words starting with a digit, underscore, or other non-letter go to _other.json.",
                "Each index file has the shape {\"words\": [entry, entry, ...]}.",
            ],
        }
        schema_path = out_dir / "schema.json"
        schema_path.write_text(
            json.dumps(schema, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2),
            encoding="utf-8",
        )
        written_files.append(schema_path)
        sz = schema_path.stat().st_size
        total_output_bytes += sz
        if sz > largest_file[1]:
            largest_file = (str(schema_path.relative_to(out_dir)), sz)

        # --- 7e. PNG artifacts ---
        chapter_titles = [c["title"] for c in chapters]

        artifacts["alphabet_histogram"] = out_dir / "01_alphabet_histogram.png"
        _alphabet_histogram(
            alphabet_distribution,
            artifacts["alphabet_histogram"],
            title=f"{filepath.stem} — распределение по первой букве",
        )
        written_files.append(artifacts["alphabet_histogram"])
        sz = artifacts["alphabet_histogram"].stat().st_size
        total_output_bytes += sz
        if sz > largest_file[1]:
            largest_file = (str(artifacts["alphabet_histogram"].relative_to(out_dir)), sz)

        artifacts["wordlength_histogram"] = out_dir / "02_wordlength_histogram.png"
        _wordlength_histogram(
            np.array(word_lengths, dtype=np.int32) if word_lengths else np.array([], dtype=np.int32),
            artifacts["wordlength_histogram"],
            title=f"{filepath.stem} — длины слов",
        )
        written_files.append(artifacts["wordlength_histogram"])
        sz = artifacts["wordlength_histogram"].stat().st_size
        total_output_bytes += sz
        if sz > largest_file[1]:
            largest_file = (str(artifacts["wordlength_histogram"].relative_to(out_dir)), sz)

        artifacts["top_words"] = out_dir / "03_top100_words.png"
        _top_words_bar(
            top_words,
            artifacts["top_words"],
            title=f"{filepath.stem} — топ-{top_n} слов",
        )
        written_files.append(artifacts["top_words"])
        sz = artifacts["top_words"].stat().st_size
        total_output_bytes += sz
        if sz > largest_file[1]:
            largest_file = (str(artifacts["top_words"].relative_to(out_dir)), sz)

        artifacts["zipf_curve"] = out_dir / "04_zipf_curve.png"
        counts_sorted = np.array(
            [c for _, c in sorted_words], dtype=np.int64,
        ) if sorted_words else np.array([], dtype=np.int64)
        _zipf_curve(
            counts_sorted,
            artifacts["zipf_curve"],
            title=f"{filepath.stem} — кривая Zipf",
        )
        written_files.append(artifacts["zipf_curve"])
        sz = artifacts["zipf_curve"].stat().st_size
        total_output_bytes += sz
        if sz > largest_file[1]:
            largest_file = (str(artifacts["zipf_curve"].relative_to(out_dir)), sz)

    # ----- 8. build metrics dict for JSON -------------------------------
    metrics: Dict[str, Any] = {
        # input summary
        "file": str(filepath),
        "format": fmt,
        "total_chars": int(len(text)),
        "total_token_occurrences": int(total_tokens),
        "unique_words": int(len(word_locations)),
        "total_chapters": int(n_chap),
        "chapter_titles": [c["title"] for c in chapters],
        # size-bounding config (echo for audit)
        "max_locations": int(max_locations),
        "top_n": int(top_n),
        "sampling_threshold": SAMPLING_THRESHOLD,
        "sample_first_n": SAMPLE_FIRST_N,
        "max_file_bytes": MAX_FILE_BYTES,
        # index shape
        "sampled_words_count": int(sampled_count),
        "truncated_words_count": int(truncated_count),
        "letters_seen": sorted(letter_total.keys()),
        "alphabet_distribution": {k: v for k, v in alphabet_distribution},
        # frequency stats
        "top_words": [[w, c] for w, c in top_words],
        "most_frequent_word": most_frequent_word,
        "most_frequent_word_count": int(sorted_words[0][1]) if sorted_words else 0,
        "longest_word": longest_word,
        "longest_word_len": len(longest_word),
        "mean_word_length": float(np.mean(word_lengths)) if word_lengths else 0.0,
        "median_word_length": float(np.median(word_lengths)) if word_lengths else 0.0,
        # size report (only meaningful if out_dir was given)
        "size_report": {
            "total_output_bytes": int(total_output_bytes),
            "total_output_mb": round(total_output_bytes / (1024 * 1024), 4),
            "largest_file": largest_file[0],
            "largest_file_bytes": int(largest_file[1]),
            "file_count": len(written_files),
        },
    }

    # ----- 9. Markdown verdict -----------------------------------------
    size_mb = total_output_bytes / (1024 * 1024)
    budget_ok = total_output_bytes < 5 * 1024 * 1024
    if most_frequent_word:
        verdict_text = (
            f"Индекс: {total_tokens:,} вхождений, {len(word_locations):,} уникальных слов, "
            f"{n_chap} глав. Топ-1: «{most_frequent_word}» ({sorted_words[0][1]:,}). "
            f"Самое длинное: «{longest_word}» ({len(longest_word)} симв.). "
            f"Size-bounding: {sampled_count} sampled, {truncated_count} truncated. "
            f"Объём вывода: {size_mb:.2f} MB "
            f"({'OK' if budget_ok else 'ВНИМАНИЕ: превышает 5 MB'})"
        )
    else:
        verdict_text = "Текст пуст — индекс не построен."

    verdict_lines = report.verdict_block(
        f"Themes Alpha: {filepath.name}",
        report.metric_table(
            ["Метрика", "Значение"],
            [
                ["Файл", str(filepath)],
                ["Формат", fmt],
                ["Символов", str(metrics["total_chars"])],
                ["Токенов (regex)", f"{metrics['total_token_occurrences']:,}"],
                ["Уникальных слов", f"{metrics['unique_words']:,}"],
                ["Глав", str(metrics["total_chapters"])],
                ["Букв в индексе", str(len(metrics["letters_seen"]))],
                ["Top-N", str(metrics["top_n"])],
                ["Max locations/word", str(metrics["max_locations"])],
                ["Sampling threshold", str(metrics["sampling_threshold"])],
                ["Sample first-N", str(metrics["sample_first_n"])],
                ["Max file bytes", f"{metrics['max_file_bytes']:,}"],
                ["Sampled words", str(metrics["sampled_words_count"])],
                ["Truncated words", str(metrics["truncated_words_count"])],
                ["Топ-1 слово", most_frequent_word or "(none)"],
                ["  count", str(metrics["most_frequent_word_count"])],
                ["Самое длинное", longest_word or "(none)"],
                ["  длина", str(metrics["longest_word_len"])],
                ["Средняя длина", f"{metrics['mean_word_length']:.3f}"],
                ["Медианная длина", f"{metrics['median_word_length']:.3f}"],
                ["Объём вывода (bytes)", f"{total_output_bytes:,}"],
                ["Объём вывода (MB)", f"{size_mb:.4f}"],
                ["Лимит (MB)", "5.0000"],
                ["В бюджет?", "ДА" if budget_ok else "НЕТ"],
                ["Файлов написано", str(len(written_files))],
                ["Крупнейший файл", largest_file[0]],
                ["  размер (bytes)", f"{largest_file[1]:,}"],
            ],
        ),
        [
            "## Распределение по буквам",
            "",
            *(
                "| Буква | Всего слов | Уникальных |" 
                for _ in [0]
            ),
            "|---|---|---|",
            *(
                f"| {k} | {letter_total[k]:,} | {letter_unique[k]:,} |"
                for k in sorted(letter_total.keys())
            ),
            "",
        ],
        ["## Вердикт", "", f"**{verdict_text}**", ""],
    )

    # ----- 10. wrap into standard report bundle -------------------------
    full_report = report.wrap_report(
        task_name="themes_alpha",
        engine="POLER toolkit inverted index + size optimization",
        input_files=[str(filepath)],
        metrics=metrics,
        output_files={k: str(v) for k, v in artifacts.items()},
        verdict=verdict_text,
        extra={
            "letters_seen": sorted(letter_total.keys()),
            "size_budget_mb": 5.0,
            "size_actual_mb": round(size_mb, 4),
            "size_budget_ok": bool(budget_ok),
            "index_files": [str(p) for p in written_files],
        },
    )

    return {
        "report": full_report,
        "verdict_lines": verdict_lines,
        "artifacts": artifacts,
    }


__all__ = ["recipe_themes_alpha"]


# ============================================================
# Standalone test entry-point
# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Run:  python3 poler_toolkit/recipes_ext/themes_alpha.py [FILE]
# If no FILE is given, defaults to $POLER_TOOLKIT_TEST_FILE or the
# Cassiopeia canon sample (if available).
# Output: $POLER_TOOLKIT_OUTPUT/test_themes_alpha_<ts>/ with summary.json,
# schema.json, letter_index.json, index/*.json, 4 PNG artifacts,
# report.json, verdict.md.
# ============================================================
if __name__ == "__main__":
    import sys
    from datetime import datetime

    logging.basicConfig(
        level=logging.INFO,
        format="%(asctime)s [%(name)s] %(levelname)s: %(message)s",
    )

    import os
    default_file = os.environ.get("POLER_TOOLKIT_TEST_FILE", "")
    if not default_file or not Path(default_file).exists():
        for cand in [
            "upload/Касіопея исп канон главы 1 -58 (1).txt",
            "upload/Касіопея исп канон главы 1 -58 (1).txt",
        ]:
            if Path(cand).exists():
                default_file = cand
                break
    test_file = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else default_file

    # Output dir via paths.py (env-aware)
    from .. import paths as _paths
    ts = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
    out_dir = _paths.get_output_root() / f"test_themes_alpha_{ts}"
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    print(f"Running themes_alpha on: {test_file}")
    print(f"Output dir: {out_dir}")

    bundle = recipe_themes_alpha(
        [test_file],
        chapter_pattern=None,
        lang="auto",
        max_locations=20,
        top_n=100,
        out_dir=out_dir,
    )

    # Write report.json + verdict.md (mirrors what runner.run() does)
    (out_dir / "report.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(
            bundle["report"], ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, default=str,
        ),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    (out_dir / "verdict.md").write_text(
        "\n".join(bundle["verdict_lines"]), encoding="utf-8",
    )

    print("\n=== DONE ===")
    print(f"Artifacts ({len(bundle['artifacts'])}):")
    for name, p in bundle["artifacts"].items():
        print(f"  {name:22s} -> {p}  (exists={p.exists()})")
    print(f"\nreport.json: {out_dir / 'report.json'}")
    print(f"verdict.md:  {out_dir / 'verdict.md'}")
    print(f"\nVerdict: {bundle['report']['verdict']}")

    # Print size summary
    sr = bundle["report"]["metrics"]["size_report"]
    print(f"\nSize report:")
    print(f"  total bytes: {sr['total_output_bytes']:,}")
    print(f"  total MB:    {sr['total_output_mb']:.4f}")
    print(f"  files:       {sr['file_count']}")
    print(f"  largest:     {sr['largest_file']} ({sr['largest_file_bytes']:,} bytes)")
```
