```
"""
poler_toolkit.serve — HTTP API wrapper for poler_toolkit.

Exposes every toolkit recipe (4 core + 5 ext = 9 total) as a REST
endpoint, plus a status endpoint and an OpenAPI 3.0 spec.

Files
-----
- ``__init__.py``       (this file) — recipe registry + package metadata.
- ``server.py``         — HTTP server (http.server + threading).
- ``openapi_spec.py``   — programmatic OpenAPI 3.0 spec generator.
- ``task_store.py``     — thread-safe task store with 1-hour expiry.

Why a serve-side recipe registry?
---------------------------------
The core ``poler_toolkit.recipes.RECIPES`` dict only contains the 4
Stage-1 recipes. Stage 2 added 5 extension recipes in
``recipes_ext/`` but the spec says "do NOT modify recipes.py". We
therefore build our own ``SERVE_RECIPES`` registry here that fuses
both lists. The runner is *not* used for ext recipes because
``runner.run()`` only forwards ``keyword / chapter_pattern / lang /
top_n / names``; the ext recipes have their own option sets
(``plugin_path``, ``window_size``, ``extensions``, ``max_locations``…)
that the runner can't forward. The serve dispatcher therefore calls
each recipe function directly and replicates the runner's
report.json / verdict.md saving step.

Usage
-----
    # Start the server (foreground)
    python -m poler_toolkit.serve.server --port 8765 --host 127.0.0.1

    # Or as a module entry point (also works)
    python -m poler_toolkit.serve --port 8765

    # From Python
    from poler_toolkit.serve.server import run_server
    run_server(port=8765)
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Callable, Dict, List, Optional

# Recipe functions ---------------------------------------------------------
from ..recipes import (
    recipe_analyze,
    recipe_compare,
    recipe_structure,
    recipe_characters,
)
from ..recipes_ext import (
    recipe_theme_evolution,
    recipe_themes_alpha,
    recipe_diff_versions,
    recipe_batch_directory,
    recipe_custom_plugin,
    recipe_smart_read,
    recipe_smart_search,
    recipe_smart_batch,
    recipe_smart_cache,
    recipe_smart_diff,
)

__version__ = "1.0.0"

# Language enum shared by every recipe that takes a ``lang`` option.
LANG_ENUM = ["auto", "ru", "ua", "en", "inline_ru", "inline_en"]


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Option schema helper
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Each option is described by a small dict so the OpenAPI generator can
# build a JSON Schema automatically. ``required`` here means "the user
# must explicitly pass this option" — most options are optional because
# the recipes have sensible defaults.
def _opt(
    type_: str,
    *,
    default: Any = None,
    required: bool = False,
    enum: Optional[List[str]] = None,
    description: str = "",
    items: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
    minimum: Optional[float] = None,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    d: Dict[str, Any] = {
        "type": type_,
        "required": required,
        "description": description,
    }
    if default is not None:
        d["default"] = default
    if enum is not None:
        d["enum"] = enum
    if items is not None:
        d["items"] = items
    if minimum is not None:
        d["minimum"] = minimum
    return d


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Recipe registry
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Each entry mirrors the structure of ``recipes.RECIPES`` but adds a
# typed ``options`` dict for OpenAPI generation. ``n_files`` is either
# an int (exact count) or None (variable — used by custom_plugin).
SERVE_RECIPES: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {
    "analyze": {
        "fn": recipe_analyze,
        "n_files": 1,
        "description": (
            "Deep analysis of one file: chapters, POLER analyze_large_file "
            "by keyword, fragment clustering, TF-IDF + KMeans + PCA, "
            "Shannon entropy, character map. 5-7 PNG + JSON + Markdown."
        ),
        "options": {
            "keyword": _opt("string", description="POLER keyword (default: auto-detect)."),
            "chapter_pattern": _opt("string", description="Custom regex for chapter headings (overrides --lang)."),
            "lang": _opt("string", default="auto", enum=LANG_ENUM, description="Chapter-pattern language hint."),
            "top_n": _opt("integer", default=15, minimum=1, description="top_n for POLER analyze."),
        },
    },
    "compare": {
        "fn": recipe_compare,
        "n_files": 2,
        "description": (
            "Run analyze on both files and compare: neighbour cosine, "
            "silhouette, PCA explained variance, chapter similarity, "
            "jump fractions, entropy. Dashboard + verdict."
        ),
        "options": {
            "keyword": _opt("string"),
            "chapter_pattern": _opt("string"),
            "lang": _opt("string", default="auto", enum=LANG_ENUM),
        },
    },
    "structure": {
        "fn": recipe_structure,
        "n_files": 1,
        "description": (
            "Structural analysis only — no POLER engine. Chapters + "
            "TF-IDF + KMeans + PCA + entropy + chapter matrix. Faster "
            "than analyze. 4 PNG + JSON + Markdown."
        ),
        "options": {
            "chapter_pattern": _opt("string"),
            "lang": _opt("string", default="auto", enum=LANG_ENUM),
        },
    },
    "characters": {
        "fn": recipe_characters,
        "n_files": 1,
        "description": (
            "Character map: POLER grep_search_file per name + co-"
            "occurrence matrix. If --names not given, auto-detects "
            "top-10 capitalised words."
        ),
        "options": {
            "names": _opt(
                "array",
                items={"type": "string"},
                description="List of character names. Default: auto-detect.",
            ),
        },
    },
    "theme_evolution": {
        "fn": recipe_theme_evolution,
        "n_files": 1,
        "description": (
            "Track poler_v6.EMOTIONAL_MARKERS evolution across chapters. "
            "Stacked area + heatmap + line chart (top 5)."
        ),
        "options": {
            "chapter_pattern": _opt("string"),
            "lang": _opt("string", default="auto", enum=LANG_ENUM),
            "top_n": _opt("integer", default=20, minimum=1, description="Top markers to keep for heatmap / stacked area."),
        },
    },
    "themes_alpha": {
        "fn": recipe_themes_alpha,
        "n_files": 1,
        "description": (
            "Alphabetical inverted word index with source tracking "
            "(chapter / line / char-offset) and aggressive size "
            "optimization (sampled storage for very frequent words)."
        ),
        "options": {
            "chapter_pattern": _opt("string"),
            "lang": _opt("string", default="auto", enum=LANG_ENUM),
            "max_locations": _opt("integer", default=20, minimum=0, description="Max exact locations stored per ordinary word."),
            "top_n": _opt("integer", default=100, minimum=1, description="Top-frequency words to expose in summary.json + top-words PNG."),
        },
    },
    "diff_versions": {
        "fn": recipe_diff_versions,
        "n_files": 2,
        "description": (
            "Compare two versions of the same narrative text: POLER "
            "diff_files (semantic delta) + chapter-level TF-IDF cosine "
            "matrix + difflib token delta. 4 PNG visualisations."
        ),
        "options": {
            "keyword": _opt("string", description="Shared keyword for POLER diff_files. Default: auto-detect across both files."),
            "window_size": _opt("integer", default=3000, minimum=100, description="POLER window size in characters."),
            "chapter_pattern": _opt("string"),
            "lang": _opt("string", default="auto", enum=LANG_ENUM),
        },
    },
    "batch_directory": {
        "fn": recipe_batch_directory,
        "n_files": 1,
        "description": (
            "Analyse every file in a directory: per-file metrics + "
            "cross-file TF-IDF cosine + PCA + outlier detection + "
            "POLER analyze_directory. 5 PNG + 4 text artifacts."
        ),
        "options": {
            "keyword": _opt("string", description="Shared keyword for POLER analyze_directory."),
            "extensions": _opt(
                "array",
                items={"type": "string"},
                description="File extensions to include (e.g. ['.txt', '.md']). Default: text files only.",
            ),
            "recursive": _opt("boolean", default=True, description="Recurse into subdirectories."),
            "top_n": _opt("integer", default=10, minimum=1),
        },
    },
    "custom_plugin": {
        "fn": recipe_custom_plugin,
        "n_files": None,
        "description": (
            "Load and run an external Python plugin file. Sandbox-"
            "lite: timeout + error capture + os.environ / subprocess / "
            "socket warning hooks. Auto-generated plugin_info.md."
        ),
        "options": {
            "plugin_path": _opt(
                "string",
                description="Path to plugin .py file. If omitted, filepaths[0] must end in .py.",
            ),
            "timeout": _opt("integer", default=300, minimum=1, description="Plugin execution timeout in seconds."),
            "plugin_opts": _opt(
                "object",
                description="Free-form dict passed as plugin_opts to the plugin's run().",
            ),
        },
    },
    "smart_read": {
        "fn": recipe_smart_read,
        "n_files": 1,
        "description": "Token-efficient file read: summary / context / full / streaming modes.",
        "options": {
            "mode": _opt("string", default="summary", enum=["summary", "context", "full", "streaming"],
                         description="Read mode."),
            "query": _opt("string", description="Query for context mode."),
            "max_chars": _opt("integer", default=2000, minimum=100, description="Max chars for full mode."),
            "top_n": _opt("integer", default=20, minimum=1, description="Top-N words for summary."),
            "context_chars": _opt("integer", default=500, minimum=50, description="Context window size."),
        },
    },
    "smart_search": {
        "fn": recipe_smart_search,
        "n_files": None,
        "description": "Unified search: regex + full-text + file discovery. Ranked compact results.",
        "options": {
            "pattern": _opt("string", description="Regex pattern to search."),
            "keyword": _opt("string", description="Keyword for full-text search."),
            "include": _opt("string", description="File glob filter (e.g. '*.md')."),
            "exclude": _opt("string", description="Exclude glob pattern."),
            "max_results": _opt("integer", default=50, minimum=1, description="Max results to return."),
            "context_lines": _opt("integer", default=2, minimum=0, description="Context lines around matches."),
            "ignore_case": _opt("boolean", default=True, description="Case-insensitive search."),
            "recursive": _opt("boolean", default=True, description="Recurse into subdirectories."),
        },
    },
    "smart_batch": {
        "fn": recipe_smart_batch,
        "n_files": 1,
        "description": "Batch process files: compact per-file stats, no full content.",
        "options": {
            "keyword": _opt("string", description="Shared keyword."),
            "extensions": _opt("array", items={"type": "string"},
                               description="File extensions to include."),
            "recursive": _opt("boolean", default=True, description="Recurse into subdirectories."),
            "top_n": _opt("integer", default=10, minimum=1, description="Top-N files to show."),
        },
    },
    "smart_cache": {
        "fn": recipe_smart_cache,
        "n_files": None,
        "description": "Persistent analysis cache: store / get / list / clear.",
        "options": {
            "action": _opt("string", default="get", enum=["store", "get", "list", "clear"],
                           description="Cache action."),
            "cache_dir": _opt("string", description="Cache directory path."),
            "keyword": _opt("string", description="Keyword for analysis."),
        },
    },
    "smart_diff": {
        "fn": recipe_smart_diff,
        "n_files": None,
        "description": "Fast differential: compare two files or file vs cached version.",
        "options": {
            "keyword": _opt("string", description="Keyword for analysis."),
            "cache_dir": _opt("string", description="Cache directory path."),
        },
    },
}


def list_recipes() -> Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Return a JSON-serialisable copy of the recipe registry.

    Drops the non-serialisable ``fn`` reference and any ``items`` sub-
    dicts that the OpenAPI generator needs but a plain JSON dump might
    choke on if a Path sneaks in. Keeps everything else.
    """
    out: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for name, spec in SERVE_RECIPES.items():
        out[name] = {
            "n_files": spec["n_files"],
            "description": spec["description"],
            "options": spec["options"],
        }
    return out


__all__ = [
    "SERVE_RECIPES",
    "LANG_ENUM",
    "list_recipes",
    "__version__",
]

```
"""
poler_toolkit.serve.openapi_spec — programmatic OpenAPI 3.0 generator.

Builds an OpenAPI 3.0 spec from the ``SERVE_RECIPES`` registry in
``poler_toolkit.serve.__init__``. The spec covers:

* ``GET  /``             — service info.
* ``GET  /health``       — health check.
* ``GET  /openapi.json`` — this spec.
* ``GET  /recipes``      — recipe list.
* ``GET  /tasks/{id}``   — poll task status.
* ``POST /<recipe>``     — submit a recipe for async execution.

The request schema is the same for every POST endpoint (filepaths +
options); the response schema is the same async-ack envelope. Per-
recipe options are exposed as a discriminated ``options`` sub-schema
via ``oneOf`` so a client can introspect what each recipe accepts.

Stdlib only (``json``).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Dict

from . import SERVE_RECIPES, __version__ as PKG_VERSION


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Shared schema fragments
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _filepaths_schema() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "type": "array",
        "items": {"type": "string"},
        "description": (
            "List of input file paths. Most recipes take exactly one file; "
            "`compare` and `diff_versions` take two; `batch_directory` takes "
            "a single directory path; `custom_plugin` takes a variable "
            "number (plugin_path + target files, or just targets when "
            "plugin_path is given in options)."
        ),
    }


def _options_for_recipe(name: str, spec: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Build a JSON-Schema fragment for one recipe's options dict."""
    props: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    required: list = []
    for opt_name, opt_spec in spec.get("options", {}).items():
        prop: Dict[str, Any] = {"type": opt_spec["type"]}
        if "description" in opt_spec and opt_spec["description"]:
            prop["description"] = opt_spec["description"]
        if "enum" in opt_spec and opt_spec["enum"] is not None:
            prop["enum"] = list(opt_spec["enum"])
        if "default" in opt_spec and opt_spec["default"] is not None:
            prop["default"] = opt_spec["default"]
        if "minimum" in opt_spec and opt_spec["minimum"] is not None:
            prop["minimum"] = opt_spec["minimum"]
        if "items" in opt_spec and opt_spec["items"] is not None:
            prop["items"] = opt_spec["items"]
        props[opt_name] = prop
        if opt_spec.get("required"):
            required.append(opt_name)
    schema: Dict[str, Any] = {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": props,
        "additionalProperties": True,
        "description": f"Options for the `{name}` recipe.",
    }
    if required:
        schema["required"] = required
    return schema


def _request_body_for_recipe(name: str, spec: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Build the OpenAPI requestBody object for a POST /<recipe> endpoint."""
    options_schema = _options_for_recipe(name, spec)
    n_files = spec.get("n_files")
    if n_files == 1:
        filepaths_desc = "Exactly one file path."
    elif n_files == 2:
        filepaths_desc = "Exactly two file paths (order matters for compare / diff_versions)."
    elif n_files is None:
        filepaths_desc = (
            "Variable file count: when `options.plugin_path` is set, this is "
            "the list of target files; otherwise the first entry must be the "
            "plugin .py file."
        )
    else:
        filepaths_desc = f"Exactly {n_files} file paths."
    schema = {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
            "filepaths": {
                "type": "array",
                "items": {"type": "string"},
                "description": filepaths_desc,
            },
            "options": options_schema,
        },
        "required": ["filepaths"],
        "additionalProperties": False,
    }
    return {
        "required": True,
        "content": {
            "application/json": {"schema": schema},
        },
    }


def _async_ack_schema() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Schema for the immediate POST response (task accepted, not done)."""
    return {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
            "status": {"type": "string", "enum": ["pending"]},
            "task_id": {
                "type": "string",
                "description": "12-char hex ID. Poll GET /tasks/{task_id} for status.",
            },
            "recipe": {"type": "string"},
            "submitted_at": {"type": "string", "format": "date-time"},
            "poll_url": {
                "type": "string",
                "description": "Absolute or relative URL to poll for task status.",
            },
        },
        "required": ["status", "task_id", "recipe"],
    }


def _task_status_schema() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Schema for GET /tasks/{task_id} responses (all states)."""
    return {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
            "task_id": {"type": "string"},
            "recipe": {"type": "string"},
            "status": {
                "type": "string",
                "enum": ["pending", "running", "completed", "failed", "expired"],
            },
            "created_at": {"type": "number", "format": "float"},
            "updated_at": {"type": "number", "format": "float"},
            # present only when status == "completed":
            "output_dir": {"type": "string"},
            "report": {"type": "object"},
            "verdict": {"type": "string"},
            "artifacts": {
                "type": "object",
                "additionalProperties": {"type": "string"},
                "description": "Mapping artifact_name → absolute file path.",
            },
            # present only when status == "failed":
            "error": {"type": "string"},
            "error_type": {"type": "string"},
        },
        "required": ["task_id", "recipe", "status"],
    }


def _error_schema() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
            "status": {"type": "string", "enum": ["error"]},
            "error": {"type": "string"},
            "error_type": {"type": "string"},
        },
        "required": ["status", "error"],
    }


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Top-level spec builder
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
def build_spec(server_host: str = "127.0.0.1", server_port: int = 8000) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Build the full OpenAPI 3.0 spec as a JSON-serialisable dict.

    Args:
        server_host: hostname used in the ``servers`` list (informational).
        server_port: port used in the ``servers`` list (informational).
    """
    base_url = f"http://{server_host}:{server_port}"

    paths: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}

    # ---- GET / -------------------------------------------------------
    paths["/"] = {
        "get": {
            "summary": "Service info + list of recipes",
            "operationId": "getServiceInfo",
            "tags": ["meta"],
            "responses": {
                "200": {
                    "description": "Service metadata and recipe list.",
                    "content": {
                        "application/json": {
                            "schema": {
                                "type": "object",
                                "properties": {
                                    "name": {"type": "string"},
                                    "version": {"type": "string"},
                                    "endpoints": {"type": "object"},
                                    "recipes": {"type": "object"},
                                },
                            },
                        },
                    },
                },
            },
        }
    }

    # ---- GET /health -------------------------------------------------
    paths["/health"] = {
        "get": {
            "summary": "Health check",
            "operationId": "healthCheck",
            "tags": ["meta"],
            "responses": {
                "200": {
                    "description": "Server is healthy.",
                    "content": {
                        "application/json": {
                            "schema": {
                                "type": "object",
                                "properties": {
                                    "status": {"type": "string", "enum": ["ok"]},
                                    "version": {"type": "string"},
                                    "time": {"type": "string", "format": "date-time"},
                                    "tasks_active": {"type": "integer"},
                                },
                                "required": ["status"],
                            },
                        },
                    },
                },
            },
        }
    }

    # ---- GET /openapi.json -------------------------------------------
    paths["/openapi.json"] = {
        "get": {
            "summary": "This OpenAPI 3.0 spec",
            "operationId": "getOpenApiSpec",
            "tags": ["meta"],
            "responses": {
                "200": {
                    "description": "OpenAPI 3.0 JSON document.",
                    "content": {
                        "application/json": {
                            "schema": {"type": "object"},
                        },
                    },
                },
            },
        }
    }

    # ---- GET /recipes ------------------------------------------------
    paths["/recipes"] = {
        "get": {
            "summary": "List recipes with their options",
            "operationId": "listRecipes",
            "tags": ["meta"],
            "responses": {
                "200": {
                    "description": "Recipe registry: name → {description, n_files, options}.",
                    "content": {
                        "application/json": {
                            "schema": {
                                "type": "object",
                                "additionalProperties": {"type": "object"},
                            },
                        },
                    },
                },
            },
        }
    }

    # ---- GET /tasks/{task_id} ----------------------------------------
    paths["/tasks/{task_id}"] = {
        "get": {
            "summary": "Poll task status by ID",
            "operationId": "getTaskStatus",
            "tags": ["tasks"],
            "parameters": [
                {
                    "name": "task_id",
                    "in": "path",
                    "required": True,
                    "schema": {"type": "string"},
                    "description": "12-char hex task ID returned by any POST /<recipe> endpoint.",
                },
            ],
            "responses": {
                "200": {
                    "description": "Current task state. `result` is populated when `status == 'completed'`; `error`/`error_type` when `status == 'failed'`.",
                    "content": {
                        "application/json": {
                            "schema": _task_status_schema(),
                        },
                    },
                },
                "404": {
                    "description": "Task not found or expired (TTL is 1 hour).",
                    "content": {
                        "application/json": {"schema": _error_schema()},
                    },
                },
            },
        }
    }

    # ---- POST /<recipe> (one path per recipe) ------------------------
    for name, spec in SERVE_RECIPES.items():
        path = f"/{name}"
        paths[path] = {
            "post": {
                "summary": f"Run the `{name}` recipe (async)",
                "description": spec["description"],
                "operationId": f"runRecipe_{name}",
                "tags": ["recipes"],
                "requestBody": _request_body_for_recipe(name, spec),
                "responses": {
                    "202": {
                        "description": "Task accepted; client should poll GET /tasks/{task_id}.",
                        "content": {
                            "application/json": {"schema": _async_ack_schema()},
                        },
                    },
                    "400": {
                        "description": "Malformed request body or wrong file count.",
                        "content": {
                            "application/json": {"schema": _error_schema()},
                        },
                    },
                    "404": {
                        "description": "Unknown recipe.",
                        "content": {
                            "application/json": {"schema": _error_schema()},
                        },
                    },
                    "500": {
                        "description": "Internal error submitting the task.",
                        "content": {
                            "application/json": {"schema": _error_schema()},
                        },
                    },
                },
            }
        }

    spec: Dict[str, Any] = {
        "openapi": "3.0.3",
        "info": {
            "title": "POLER Toolkit HTTP API",
            "description": (
                "REST wrapper around the poler_toolkit recipe runner. "
                "Every POST endpoint accepts a list of filepaths + an "
                "options dict, returns a task_id immediately, and runs "
                "the recipe in a background thread. Poll "
                "GET /tasks/{task_id} for the result. Tasks auto-expire "
                "after 1 hour."
            ),
            "version": PKG_VERSION,
            "license": {"name": "MIT"},
        },
        "servers": [{"url": base_url}],
        "tags": [
            {"name": "meta", "description": "Service metadata endpoints."},
            {"name": "recipes", "description": "Recipe execution endpoints (async)."},
            {"name": "tasks", "description": "Task status polling."},
        ],
        "paths": paths,
    }
    return spec


__all__ = ["build_spec"]

```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
poler_toolkit.serve.server — HTTP API server.

Exposes every poler_toolkit recipe (4 core + 5 ext = 9 total) as an
async REST endpoint, plus status / health / OpenAPI endpoints. Uses
stdlib only (``http.server`` + ``threading`` + ``json``).

Endpoints
---------
    GET  /                       service info + recipe list
    GET  /health                 {"status": "ok", ...}
    GET  /openapi.json           OpenAPI 3.0 spec
    GET  /recipes                recipe registry
    GET  /tasks/<task_id>        poll task status
    POST /analyze                run analyze (1 file)
    POST /compare                run compare (2 files)
    POST /structure              run structure (1 file)
    POST /characters             run characters (1 file)
    POST /theme_evolution        run theme_evolution (1 file)
    POST /themes_alpha           run themes_alpha (1 file)
    POST /diff_versions          run diff_versions (2 files)
    POST /batch_directory        run batch_directory (1 dir path)
    POST /custom_plugin          run custom_plugin (variable)

Request body for POST endpoints
-------------------------------
    {
      "filepaths": ["/path/to/file.txt"],
      "options": {"keyword": "Лёша", "lang": "auto"}
    }

Response (POST, immediate)
--------------------------
    {
      "status": "pending",
      "task_id": "abc123def456",
      "recipe": "structure",
      "submitted_at": "2026-...",
      "poll_url": "/tasks/abc123def456"
    }

Response (GET /tasks/<task_id>, when complete)
----------------------------------------------
    {
      "task_id": "abc123def456",
      "recipe": "structure",
      "status": "completed",
      "output_dir": "<output_root>/structure_<timestamp>",
      "report": {...},
      "verdict": "...",
      "artifacts": {"01_...": "<output_root>/structure_<timestamp>/01_...png", ...}
    }

On error: status == "failed" with ``error`` + ``error_type`` fields.

CLI
---
    python -m poler_toolkit.serve.server [--port 8765] [--host 127.0.0.1]
    python -m poler_toolkit.serve          [--port 8765] [--host 127.0.0.1]
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import logging
import sys
import threading
import traceback
from datetime import datetime
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional
from urllib.parse import urlparse

from . import SERVE_RECIPES, __version__ as PKG_VERSION, list_recipes
from .openapi_spec import build_spec
from .task_store import TaskStore
from .. import core
from .. import report
from ..errors import PolerToolkitError

log = logging.getLogger("poler_toolkit.serve.server")

# Shared task store — one per process is fine; ThreadingHTTPServer handles
# concurrent requests, and TaskStore is internally thread-safe.
_STORE = TaskStore()

# Module-level server config (set by ``run_server``) so the handler can
# build correct absolute poll URLs and OpenAPI servers list.
_HOST = "127.0.0.1"
_PORT = 8000


# ============================================================
# Recipe dispatcher
# ============================================================
def _filter_options(recipe_name: str, options: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Keep only options that are declared in the recipe's spec.

    Drops unknown keys silently (they would be rejected by the recipe
    function anyway). Also coerces simple type mismatches where possible
    (e.g. ``top_n`` as string → int).
    """
    spec = SERVE_RECIPES.get(recipe_name)
    if spec is None:
        return {}
    allowed = spec["options"]
    out: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    for k, v in (options or {}).items():
        if k not in allowed:
            continue
        type_ = allowed[k]["type"]
        try:
            if v is None:
                out[k] = v
            elif type_ == "integer" and not isinstance(v, int):
                out[k] = int(v)
            elif type_ == "boolean" and not isinstance(v, bool):
                out[k] = str(v).lower() in ("1", "true", "yes", "on")
            elif type_ == "string" and not isinstance(v, str):
                out[k] = str(v)
            else:
                out[k] = v
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            out[k] = v  # let the recipe reject it with a clear error
    return out


def _validate_filepaths(recipe_name: str, filepaths: List[str]) -> Optional[str]:
    """Return an error message string if file count is wrong, else None."""
    spec = SERVE_RECIPES.get(recipe_name)
    if spec is None:
        return f"unknown recipe '{recipe_name}'"
    expected = spec["n_files"]
    if expected is None:
        # variable count (custom_plugin); require at least 1 entry
        if not filepaths:
            return "filepaths must contain at least one entry"
        return None
    if len(filepaths) != expected:
        return (
            f"recipe '{recipe_name}' expects {expected} file(s), "
            f"got {len(filepaths)}"
        )
    return None


def _dispatch_recipe(
    recipe_name: str,
    filepaths: List[str],
    options: Dict[str, Any],
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Run a recipe synchronously and return the result payload.

    Returns a dict with ``output_dir``, ``report``, ``verdict``,
    ``artifacts`` keys. Raises on any error.

    This is the function the background worker thread calls. It
    replicates the runner.run() flow but supports every recipe's
    option set (not just the 5 the runner forwards).
    """
    spec = SERVE_RECIPES[recipe_name]
    fn = spec["fn"]
    filtered = _filter_options(recipe_name, options)

    # Output dir for this run.
    out_dir = core.make_output_dir(recipe_name)

    # Every recipe takes (filepaths, *, <opts>, out_dir=None).
    kwargs: Dict[str, Any] = dict(filtered)
    kwargs["out_dir"] = out_dir

    log.info(f"[dispatch] recipe={recipe_name}  files={len(filepaths)}  "
             f"opts_keys={list(filtered.keys())}  out_dir={out_dir}")
    bundle = fn(filepaths, **kwargs)

    # Persist report.json + verdict.md (mirrors runner.run).
    report_path = report.save_json_report(
        bundle["report"], out_dir, filename="report.json",
    )
    md_path = report.save_markdown_verdict(
        bundle["verdict_lines"], out_dir, filename="verdict.md",
    )

    # Serialise artifacts: Path → str.
    artifacts_serial: Dict[str, str] = {}
    for name, p in (bundle.get("artifacts") or {}).items():
        try:
            artifacts_serial[name] = str(Path(p))
        except Exception:
            artifacts_serial[name] = str(p)

    verdict_text = ""
    try:
        verdict_text = Path(md_path).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    except Exception:
        verdict_text = bundle["report"].get("verdict", "")

    return {
        "output_dir": str(out_dir),
        "report": bundle["report"],
        "verdict": verdict_text,
        "artifacts": artifacts_serial,
        "report_path": str(report_path),
        "verdict_path": str(md_path),
    }


def _worker(task_id: str, recipe_name: str, filepaths: List[str],
            options: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    """Background worker: transition task through running → done/failed."""
    _STORE.mark_running(task_id)
    try:
        result = _dispatch_recipe(recipe_name, filepaths, options)
        _STORE.mark_completed(task_id, result)
        log.info(f"[worker] task {task_id} completed: {result['output_dir']}")
    except PolerToolkitError as e:
        msg = f"{e.message}" + (f"\nHint: {e.hint}" if e.hint else "")
        _STORE.mark_failed(task_id, error=msg, error_type=type(e).__name__)
        log.warning(f"[worker] task {task_id} failed ({type(e).__name__}): {e}")
    except Exception as e:
        tb = traceback.format_exc(limit=4)
        _STORE.mark_failed(
            task_id,
            error=f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}\n{tb}",
            error_type=type(e).__name__,
        )
        log.error(f"[worker] task {task_id} crashed: {e}", exc_info=True)


# ============================================================
# HTTP helpers
# ============================================================
def _send_json(handler: BaseHTTPRequestHandler, data: Any,
               status: int = 200) -> None:
    body = json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, default=str).encode("utf-8")
    handler.send_response(status)
    handler.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=utf-8")
    handler.send_header("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", "*")
    handler.send_header("Access-Control-Allow-Methods", "GET, POST, OPTIONS")
    handler.send_header("Access-Control-Allow-Headers", "Content-Type")
    handler.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
    handler.end_headers()
    handler.wfile.write(body)


def _read_body(handler: BaseHTTPRequestHandler) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    length = int(handler.headers.get("Content-Length", 0) or 0)
    if length == 0:
        return {}
    raw = handler.rfile.read(length)
    if not raw:
        return {}
    return json.loads(raw.decode("utf-8"))


def _ok_error(handler: BaseHTTPRequestHandler, error: str,
              error_type: str = "Error", status: int = 400) -> None:
    _send_json(handler, {
        "status": "error",
        "error": error,
        "error_type": error_type,
    }, status=status)


# ============================================================
# HTTP handler
# ============================================================
class ServeHandler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    """HTTP request handler for the poler_toolkit API."""

    # BaseHTTPRequestHandler sets protocol_version = "HTTP/1.0" by
    # default, which closes the connection after every response. We
    # want keep-alive so curl/python-requests can poll efficiently.
    protocol_version = "HTTP/1.1"

    @property
    def server_version(self) -> str:  # type: ignore[override]
        return f"poler-toolkit-serve/{PKG_VERSION}"

    # ---- CORS preflight ---------------------------------------------
    def do_OPTIONS(self) -> None:
        self.send_response(204)
        self.send_header("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", "*")
        self.send_header("Access-Control-Allow-Methods", "GET, POST, OPTIONS")
        self.send_header("Access-Control-Allow-Headers", "Content-Type")
        self.send_header("Content-Length", "0")
        self.end_headers()

    # ---- GET ---------------------------------------------------------
    def do_GET(self) -> None:
        path = urlparse(self.path).path.rstrip("/") or "/"
        try:
            if path == "/":
                self._handle_root()
            elif path == "/health":
                self._handle_health()
            elif path == "/openapi.json":
                self._handle_openapi()
            elif path == "/recipes":
                self._handle_recipes()
            elif path.startswith("/tasks/"):
                task_id = path[len("/tasks/"):]
                self._handle_task_status(task_id)
            else:
                _ok_error(self, f"Not found: {path}", "NotFound", 404)
        except Exception as e:
            log.error(f"GET {path} crashed: {e}", exc_info=True)
            _ok_error(self, f"Internal error: {e}", type(e).__name__, 500)

    # ---- POST --------------------------------------------------------
    def do_POST(self) -> None:
        path = urlparse(self.path).path.rstrip("/") or "/"
        recipe_name = path.lstrip("/")
        if recipe_name not in SERVE_RECIPES:
            _ok_error(self, f"Unknown recipe endpoint: {path}", "NotFound", 404)
            return
        try:
            body = _read_body(self)
        except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
            _ok_error(self, f"Invalid JSON body: {e}", "JSONDecodeError", 400)
            return
        except Exception as e:
            _ok_error(self, f"Could not read body: {e}", type(e).__name__, 400)
            return

        # Body must be a JSON object (dict). A bare string / number / array
        # is valid JSON but not a valid request envelope.
        if not isinstance(body, dict):
            _ok_error(
                self,
                f"Request body must be a JSON object, got {type(body).__name__}",
                "BadRequest", 400,
            )
            return

        filepaths = body.get("filepaths")
        if not isinstance(filepaths, list):
            _ok_error(self, "'filepaths' must be a list of strings",
                      "BadRequest", 400)
            return
        # normalise to list[str]
        try:
            filepaths = [str(p) for p in filepaths]
        except Exception as e:
            _ok_error(self, f"filepaths contains non-string entry: {e}",
                      "BadRequest", 400)
            return

        options = body.get("options") or {}
        if not isinstance(options, dict):
            _ok_error(self, "'options' must be an object", "BadRequest", 400)
            return

        err = _validate_filepaths(recipe_name, filepaths)
        if err:
            _ok_error(self, err, "BadRequest", 400)
            return

        # Submit.
        task_id = _STORE.create(
            recipe=recipe_name, filepaths=filepaths, options=options,
        )
        thread = threading.Thread(
            target=_worker,
            args=(task_id, recipe_name, filepaths, options),
            name=f"serve-{recipe_name}-{task_id}",
            daemon=True,
        )
        thread.start()

        _send_json(self, {
            "status": "pending",
            "task_id": task_id,
            "recipe": recipe_name,
            "submitted_at": datetime.now().isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
            "poll_url": f"/tasks/{task_id}",
        }, status=202)

    # ---- GET handlers ------------------------------------------------
    def _handle_root(self) -> None:
        endpoints: Dict[str, str] = {
            "GET /": "Service info + recipe list.",
            "GET /health": "Health check.",
            "GET /openapi.json": "OpenAPI 3.0 spec.",
            "GET /recipes": "Recipe registry (name → spec).",
            "GET /tasks/{task_id}": "Poll task status.",
        }
        for name in SERVE_RECIPES:
            endpoints[f"POST /{name}"] = f"Run the {name} recipe (async)."
        _send_json(self, {
            "name": "POLER Toolkit HTTP API",
            "version": PKG_VERSION,
            "description": (
                "Async REST wrapper around poler_toolkit recipes. "
                "POST any /<recipe> to get a task_id, then poll "
                "GET /tasks/{task_id} for the result."
            ),
            "endpoints": endpoints,
            "recipes": list(SERVE_RECIPES.keys()),
            "tasks_active": len(_STORE),
        })

    def _handle_health(self) -> None:
        _send_json(self, {
            "status": "ok",
            "version": PKG_VERSION,
            "time": datetime.now().isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
            "tasks_active": len(_STORE),
        })

    def _handle_openapi(self) -> None:
        spec = build_spec(server_host=_HOST, server_port=_PORT)
        _send_json(self, spec)

    def _handle_recipes(self) -> None:
        _send_json(self, list_recipes())

    def _handle_task_status(self, task_id: str) -> None:
        if not task_id:
            _ok_error(self, "Missing task_id in path", "BadRequest", 400)
            return
        t = _STORE.get(task_id)
        if t is None:
            _ok_error(self, f"Task not found or expired: {task_id}",
                      "NotFound", 404)
            return
        # Build the response payload, including the result fields inline
        # when the task is completed (per task spec response format).
        resp: Dict[str, Any] = {
            "task_id": t["task_id"],
            "recipe": t["recipe"],
            "status": t["status"],
            "created_at": t["created_at"],
            "updated_at": t["updated_at"],
        }
        if t["status"] == "completed" and t["result"]:
            r = t["result"]
            resp.update({
                "output_dir": r.get("output_dir"),
                "report": r.get("report"),
                "verdict": r.get("verdict"),
                "artifacts": r.get("artifacts"),
            })
        elif t["status"] == "failed":
            resp.update({
                "error": t.get("error"),
                "error_type": t.get("error_type"),
            })
        _send_json(self, resp)

    # ---- Quieter logging --------------------------------------------
    def log_message(self, fmt: str, *args: Any) -> None:
        # Route access logs through the logging framework so they
        # respect --verbose / --quiet.
        msg = fmt % args
        log.info(f"{self.address_string()} - {msg}")


# ============================================================
# Server runner + CLI
# ============================================================
def run_server(host: str = "127.0.0.1", port: int = 8000) -> None:
    """Start the HTTP server (blocking)."""
    global _HOST, _PORT
    _HOST = host
    _PORT = port

    server = ThreadingHTTPServer((host, port), ServeHandler)
    bar = "=" * 64
    print(bar)
    print(f"  POLER Toolkit HTTP API  v{PKG_VERSION}")
    print(bar)
    print(f"  Listening:  http://{host}:{port}")
    print(f"  Endpoints:")
    print(f"    GET  /                  service info + recipes")
    print(f"    GET  /health            health check")
    print(f"    GET  /openapi.json      OpenAPI 3.0 spec")
    print(f"    GET  /recipes           recipe list")
    print(f"    GET  /tasks/{{id}}        poll task status")
    print(f"    POST /<recipe>          async recipe submit")
    print(f"           recipes: {', '.join(SERVE_RECIPES.keys())}")
    print(bar)
    print(f"  Task TTL: 1 hour. Ctrl-C to stop.")
    print()
    try:
        server.serve_forever()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\nShutting down...")
        server.shutdown()
        server.server_close()


def _build_argparser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        prog="poler-toolkit-serve",
        description="HTTP API server for poler_toolkit (async REST wrapper).",
    )
    p.add_argument("--host", default="127.0.0.1",
                   help="Bind host (default: 127.0.0.1).")
    p.add_argument("--port", "-p", type=int, default=8000,
                   help="Bind port (default: 8000).")
    p.add_argument("--verbose", "-v", action="store_true",
                   help="DEBUG logging.")
    p.add_argument("--quiet", "-q", action="store_true",
                   help="WARN+ logging only.")
    return p


def main(argv: Optional[List[str]] = None) -> int:
    args = _build_argparser().parse_args(argv)
    level = logging.DEBUG if args.verbose else (
        logging.WARNING if args.quiet else logging.INFO
    )
    logging.basicConfig(
        level=level,
        format="%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(name)s: %(message)s",
        datefmt="%H:%M:%S",
    )
    try:
        run_server(host=args.host, port=args.port)
    except OSError as e:
        print(f"ERROR: cannot bind {args.host}:{args.port} — {e}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

"""
poler_toolkit.serve.task_store — thread-safe async task tracker.

A POST to /<recipe> returns immediately with a ``task_id``; the recipe
runs in a background thread and the client polls ``GET /tasks/<task_id>``
for status. This module provides the in-memory store that backs that
flow.

Design
------
* Thread-safe: a single ``threading.RLock`` guards every mutation.
* Auto-expiry: tasks older than ``TTL_SECONDS`` (default 3600 = 1 hour)
  are pruned on every read/write. Expired task IDs return 404.
* States: ``pending`` → ``running`` → ``completed`` | ``failed``.
* Result payload: on completion the store holds the full result dict
  (output_dir, report, verdict, artifacts). On failure it holds the
  error message + error_type.
* Cleanup is lazy — no background reaper thread, because (a) the dict
  is small (a few hundred tasks max in any realistic dev session) and
  (b) pruning on every read/write is O(n) but n is tiny.

This module uses stdlib only (``threading`` + ``time`` + ``uuid``).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import threading
import time
import uuid
from typing import Any, Dict, Optional

# Default task time-to-live, in seconds (1 hour per task spec).
TTL_SECONDS: int = 3600

# Valid task states.
STATES = ("pending", "running", "completed", "failed")


class TaskStore:
    """In-memory, thread-safe, auto-expiring task registry.

    Usage::

        store = TaskStore()
        task_id = store.create(recipe="structure", filepaths=["a.txt"])
        # ... background thread runs the recipe ...
        store.mark_running(task_id)
        try:
            result = run_recipe(...)
            store.mark_completed(task_id, result)
        except Exception as e:
            store.mark_failed(task_id, error=str(e), error_type=type(e).__name__)
        # ... later ...
        state = store.get(task_id)  # → dict with status, result, etc.
    """

    def __init__(self, ttl_seconds: int = TTL_SECONDS) -> None:
        self._ttl = int(ttl_seconds)
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._tasks: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Internal: expiry
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _now(self) -> float:
        return time.time()

    def _prune_locked(self) -> int:
        """Remove expired tasks. Caller must hold ``_lock``.

        Returns the number of tasks pruned. A task is expired if it has
        been in a terminal state (``completed`` / ``failed``) for longer
        than ``ttl_seconds``, OR if it has been pending/running for
        longer than ``ttl_seconds`` (the latter guards against stuck
        background threads).
        """
        now = self._now()
        expired_ids = []
        for tid, t in self._tasks.items():
            age = now - t["created_at"]
            if age > self._ttl:
                expired_ids.append(tid)
        for tid in expired_ids:
            del self._tasks[tid]
        return len(expired_ids)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Public API
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def create(
        self,
        *,
        recipe: str,
        filepaths: list,
        options: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
    ) -> str:
        """Create a new task in the ``pending`` state.

        Returns the generated task_id (a short hex string).
        """
        tid = uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]
        with self._lock:
            self._prune_locked()
            self._tasks[tid] = {
                "task_id": tid,
                "recipe": recipe,
                "filepaths": list(filepaths),
                "options": dict(options or {}),
                "status": "pending",
                "created_at": self._now(),
                "updated_at": self._now(),
                "result": None,
                "error": None,
                "error_type": None,
            }
        return tid

    def mark_running(self, task_id: str) -> bool:
        """Transition a task from ``pending`` → ``running``.

        Returns True if the transition happened, False if the task was
        not found or was not in the ``pending`` state.
        """
        with self._lock:
            self._prune_locked()
            t = self._tasks.get(task_id)
            if t is None or t["status"] != "pending":
                return False
            t["status"] = "running"
            t["updated_at"] = self._now()
            return True

    def mark_completed(self, task_id: str, result: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
        """Mark a task as ``completed`` with the given result payload.

        Returns True if the task was found, False otherwise.
        """
        with self._lock:
            self._prune_locked()
            t = self._tasks.get(task_id)
            if t is None:
                return False
            t["status"] = "completed"
            t["result"] = result
            t["updated_at"] = self._now()
            return True

    def mark_failed(
        self,
        task_id: str,
        *,
        error: str,
        error_type: str,
    ) -> bool:
        """Mark a task as ``failed`` with an error message + type name."""
        with self._lock:
            self._prune_locked()
            t = self._tasks.get(task_id)
            if t is None:
                return False
            t["status"] = "failed"
            t["error"] = error
            t["error_type"] = error_type
            t["updated_at"] = self._now()
            return True

    def get(self, task_id: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Return the full task record, or None if not found / expired."""
        with self._lock:
            self._prune_locked()
            t = self._tasks.get(task_id)
            if t is None:
                return None
            # Return a shallow copy so the caller can't mutate internal state.
            return dict(t)

    def list(self) -> list:
        """Return a list of all (non-expired) task records, newest first."""
        with self._lock:
            self._prune_locked()
            records = [dict(t) for t in self._tasks.values()]
        records.sort(key=lambda r: r["created_at"], reverse=True)
        return records

    def __len__(self) -> int:
        with self._lock:
            return len(self._tasks)


__all__ = ["TaskStore", "TTL_SECONDS", "STATES"]

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
poler_toolkit.tests.integration_test — end-to-end CLI smoke tests.

Runs every CLI subcommand against real fixtures and verifies it exits
cleanly (rc=0) and produces the expected output artifacts.  Designed to
be runnable from the extracted archive; test fixtures are resolved
via env vars with sensible fallbacks.

Usage
-----
    python3 -m poler_toolkit.tests.integration_test
    python3 poler_toolkit/tests/integration_test.py

Env vars
--------
    POLER_TOOLKIT_TEST_FILE  — main test corpus (default: tries known paths)
    POLER_TOOLKIT_TEST_PLUGIN — plugin file for custom_plugin test
    POLER_TOOLKIT_OUTPUT      — output root (default: ~/.local/share/poler_toolkit)

Returns 0 if all tests pass, 1 otherwise. Prints a per-test pass/fail
table to stdout and writes a machine-readable JSON report to
$POLER_TOOLKIT_OUTPUT/integration_results.json.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import time
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Fixtures (env-aware)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

# Resolve test fixtures via env vars or common known paths.
_candidates_cassiopeia = [
    os.environ.get("POLER_TOOLKIT_TEST_FILE", ""),
    "upload/Касіопея исп канон главы 1 -58 (1).txt",
    "upload/Касіопея исп канон главы 1 -58 (1).txt",
    "Касіопея исп канон главы 1 -58 (1).txt",
]
CASSIOPEIA: Optional[Path] = None
for _c in _candidates_cassiopeia:
    if _c and Path(_c).exists():
        CASSIOPEIA = Path(_c)
        break

_candidates_plugin = [
    os.environ.get("POLER_TOOLKIT_TEST_PLUGIN", ""),
    "tmp/test_plugin.py",
    "tmp/test_plugin.py",
    "test_plugin.py",
]
TEST_PLUGIN: Optional[Path] = None
for _c in _candidates_plugin:
    if _c and Path(_c).exists():
        TEST_PLUGIN = Path(_c)
        break

# A small directory to use for batch_directory smoke test — we make a
# temp dir with two tiny .txt files so the test is self-contained.
def _make_batch_dir() -> Path:
    d = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="poler_batch_test_"))
    (d / "alpha.txt").write_text(
        "Глава 1\n\nThe forest was quiet. Trees stood like sentinels.\n"
        "A bird called. The wind whispered through the leaves.\n" * 5,
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    (d / "beta.txt").write_text(
        "Глава 1\n\nThe city was loud. Cars honked. People shouted.\n"
        "Sirens wailed in the distance. Neon flickered.\n" * 5,
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    return d


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _run_cli(args: List[str], cwd: Optional[Path] = None,
             timeout: int = 600) -> Tuple[int, str, str]:
    """Run `python3 -m poler_toolkit ARGS...` and return (rc, stdout, stderr)."""
    cmd = [sys.executable, "-m", "poler_toolkit"] + args
    proc = subprocess.run(
        cmd,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        cwd=str(cwd) if cwd else None,
        timeout=timeout,
    )
    return proc.returncode, proc.stdout, proc.stderr


def _run_cli_with_cwd(args: List[str], cwd: Path,
                      timeout: int = 600) -> Tuple[int, str, str]:
    return _run_cli(args, cwd=cwd, timeout=timeout)


def _output_dir_from_stdout(stdout: str) -> Optional[Path]:
    """Parse 'Output: <path>' line from CLI success banner."""
    for line in stdout.splitlines():
        line = line.strip()
        if line.startswith("Output:"):
            return Path(line.split(":", 1)[1].strip())
    return None


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Individual tests
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def test_01_list_recipes() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """`list` shows all 9 recipes."""
    rc, out, err = _run_cli(["list"])
    expected = {
        "analyze", "compare", "structure", "characters",
        "theme_evolution", "themes_alpha", "diff_versions",
        "batch_directory", "custom_plugin",
    }
    found = set()
    for line in out.splitlines():
        line = line.strip()
        if line and line.split(" ")[0] in expected:
            found.add(line.split(" ")[0])
    missing = expected - found
    ok = rc == 0 and not missing
    return {
        "name": "test_01_list_recipes",
        "description": "`poler_toolkit list` shows all 9 recipes",
        "pass": ok,
        "rc": rc,
        "missing": sorted(missing),
        "stderr_excerpt": err[-300:] if err else "",
    }


def test_02_structure_smoke() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """`structure FILE` produces artifacts."""
    if CASSIOPEIA is None or not CASSIOPEIA.exists():
        return {"name": "test_02_structure_smoke", "pass": False,
                "error": f"fixture not found: {CASSIOPEIA}"}
    out_root = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="poler_int_")
    rc, out, err = _run_cli(
        ["--quiet", "--output", out_root,
         "structure", str(CASSIOPEIA), "--label", "int_test_structure"],
        timeout=600,
    )
    out_dir = _output_dir_from_stdout(out)
    has_report = False
    has_verdict = False
    if out_dir and out_dir.exists():
        has_report = (out_dir / "report.json").exists()
        has_verdict = (out_dir / "verdict.md").exists()
    return {
        "name": "test_02_structure_smoke",
        "description": "`structure FILE` runs and emits report.json + verdict.md",
        "pass": rc == 0 and has_report and has_verdict,
        "rc": rc,
        "output_dir": str(out_dir) if out_dir else None,
        "has_report_json": has_report,
        "has_verdict_md": has_verdict,
        "stderr_excerpt": err[-400:] if err else "",
    }


def test_03_theme_evolution_smoke() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """`theme_evolution FILE` produces artifacts."""
    if CASSIOPEIA is None or not CASSIOPEIA.exists():
        return {"name": "test_03_theme_evolution_smoke", "pass": False,
                "error": f"fixture not found: {CASSIOPEIA}"}
    out_root = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="poler_int_")
    rc, out, err = _run_cli(
        ["--quiet", "--output", out_root,
         "theme_evolution", str(CASSIOPEIA), "--label", "int_test_te"],
        timeout=600,
    )
    out_dir = _output_dir_from_stdout(out)
    has_report = False
    has_verdict = False
    if out_dir and out_dir.exists():
        has_report = (out_dir / "report.json").exists()
        has_verdict = (out_dir / "verdict.md").exists()
    return {
        "name": "test_03_theme_evolution_smoke",
        "description": "`theme_evolution FILE` runs and emits report.json + verdict.md",
        "pass": rc == 0 and has_report and has_verdict,
        "rc": rc,
        "output_dir": str(out_dir) if out_dir else None,
        "has_report_json": has_report,
        "has_verdict_md": has_verdict,
        "stderr_excerpt": err[-400:] if err else "",
    }


def test_04_themes_alpha_smoke() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """`themes_alpha FILE` produces artifacts."""
    if CASSIOPEIA is None or not CASSIOPEIA.exists():
        return {"name": "test_04_themes_alpha_smoke", "pass": False,
                "error": f"fixture not found: {CASSIOPEIA}"}
    out_root = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="poler_int_")
    # Use --max-locations 5 --top-n 10 to keep size/speed reasonable.
    rc, out, err = _run_cli(
        ["--quiet", "--output", out_root,
         "themes_alpha", str(CASSIOPEIA),
         "--max-locations", "5", "--top-n", "10",
         "--label", "int_test_ta"],
        timeout=600,
    )
    out_dir = _output_dir_from_stdout(out)
    has_report = False
    has_verdict = False
    if out_dir and out_dir.exists():
        has_report = (out_dir / "report.json").exists()
        has_verdict = (out_dir / "verdict.md").exists()
    return {
        "name": "test_04_themes_alpha_smoke",
        "description": "`themes_alpha FILE` runs and emits report.json + verdict.md",
        "pass": rc == 0 and has_report and has_verdict,
        "rc": rc,
        "output_dir": str(out_dir) if out_dir else None,
        "has_report_json": has_report,
        "has_verdict_md": has_verdict,
        "stderr_excerpt": err[-400:] if err else "",
    }


def test_05_diff_versions_smoke() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """`diff_versions FILE FILE` (same file twice → trivial diff) works."""
    if CASSIOPEIA is None or not CASSIOPEIA.exists():
        return {"name": "test_05_diff_versions_smoke", "pass": False,
                "error": f"fixture not found: {CASSIOPEIA}"}
    out_root = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="poler_int_")
    rc, out, err = _run_cli(
        ["--quiet", "--output", out_root,
         "diff_versions", str(CASSIOPEIA), str(CASSIOPEIA),
         "--label", "int_test_dv"],
        timeout=600,
    )
    out_dir = _output_dir_from_stdout(out)
    has_report = False
    has_verdict = False
    has_diff_md = False
    if out_dir and out_dir.exists():
        has_report = (out_dir / "report.json").exists()
        has_verdict = (out_dir / "verdict.md").exists()
        has_diff_md = (out_dir / "diff.md").exists()
    return {
        "name": "test_05_diff_versions_smoke",
        "description": "`diff_versions FILE1 FILE2` runs (trivial self-compare)",
        "pass": rc == 0 and has_report and has_verdict,
        "rc": rc,
        "output_dir": str(out_dir) if out_dir else None,
        "has_report_json": has_report,
        "has_verdict_md": has_verdict,
        "has_diff_md": has_diff_md,
        "stderr_excerpt": err[-400:] if err else "",
    }


def test_06_batch_directory_smoke() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """`batch_directory DIR` produces artifacts."""
    batch_dir = _make_batch_dir()
    try:
        out_root = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="poler_int_")
        rc, out, err = _run_cli(
            ["--quiet", "--output", out_root,
             "batch_directory", str(batch_dir),
             "--label", "int_test_bd"],
            timeout=600,
        )
        out_dir = _output_dir_from_stdout(out)
        has_report = False
        has_verdict = False
        if out_dir and out_dir.exists():
            has_report = (out_dir / "report.json").exists()
            has_verdict = (out_dir / "verdict.md").exists()
        return {
            "name": "test_06_batch_directory_smoke",
            "description": "`batch_directory DIR` runs on 2-file temp dir",
            "pass": rc == 0 and has_report and has_verdict,
            "rc": rc,
            "output_dir": str(out_dir) if out_dir else None,
            "has_report_json": has_report,
            "has_verdict_md": has_verdict,
            "stderr_excerpt": err[-400:] if err else "",
        }
    finally:
        shutil.rmtree(batch_dir, ignore_errors=True)


def test_07_custom_plugin_smoke() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """`custom_plugin --plugin-path PLUGIN FILE` works."""
    if TEST_PLUGIN is None or not TEST_PLUGIN.exists():
        return {"name": "test_07_custom_plugin_smoke", "pass": False,
                "error": f"plugin fixture not found: {TEST_PLUGIN}"}
    if CASSIOPEIA is None or not CASSIOPEIA.exists():
        return {"name": "test_07_custom_plugin_smoke", "pass": False,
                "error": f"target fixture not found: {CASSIOPEIA}"}
    out_root = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="poler_int_")
    rc, out, err = _run_cli(
        ["--quiet", "--output", out_root,
         "custom_plugin",
         "--plugin-path", str(TEST_PLUGIN),
         "--timeout", "120",
         "--opt", "title=integration_test",
         "--opt", "dpi=100",
         str(CASSIOPEIA),
         "--label", "int_test_cp"],
        timeout=300,
    )
    out_dir = _output_dir_from_stdout(out)
    has_report = False
    has_verdict = False
    has_plugin_info = False
    if out_dir and out_dir.exists():
        has_report = (out_dir / "report.json").exists()
        has_verdict = (out_dir / "verdict.md").exists()
        has_plugin_info = (out_dir / "plugin_info.md").exists()
    return {
        "name": "test_07_custom_plugin_smoke",
        "description": "`custom_plugin --plugin-path PLUGIN FILE` runs",
        "pass": rc == 0 and has_report and has_verdict and has_plugin_info,
        "rc": rc,
        "output_dir": str(out_dir) if out_dir else None,
        "has_report_json": has_report,
        "has_verdict_md": has_verdict,
        "has_plugin_info_md": has_plugin_info,
        "stderr_excerpt": err[-400:] if err else "",
    }


def test_08_help() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """`--help` works."""
    rc, out, err = _run_cli(["--help"])
    # argparse prints help to stdout, exits with 0
    return {
        "name": "test_08_help",
        "description": "`poler_toolkit --help` prints help",
        "pass": rc == 0 and "RECIPE" in out and "serve" in out,
        "rc": rc,
        "stdout_has_serve": "serve" in out,
        "stdout_has_custom_plugin": "custom_plugin" in out,
    }


def test_09_analyze_help() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """`analyze --help` works (subcommand help)."""
    rc, out, err = _run_cli(["analyze", "--help"])
    return {
        "name": "test_09_analyze_help",
        "description": "`poler_toolkit analyze --help` prints subcommand help",
        "pass": rc == 0 and "--keyword" in out and "--top-n" in out,
        "rc": rc,
        "stdout_has_keyword": "--keyword" in out,
        "stdout_has_top_n": "--top-n" in out,
    }


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Runner
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

TESTS = [
    test_01_list_recipes,
    test_02_structure_smoke,
    test_03_theme_evolution_smoke,
    test_04_themes_alpha_smoke,
    test_05_diff_versions_smoke,
    test_06_batch_directory_smoke,
    test_07_custom_plugin_smoke,
    test_08_help,
    test_09_analyze_help,
]


def main() -> int:
    print("=" * 78)
    print("poler_toolkit integration tests — Stage 2")
    print("=" * 78)
    print()

    results: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    for i, test_fn in enumerate(TESTS, 1):
        name = test_fn.__name__
        print(f"[{i}/{len(TESTS)}] {name} ...", end=" ", flush=True)
        t0 = time.time()
        try:
            result = test_fn()
        except Exception as e:
            result = {
                "name": name,
                "pass": False,
                "error": f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}",
            }
        dt = time.time() - t0
        result["duration_s"] = round(dt, 2)
        results.append(result)
        status = "PASS" if result.get("pass") else "FAIL"
        print(f"{status}  ({dt:.2f}s)")
        if not result.get("pass"):
            err = result.get("error") or result.get("stderr_excerpt", "")
            if err:
                print(f"           → {err[:200]}")

    print()
    print("=" * 78)
    print("Summary")
    print("=" * 78)
    n_pass = sum(1 for r in results if r.get("pass"))
    n_fail = len(results) - n_pass
    print(f"  Total:  {len(results)}")
    print(f"  Passed: {n_pass}")
    print(f"  Failed: {n_fail}")
    print()
    print(f"{'#':<3} {'Test':<40} {'Status':<8} {'Time':>6}")
    print("-" * 60)
    for i, r in enumerate(results, 1):
        status = "PASS" if r.get("pass") else "FAIL"
        print(f"{i:<3} {r['name']:<40} {status:<8} {r.get('duration_s', '?'):>6}")
    print()

    # Machine-readable report (env-aware)
    from .. import paths as _paths
    report_path = _paths.get_output_root() / "integration_results.json"
    try:
        report_path.write_text(
            json.dumps(results, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False, default=str),
            encoding="utf-8",
        )
        print(f"Machine-readable report: {report_path}")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"WARNING: could not write JSON report: {e}")

    return 0 if n_fail == 0 else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
